local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
if env.__gsTriggerUnload then pcall(env.__gsTriggerUnload) end

local require = (function()
	local origRequire = require
	local function elevate()
		pcall(function() setthreadidentity(8) end)
		pcall(function() setidentity(8) end)
		pcall(function() setthreadcontext(8) end)
		pcall(function() syn.set_thread_identity(8) end)
	end
	env.__gsElevate = elevate
	return function(...)
		local res = table.pack(pcall(origRequire, ...))
		elevate()
		if not res[1] then error(res[2], 0) end
		return table.unpack(res, 2, res.n)
	end
end)()

local Library = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/malecoco6-bit/8-bit-/main/gamesense"))()

local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local Window = Library:CreateWindow({
	Size = Vector2.new(660, 560),
	Bind = Enum.KeyCode.World95,
	KeybindList = false,
	Watermark = false,
})
pcall(function() Window.Gui.DisplayOrder = 998 end)

-- Fix ImportConfig to wait for weapon/skin lists to populate before setting saved values
do
	local origImport = Window.ImportConfig
	-- remember the last imported config: the knife changer builds its dropdowns
	-- late (it waits for the game's runtime asset folders), so a config loaded
	-- before those elements exist never reaches them. the knife changer re-runs
	-- the import once its UI is built so its saved values are applied.
	env.__gsLastImportJson = nil
	Window.ImportConfig = function(self, json)
		local ok, err = origImport(self, json)
		pcall(function()
			if type(json) == "string" and #json > 0 then env.__gsLastImportJson = json end
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.2)
			pcall(function()
				if bumpEquip then bumpEquip() end
			end)
		end)
		return ok, err
	end
end

local RageTab   = Window:AddTab({ Name = "Rage",    Icon = "rage" })
local LegitTab  = Window:AddTab({ Name = "Legit",   Icon = "legit" })
local VisualTab = Window:AddTab({ Name = "Visuals", Icon = "visuals" })
local MiscTab   = Window:AddTab({ Name = "Misc",    Icon = "misc" })
local SkinsTab  = Window:AddTab({ Name = "Skins",   Icon = "skins" })

-- ───────────── SKIN CHANGER (Skins tab) ─────────────
-- runs on its own thread so waiting for the game's skin data never
-- delays the rest of the menus
do
	task.spawn(function()
		local function fixId()
			pcall(function() setthreadidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setthreadcontext(8) end)
			pcall(function() syn.set_thread_identity(8) end)
		end
		fixId()

		local okMod, Skins = pcall(function()
			return require(ReplicatedStorage.Database.Components.Libraries.Skins)
		end)
		fixId()
		local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
		local WeaponsFolder = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
		local SkinsFolder = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")

		if not okMod or type(Skins) ~= "table" or not WeaponsFolder or not SkinsFolder then
			Window:Notify("Skin changer: Skins library or Assets folders not found", 5)
		else
			local function dataLoaded()
				for _, f in ipairs(WeaponsFolder:GetChildren()) do
					if f:IsA("Folder") and Skins.GetAllSkinsForWeapon(f.Name) then return true end
				end
				return false
			end
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while not dataLoaded() and os.clock() - t0 < 5 do task.wait(0.1) end
			fixId()

			local groups = { Weapons = {} }

			for _, f in ipairs(WeaponsFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if f:IsA("Folder") then
					local name = f.Name
					local list = Skins.GetAllSkinsForWeapon(name)
					local names, seen, wtype = {}, {}, nil

					if list then
						for _, sch in ipairs(list) do
							wtype = wtype or sch.type
							if sch.skin and sch.skin ~= "Stock" and not seen[sch.skin]
								and Skins.IsSkinAvailable(name, sch.skin) then
								seen[sch.skin] = true
								names[#names + 1] = sch.skin
							end
						end
					end

					if not wtype then
						local ok, st = pcall(Skins.GetSkinInformation, name, "Stock")
						wtype = ok and st and st.type or nil
					end
					if not wtype and string.find(string.lower(name), "knife", 1, true) then
						wtype = "Melee"
					end

					table.sort(names)

					if wtype ~= "Melee" and wtype ~= "Glove" and #names > 0 then
						table.insert(groups.Weapons, { name = name, skins = names })
					end
				end
			end

			table.sort(groups.Weapons, function(a, b) return a.name < b.name end)

			fixId()
			local setSec = SkinsTab:AddSection("Skin changer", "Left")
			local skinOn = setSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
			local skinWear = setSec:AddDropdown("Wear", {
				"Default", "Factory New", "Minimal Wear", "Field-Tested", "Well-Worn", "Battle-Scarred",
			}, "Default")

			local WEAR_FLOAT = {
				["Factory New"] = 0.03,
				["Minimal Wear"] = 0.11,
				["Field-Tested"] = 0.26,
				["Well-Worn"] = 0.41,
				["Battle-Scarred"] = 0.70,
			}
			local WEAR_ORDER = { "Factory New", "Minimal Wear", "Field-Tested", "Well-Worn", "Battle-Scarred" }

			local picks = {}

			local weaponSec = SkinsTab:AddSection("Weapons", "Right")
			for _, e in ipairs(groups.Weapons) do
				local opts = { "Default" }
				for _, s in ipairs(e.skins) do opts[#opts + 1] = s end
				picks[e.name] = weaponSec:AddDropdown(e.name, opts, "Default")
			end

			local function spectatingOther()
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				local subj = cam and cam.CameraSubject
				if not subj then return false end
				local m = subj
				if subj:IsA("Humanoid") or subj:IsA("BasePart") then m = subj.Parent end
				if m and m:IsA("Model") and m.Name ~= lp.Name then
					local p = Players:FindFirstChild(m.Name)
					return p ~= nil and p ~= lp
				end
				return false
			end

			local function map(weapon, skin, float)
				if not skinOn:Get() or type(weapon) ~= "string" or spectatingOther() then
					return weapon, skin, float
				end

				local dd = picks[weapon]
				if not dd then return weapon, skin, float end

				local s = skin
				local pick = dd:Get()
				if pick and pick ~= "Default" then
					s = pick
				end

				local f = float
				local wear = skinWear:Get()
				if wear and wear ~= "Default" then f = WEAR_FLOAT[wear] end

				return weapon, s, f
			end

			local function forceReequip()
				local char = lp.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
				if not (hum and tool) then return end
				pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end)
				task.wait(0.05)
				pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
			end

			local lastRefresh = 0
			bumpEquip = function()
				if os.clock() - lastRefresh < 0.5 then return end
				lastRefresh = os.clock()
				task.spawn(forceReequip)
			end

			local function onPickChanged()
				bumpEquip()
			end
			skinOn:OnChanged(onPickChanged)
			skinWear:OnChanged(onPickChanged)
			for _, dd in pairs(picks) do dd:OnChanged(onPickChanged) end

			local originals, wrappers, mine, nameOf = {}, {}, {}, {}
			local skipTables = { [originals] = true, [wrappers] = true, [mine] = true, [nameOf] = true }

			local function makeWrapper(fname, orig)
				local w = function(weapon, skin, float, ...)
					local nw, ns, nf = map(weapon, skin, float)
					return orig(nw, ns, nf, ...)
				end
				mine[w] = true
				return w
			end

			local function replaceMany(swap)
				local counts = {}
				if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return counts, "getgc missing" end
				local okGc, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
				if not okGc or type(gc) ~= "table" then return counts, "getgc failed" end

				local getups = (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues
				local setup = (debug and debug.setupvalue) or setupvalue
				local canUp = type(getups) == "function" and type(setup) == "function"

				for _, v in ipairs(gc) do
					local t = type(v)
					if t == "function" then
						if canUp and not mine[v] then
							pcall(function()
								for i, up in pairs(getups(v)) do
									local new = swap[up]
									if new then
										setup(v, i, new)
										counts[up] = (counts[up] or 0) + 1
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					elseif t == "table" and not skipTables[v] then
						pcall(function()
							for k, val in pairs(v) do
								if type(val) == "function" then
									local new = swap[val]
									if new then
										rawset(v, k, new)
										counts[val] = (counts[val] or 0) + 1
									end
								end
							end
						end)
					end
				end
				return counts, nil
			end

			local hookList = { "GetCameraModel", "GetInspectModel", "GetMagazine" }

			pcall(function() if setreadonly then setreadonly(Skins, false) end end)
			local swap = {}
			for _, fname in ipairs(hookList) do
				local orig = Skins[fname]
				if type(orig) == "function" and not mine[orig] then
					local w = makeWrapper(fname, orig)
					originals[fname] = orig
					wrappers[fname] = w
					nameOf[orig] = fname
					swap[orig] = w
					Skins[fname] = w
				end
			end
			skipTables[swap] = true

			pcall(replaceMany, swap)

			local lastReapply = os.clock()
			local function reapplyHooks(silent)
				lastReapply = os.clock()
				local sw = {}
				for fname, orig in pairs(originals) do sw[orig] = wrappers[fname] end
				local counts, err = replaceMany(sw)
				local total = 0
				for _, n in pairs(counts) do total += n end
				if not silent then
					Window:Notify(err and ("Hooks: " .. err) or ("Re-applied hooks, " .. total .. " references swapped"))
				end
			end

			task.delay(3, function() pcall(reapplyHooks, true) end)
			-- after a death the game rebuilds the loadout and caches fresh references to the originals: re-apply
			-- on every respawn (no 20s gate) and a few times, since the new viewmodels appear over ~3 seconds
			local function afterRespawn()
				for _, d in ipairs({ 0.5, 1.5, 3 }) do
					task.delay(d, function() pcall(reapplyHooks, true) end)
				end
				pcall(bumpEquip)
			end
			local respawnConn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(afterRespawn)
			local deadConn = lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Dead"):Connect(function()
				if lp:GetAttribute("Dead") ~= true then afterRespawn() end
			end)

			-- only names that actually have a skin dropdown (groups.Weapons) go into the index,
			-- so a knife mesh can never "win" and get treated like a gun skin
			local skinnable = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(groups.Weapons) do skinnable[e.name] = true end

			local meshIndex = {}
			for _, f in ipairs(WeaponsFolder:GetChildren()) do
				if f:IsA("Folder") and skinnable[f.Name] then
					for _, sub in ipairs({ "Camera", "Character" }) do
						local m = f:FindFirstChild(sub)
						if m then
							for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
								if d:IsA("MeshPart") and d.MeshId ~= "" then
									local cur = meshIndex[d.MeshId]
									if cur == nil then
										meshIndex[d.MeshId] = f.Name
									elseif cur ~= f.Name then
										meshIndex[d.MeshId] = false
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function wearFolderFor(texFolder)
				local order = {}
				local want = skinWear:Get()
				if want and want ~= "Default" then order[1] = want end
				for _, n in ipairs(WEAR_ORDER) do order[#order + 1] = n end
				for _, n in ipairs(order) do
					local f = texFolder:FindFirstChild(n)
					if f and f:IsA("Folder") then return f end
				end
				return nil
			end

			local function processRoot(root, sub)
				local votes, parts = {}, {}
				local function add(d)
					if d:IsA("MeshPart") then
						parts[#parts + 1] = d
						local w = meshIndex[d.MeshId]
						if w then votes[w] = (votes[w] or 0) + 1 end
					end
				end
				add(root)
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do add(d) end

				local best, bestN = nil, 0
				for w, n in pairs(votes) do
					if n > bestN then best, bestN = w, n end
				end
				if not best or not skinnable[best] then return end

				local dd = picks[best]
				local pick = dd and dd:Get()
				if not pick or pick == "Default" then return end

				local key = best .. "|" .. pick .. "|" .. tostring(skinWear:Get())
				if root:GetAttribute("GSSkin") == key then return end

				local sw = SkinsFolder:FindFirstChild(best)
				local sf = sw and sw:FindFirstChild(pick)
				local tf = sf and sf:FindFirstChild(sub)
				local wf = tf and wearFolderFor(tf)
				if not wf then return end

				for _, sa in ipairs(wf:GetChildren()) do
					if sa:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
						for _, mp in ipairs(parts) do
							if mp.Name == sa.Name then
								local old = mp:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")
								if old then old:Destroy() end
								sa:Clone().Parent = mp
							end
						end
					end
				end
				root:SetAttribute("GSSkin", key)
			end

			local function liveUpdate()
				if spectatingOther() then return end

				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if cam then
					for _, c in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do processRoot(c, "Camera") end
				end
				for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
					local n = string.lower(c.Name)
					if string.find(n, "viewmodel", 1, true) or string.find(n, "view_model", 1, true) then
						processRoot(c, "Camera")
					end
				end

				local seenRoot = {}
				local function own(m)
					if m and not seenRoot[m] then
						seenRoot[m] = true
						processRoot(m, "Character")
					end
				end
				own(lp.Character)
				local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
				own(chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name))
			end

			-- ── inventory names (gun slots): show the equipped skin next to the weapon name,
			-- same style as the knife changer's melee slot ──
			local function updateGunNames()
				if not skinOn:Get() or spectatingOther() then return end
				local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
				local invGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
				if not invGui then return end
				local gameplay = invGui:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
				local bottom = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("Bottom")
				local inv = bottom and bottom:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
				if not inv then return end
				local star = utf8.char(9733)
				for _, slot in ipairs(inv:GetChildren()) do
					if slot.Name == "Melee" then continue end -- the knife changer owns that slot
					local weapon = slot:FindFirstChild("Weapon")
					local label = weapon and weapon:FindFirstChild("WeaponName")
					if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then
						-- strip our own decoration first so it never stacks on re-checks
						local base = string.gsub(label.Text, "^" .. star .. " ", "")
						base = string.gsub(base, " | .*$", "")
						local dd = skinnable[base] and picks[base]
						local pick = dd and dd:Get()
						if pick and pick ~= "Default" then
							local txt = base .. " | " .. pick
							if label.Text ~= txt then label.Text = txt end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local lastLive = 0
			local liveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not skinOn:Get() then return end
				if os.clock() - lastLive < 0.25 then return end
				lastLive = os.clock()
				pcall(liveUpdate)
				pcall(updateGunNames)
			end)

			setSec:AddButton("Re-apply hooks", function()
				reapplyHooks(false)
			end)

			fixId()

			env.__gsSkinCleanup = function()
				pcall(function() liveConn:Disconnect() end)
				pcall(function() respawnConn:Disconnect() end)
				pcall(function() deadConn:Disconnect() end)
				local back = {}
				for fname, w in pairs(wrappers) do back[w] = originals[fname] end
				pcall(replaceMany, back)
				pcall(function() if setreadonly then setreadonly(Skins, false) end end)
				for fname, orig in pairs(originals) do Skins[fname] = orig end
			end
		end
	end)
end
-- ───────────── KNIFE CHANGER (Skins tab) ─────────────
-- runs on its own thread: waiting for the game's runtime skins/weapons
-- folders used to block every menu built after this point
do
	task.spawn(function()
		local function fixId()
			pcall(function() setthreadidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setthreadcontext(8) end)
			pcall(function() syn.set_thread_identity(8) end)
		end

		-- some executors can't keep these hooks alive
		local executorName = (identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor()) or "Unknown"
		local hookSupported = not (string.find(executorName, "RonixExploit", 1, true)
			or string.find(executorName, "Xeno", 1, true)
			or string.find(executorName, "Solara", 1, true))

		-- the game builds Assets/Skins at runtime: it does not exist yet when an
		-- autoexec script first runs. Waiting for it is what makes knife-named skin
		-- folders like the LightSaber actually populate.
		local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
		local SkinsRoot = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
		if not SkinsRoot then
			local t0 = os.clock()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.2)
				assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
				SkinsRoot = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
			until SkinsRoot or os.clock() - t0 > 10
		end

		local Checkifbaseknife = { "CT Knife", "T Knife", "Knife" }
		local function Checkknife(w)
			if not w then return false end
			for _, k in ipairs(Checkifbaseknife) do if w == k then return true end end
			return false
		end

		-- ── discover every knife model and every glove model that has skins in the game's data ──
		local KNIFE_WORDS = { "knife", "karambit", "bayonet", "dagger", "kukri", "machete", "scimitar", "cleaver", "saber", "sabre", "sword", "blade" }
		local function looksLikeKnife(name)
			if Checkknife(name) then return false end -- the stock knives are what gets replaced
			local l = string.lower(name)
			if l == "t knife" or l == "ct knife" then return false end
			for _, w in ipairs(KNIFE_WORDS) do
				if string.find(l, w, 1, true) then return true end
			end
			return false
		end

		local SkinSelections = {}
		local discoveredKnives = {}
		if SkinsRoot then
			pcall(function()
				for _, wf in ipairs(SkinsRoot:GetChildren()) do
					local skins = {}
					for _, sf in ipairs(wf:GetChildren()) do
						if sf.Name ~= "Default" then skins[#skins + 1] = sf.Name end
					end
					table.sort(skins)
					SkinSelections[wf.Name] = skins
				end
			end)
		end

		-- knife models live in ReplicatedStorage.Weapons, which only appears once
		-- the game has finished loading: wait briefly for it before building the list
		local WeaponsRoot = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
		if not WeaponsRoot then
			local t0 = os.clock()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.2)
				WeaponsRoot = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Weapons")
			until WeaponsRoot or os.clock() - t0 > 5
		end
		local seenKnifeName = {}
		if WeaponsRoot then
			pcall(function()
				for _, w in ipairs(WeaponsRoot:GetChildren()) do
					if looksLikeKnife(w.Name) and not seenKnifeName[w.Name] then
						seenKnifeName[w.Name] = true
						discoveredKnives[#discoveredKnives + 1] = w.Name
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		-- knife-named skin folders are still valid models, so merge them in too
		if SkinsRoot then
			pcall(function()
				for _, wf in ipairs(SkinsRoot:GetChildren()) do
					if looksLikeKnife(wf.Name) and not seenKnifeName[wf.Name] then
						seenKnifeName[wf.Name] = true
						discoveredKnives[#discoveredKnives + 1] = wf.Name
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		table.sort(discoveredKnives)

		-- "Default" = the model's own plain look. The game wants a real skin name, so Default is translated:
		-- a skin folder literally called Default / Vanilla if there is one, otherwise the plain stock finish.
		local function knifeSkinArg(kn, sel)
			if sel and sel ~= "Default" then return sel end
			local kf = SkinsRoot and SkinsRoot:FindFirstChild(kn)
			if kf and kf:FindFirstChild("Vanilla") then return "Vanilla" end
			if kf and kf:FindFirstChild("Default") then return "Default" end
			return "Stock"
		end

		local function SafeRequire(module)
			if not module then return nil end
			local success, result = pcall(function() return require(module) end)
			fixId()
			if success and result and type(result) == "table" then return result end
			return nil
		end

		-- ── UI ──
		local knifeSec = SkinsTab:AddSection("Knife changer", "Left")
		local knifeOn = knifeSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)

		-- every knife found in the skin data, plus the usual names in case the data lists them differently
		local KM_EXTRA = {
			"Karambit", "Butterfly Knife", "Flip Knife", "Gut Knife", "M9 Bayonet", "Bayonet", "Skeleton Knife",
			"Stiletto Knife", "Huntsman Knife", "Falchion Knife", "Bowie Knife", "Navaja Knife", "Ursus Knife",
			"Talon Knife", "Classic Knife", "Paracord Knife", "Survival Knife", "Nomad Knife", "Kukri Knife",
			"Shadow Daggers",
		}
		local knifeModels, seenKnife = {}, {}
		for _, kn in ipairs(discoveredKnives) do
			knifeModels[#knifeModels + 1] = kn
			seenKnife[kn] = true
		end
		for _, kn in ipairs(KM_EXTRA) do
			if SkinSelections[kn] and not seenKnife[kn] then
				knifeModels[#knifeModels + 1] = kn
				seenKnife[kn] = true
			end
		end
		-- the LightSaber lives in Assets/Skins but is easy for discovery to miss
		-- (late replication, naming): make sure it is always offered
		if SkinsRoot and SkinsRoot:FindFirstChild("LightSaber") and not seenKnife["LightSaber"] then
			knifeModels[#knifeModels + 1] = "LightSaber"
			seenKnife["LightSaber"] = true
		end
		table.sort(knifeModels)
		if #knifeModels == 0 then knifeModels = { "Karambit", "Butterfly Knife", "Flip Knife", "Gut Knife", "M9 Bayonet", "Skeleton Knife", "Stiletto Knife" } end

		local knifeModel = knifeSec:AddDropdown("Knife model", knifeModels, knifeModels[1])
		local knifeSkin = {}
		for _, kn in ipairs(knifeModels) do
			local ks = SkinSelections[kn]
			if ks then
				-- every skin list starts at Default
				local list = { "Default" }
				for _, s in ipairs(ks) do list[#list + 1] = s end
				knifeSkin[kn] = knifeSec:AddDropdown(kn .. " skin", list, "Default")
			end
		end

		local function currentKnife()
			local m = knifeModel:Get()
			local pick = knifeSkin[m]
			return m, knifeSkinArg(m, pick and pick:Get())
		end

		-- lets the change-trigger below re-swap cached function references
		local kgReapply = function() end
		-- hook-install state: the runtime classes load late on real executors
		local kgInstalled = false
		local kgOrigGCM -- pristine GetCameraModel, used to build swap meshes

		-- ── hooks (viewmodel + gloves) ──
		local hooked = {}
		local function installKGHooks()
			if kgInstalled then return end
			pcall(function()
				local SM = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Database") and ReplicatedStorage.Database:FindFirstChild("Components")
					and ReplicatedStorage.Database.Components:FindFirstChild("Libraries")
					and ReplicatedStorage.Database.Components.Libraries:FindFirstChild("Skins")
				local VM = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Classes") and ReplicatedStorage.Classes:FindFirstChild("WeaponComponent")
					and ReplicatedStorage.Classes.WeaponComponent:FindFirstChild("Classes")
					and ReplicatedStorage.Classes.WeaponComponent.Classes:FindFirstChild("Viewmodel")
				if not SM or not VM then return end

				local Sk = SafeRequire(SM)
				local Vm = SafeRequire(VM)
				if not Sk or not Vm then return end
				kgInstalled = true
				print("[KG] hooks installed (Skins + Viewmodel found)")

				local function knifeTarget(w)
					if not (knifeOn:Get() and w and Checkknife(w)) then return nil end
					return currentKnife()
				end

				-- orig -> wrapper map; the game caches direct references to these
				-- functions, so swapping the table fields alone is not enough
				local swap = {}

				if type(Sk.GetCameraModel) == "function" then
					local o = Sk.GetCameraModel
					kgOrigGCM = o
					hooked[#hooked + 1] = function() Sk.GetCameraModel = o end
					Sk.GetCameraModel = function(w, sk, ...)
						local nk, ns = knifeTarget(w)
						if nk then
							local ok, r = pcall(o, nk, ns, ...)
							if ok and r then return r end
						end
						local ok, r = pcall(o, w, sk, ...)
						if ok then return r end
						return nil
					end
					swap[o] = Sk.GetCameraModel
				end

				-- NOTE: GetCharacterModel is deliberately NOT hooked — hooking it swaps
				-- the knife mesh on every character (including other players).

				if type(Vm.new) == "function" then
					local o = Vm.new
					hooked[#hooked + 1] = function() Vm.new = o end
					Vm.new = function(vc, w, sk, ...)
						local nk, ns = knifeTarget(w)
						if nk then
							local ok, r = pcall(o, vc, nk, ns, ...)
							if ok and r then return r end
						end
						local ok, r = pcall(o, vc, w, sk, ...)
						if ok then return r end
						return nil
					end
					swap[o] = Vm.new
				end

				-- walk the gc and replace every cached reference to the originals
				-- (the game holds direct references, so field swaps alone never
				-- change the mesh — same trick the gun skin changer uses)
				local wrappers = {}
				for _, w in pairs(swap) do wrappers[w] = true end

				local function replaceRefs(sw)
					if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return end
					local okGc, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
					if not okGc or type(gc) ~= "table" then return end
					local getups = (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues
					local setup = (debug and debug.setupvalue) or setupvalue
					local canUp = type(getups) == "function" and type(setup) == "function"
					for _, v in ipairs(gc) do
						local t = type(v)
						if t == "function" then
							if canUp and not wrappers[v] then
								pcall(function()
									for i, up in pairs(getups(v)) do
										local new = sw[up]
										if new then setup(v, i, new) end
									end
								end)
							end
						elseif t == "table" then
							pcall(function()
								for k, val in pairs(v) do
									if type(val) == "function" then
										local new = sw[val]
										if new then rawset(v, k, new) end
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					end
				end

				kgReapply = function() pcall(replaceRefs, swap) end
				replaceRefs(swap)
				task.delay(3, function() pcall(replaceRefs, swap) end)
				-- slightly after the gun skin changer's respawn re-apply so this wrapper (which chains into it)
				-- ends up in front; repeated because the loadout is rebuilt over a few seconds after a death
				local function knifeAfterRespawn()
					for _, d in ipairs({ 1, 2.5, 4 }) do
						task.delay(d, function() pcall(replaceRefs, swap) end)
					end
				end
				local respawnConn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(knifeAfterRespawn)
				local deadConn = lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Dead"):Connect(function()
					if lp:GetAttribute("Dead") ~= true then knifeAfterRespawn() end
				end)
				hooked[#hooked + 1] = function()
					if respawnConn then respawnConn:Disconnect() end
					if deadConn then deadConn:Disconnect() end
				end
			end)
		end

		if hookSupported then
			task.spawn(function()
				-- the game creates its runtime classes long after autoexec fires on a
				-- real executor — keep trying until they show up
				for _ = 1, 40 do
					if kgInstalled then break end
					installKGHooks()
					if kgInstalled then break end
					task.wait(3)
				end
			end)
		end

		-- ── apply-on-change: re-swap the cached function references; the 0.5s loop
		-- below swaps the live mesh so a new knife shows immediately ──
		local lastRefresh = 0
		local function onKGChanged()
			if os.clock() - lastRefresh < 0.5 then return end
			lastRefresh = os.clock()
			kgReapply()
		end

		knifeOn:OnChanged(onKGChanged)
		knifeModel:OnChanged(onKGChanged)
		for _, dd in pairs(knifeSkin) do dd:OnChanged(onKGChanged) end

		-- ── config save/load ──
		-- these dropdowns are created seconds after the script starts (they wait
		-- for the game's runtime folders), so a config imported before now was
		-- applied to elements that did not exist yet and the saved knife values
		-- were lost. re-run the last import so the saved knife model / skin /
		-- enabled state finally lands on these elements
		do
			local saved = env.__gsLastImportJson
			if type(saved) == "string" and #saved > 0 then
				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait(0.1)
					local okImp = pcall(function() Window:ImportConfig(saved) end)
					if okImp then
						task.delay(0.2, function()
							pcall(function() if bumpEquip then bumpEquip() end end)
							onKGChanged()
						end)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		-- ── inventory name (melee slot) ──
		local function UpdateInventoryNames()
			if not knifeOn:Get() then return end
			local invGui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and lp.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
			if not invGui then return end
			local meleeSlot = invGui:FindFirstChild("Gameplay") and invGui.Gameplay:FindFirstChild("Bottom")
				and invGui.Gameplay.Bottom:FindFirstChild("Inventory")
				and invGui.Gameplay.Bottom.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Melee")
			if not meleeSlot then return end
			local weapon = meleeSlot:FindFirstChild("Weapon")
			if not weapon then return end
			local weaponName = weapon:FindFirstChild("WeaponName")
			if weaponName and weaponName:IsA("TextLabel") then
				local knifeModelName = knifeModel:Get()
				local pick = knifeSkin[knifeModelName]
				local sel = pick and pick:Get()
				local star = utf8.char(9733)
				local txt
				if sel and sel ~= "Default" then
					txt = star .. " " .. knifeModelName .. " | " .. sel
				else
					txt = star .. " " .. knifeModelName
				end
				-- rewriting the same Text every 0.4s forces a gui re-layout each tick
				if weaponName.Text ~= txt then
					weaponName.Text = txt
				end
			end
		end

		-- viewmodel model inside the camera (arms / viewmodels excluded)
		local function GetWeaponModel()
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return nil end
			for _, ch in pairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
				if ch:IsA("Model") and ch.Name ~= "Arms" and ch.Name ~= "Arms1" and ch.Name ~= "Arms2" and ch.Name ~= "Viewmodel" then
					return ch
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		-- ── live rebuild ──
		-- the game only builds the viewmodel when a round or side starts. to change
		-- the knife mid-round we find the live Viewmodel objects through the gc,
		-- point them at the chosen knife, and rebuild them with their own
		-- construct() method — the same path the game uses — so scale, welds,
		-- muzzle parts, sleeves, gloves and the viewmodel mirror all stay correct.
		local kgSwapped = {}
		local knifeSet = {}
		for _, kn in ipairs(knifeModels) do knifeSet[kn] = true end
		local function isKnifeName(n)
			return type(n) == "string" and (Checkknife(n) or knifeSet[n] == true)
		end
		-- true for any live knife viewmodel: stock knife, one we swapped, or one the Vm.new hook already
		-- built as the chosen knife (those used to be skipped, so changing the dropdown never updated them)
		local function kgIsKnifeVM(vm)
			if kgSwapped[vm] then return true end
			local okk, r = pcall(function()
				return (vm.Model and isKnifeName(vm.Model.Name)) or isKnifeName(vm.Weapon) or isKnifeName(vm.CameraModelWeapon)
			end)
			return okk and r == true
		end

		-- Viewmodel objects whose model lives under the camera (equipped or stashed)
		local kgVmCache, kgVmAt = {}, -10
		local function kgFindViewmodels(force)
			-- getgc(true) walks the whole heap and caused lag spikes: never more than
			-- once every 5s unless forced (the Vm.new hook already covers new equips)
			if not force and os.clock() - kgVmAt < 5 then
				local alive = {}
				for _, vm in ipairs(kgVmCache) do
					local ok, good = pcall(function() return vm.IsDestroyed ~= true and vm.Model and vm.Model.Parent ~= nil end)
					if ok and good then alive[#alive + 1] = vm end
				end
				return alive
			end
			kgVmAt = os.clock()
			local found = {}
			kgVmCache = found
			if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return found end
			local okGc, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
			if not okGc or type(gc) ~= "table" then return found end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return found end
			for _, v in ipairs(gc) do
				if type(v) == "table" then
					local ok, match = pcall(function()
						return type(v.construct) == "function"
							and v.Player == lp
							and typeof(v.Model) == "Instance"
							and v.Model:IsDescendantOf(cam)
							and v.WeaponComponent ~= nil
							and v.IsDestroyed ~= true
					end)
					if ok and match then found[#found + 1] = v end
				end
			end
			return found
		end

		-- building a test clone just to check a knife exists is expensive, and a
		-- knife that cannot build gets re-tested every 0.4s while equipped: cache
		-- the result for a few seconds so a bad pick stops hammering the builder
		local kgBuildCheck = {}
		local function kgCanBuild(nk, ns)
			if not kgOrigGCM then return false end
			local ck = nk .. "|" .. tostring(ns)
			local c = kgBuildCheck[ck]
			if c and os.clock() - c.at < 5 then return c.ok end
			local ok, m = pcall(kgOrigGCM, nk, ns)
			local good = ok and typeof(m) == "Instance"
			if good then pcall(function() m:Destroy() end) end
			kgBuildCheck[ck] = { ok = good, at = os.clock() } -- the test clone was never destroyed before
			return good
		end

		local function kgApplyTo(vm, nk, ns)
			if not kgCanBuild(nk, ns) then
				print("[KG] cannot build " .. nk .. " | " .. tostring(ns) .. " — skipping rebuild")
				return false
			end
			local wc = vm.WeaponComponent
			local char = wc and wc.Character or lp.Character
			if not char then return false end
			if not kgSwapped[vm] then
				kgSwapped[vm] = {
					weapon = (type(vm.Weapon) == "string" and vm.Weapon) or vm.CameraModelWeapon or vm.Weapon,
					skin = vm.Skin,
					key = nil,
				}
			end
			-- the Vm.new hook already built this viewmodel as the chosen knife, so
			-- constructing again made the knife visibly refresh a second time on equip
			if vm.CameraModelWeapon == nk and vm.Skin == ns then
				return true
			end
			vm.CameraModelWeapon = nk
			vm.Skin = ns
			local ok, err = pcall(vm.construct, vm, char, wc)
			if not ok then
				print("[KG] construct failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
			return ok
		end

		local function kgRestoreVM(vm)
			local st = kgSwapped[vm]
			kgSwapped[vm] = nil
			if not st or vm.IsDestroyed then return end
			local wc = vm.WeaponComponent
			local char = wc and wc.Character or lp.Character
			if not char then return end
			vm.CameraModelWeapon = st.weapon
			vm.Skin = st.skin
			pcall(vm.construct, vm, char, wc)
		end

		-- applies the chosen knife skin's textures to the viewmodel
		local knifeSkinApplied = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local function ApplyKnifeSkin()
			if not SkinsRoot then return end
			local wm = GetWeaponModel()
			if not wm or not Checkknife(wm.Name) then return end
			local ewn, sel = currentKnife()
			if not sel or sel == "Default" then return end
			local akey = ewn .. "|" .. sel
			if knifeSkinApplied[wm] == akey then return end
			local wsf = SkinsRoot:FindFirstChild(ewn)
			if not wsf then return end
			local sf = wsf:FindFirstChild(sel)
			if not sf then return end
			local cf = sf:FindFirstChild("Camera")
			if not cf then return end
			local fn = cf:FindFirstChild("Factory New")
			if not fn then return end
			for _, sa in pairs(fn:GetChildren()) do
				if sa:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
					local pt = wm:FindFirstChild(sa.Name, true)
					if pt and (pt:IsA("BasePart") or pt:IsA("MeshPart")) then
						for _, old in pairs(pt:GetChildren()) do
							if old:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then old:Destroy() end
						end
						sa:Clone().Parent = pt
					end
				end
			end
			knifeSkinApplied[wm] = akey
			UpdateInventoryNames()
		end

		-- One pass of the knife logic. force=true ignores the cached key and rebuilds
		-- even if the current selection already matches what was last applied — used
		-- by the Refresh button. The inventory-name update used to live only inside
		-- ApplyKnifeSkin(), which bails out as soon as the model has actually been
		-- swapped (its name is no longer "CT Knife"/"T Knife"/"Knife"), so the melee
		-- slot label got stuck on the stock knife the moment the swap worked. It now
		-- always runs here instead.
		local function ApplyKnifeNow(force)
			if not knifeOn:Get() then
				if next(kgSwapped) then
					for vm in pairs(kgSwapped) do kgRestoreVM(vm) end
				end
				return
			end

			local nk, ns = currentKnife()
			local key = nk .. "|" .. tostring(ns)
			for _, vm in ipairs(kgFindViewmodels(force)) do
				local st = kgSwapped[vm]
				if kgIsKnifeVM(vm) then
					if force or not st or st.key ~= key then
						if kgApplyTo(vm, nk, ns) and kgSwapped[vm] then
							kgSwapped[vm].key = key
						end
					end
				end
			end

			UpdateInventoryNames()
			ApplyKnifeSkin()
		end

		knifeSec:AddButton("Refresh", function()
			if not kgInstalled then installKGHooks() end
			kgReapply()
			pcall(ApplyKnifeNow, true)
			-- button callbacks run on a thread that lost its elevated identity:
			-- re-elevate before Notify (which creates Instances) and never let a
			-- notification error break the button
			fixId()
			pcall(Window.Notify, Window, kgInstalled and "Knife refreshed" or "Knife refreshed (hooks not installed — texture-only)")
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while true do
				pcall(ApplyKnifeNow)
				task.wait(0.4)
			end
		end)

		env.__gsKnifeGloveCleanup = function()
			for vm in pairs(kgSwapped) do
				pcall(kgRestoreVM, vm)
			end
			for _, restore in ipairs(hooked) do pcall(restore) end
		end
	end)
end


-- ───────────── GLOVE CHANGER (Skins tab) ─────────────
-- The viewmodel builds its gloves from the character's "EquippedGloves" attribute (a glove name + skin)
-- every time it is constructed. So the changer simply writes the chosen glove into that attribute on
-- the local character and re-runs the viewmodels' own construct() -- the same path the game uses -- so
-- the new gloves show immediately. No hooks: the game's private glove cache is keyed by the attribute, so
-- every new selection is a cache miss and gets built fresh.
do
	task.spawn(function()
		local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local function fixId()
			pcall(function() setthreadidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setidentity(8) end)
			pcall(function() setthreadcontext(8) end)
			pcall(function() syn.set_thread_identity(8) end)
		end

		local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
		local SkinsRoot = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
		if not SkinsRoot then
			local t0 = os.clock()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.2)
				assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
				SkinsRoot = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Skins")
			until SkinsRoot or os.clock() - t0 > 10
		end
		if not SkinsRoot then return end

		-- every glove model with skins (the team-specific T / CT glove folders are skipped)
		local models, skinLists = {}, {}
		pcall(function()
			for _, wf in ipairs(SkinsRoot:GetChildren()) do
				local n = wf.Name
				local l = string.lower(n)
				local teamOnly = string.sub(n, 1, 2) == "T " or string.sub(n, 1, 3) == "CT "
				if not teamOnly and (string.find(l, "glove", 1, true) or string.find(l, "wrap", 1, true)) then
					local list = { "Default" }
					for _, sf in ipairs(wf:GetChildren()) do
						if sf.Name ~= "Default" and sf.Name ~= "Stock" then list[#list + 1] = sf.Name end
					end
					table.sort(list, function(a, b)
						if a == "Default" then return b ~= "Default" end
						if b == "Default" then return false end
						return a < b
					end)
					models[#models + 1] = n
					skinLists[n] = list
				end
			end
		end)
		table.sort(models)
		if #models == 0 then
			pcall(Window.Notify, Window, "Glove changer: no glove models found in the game's skin data", 5)
			return
		end

		-- ── UI ──
		local gloveSec = SkinsTab:AddSection("Glove changer", "Left")
		local gloveOn = gloveSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
		local gloveModel = gloveSec:AddDropdown("Glove model", models, models[1])
		local gloveSkin = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(models) do
			local dd = gloveSec:AddDropdown(m .. " skin", skinLists[m], "Default")
			gloveSkin[m] = dd
			pcall(function() dd:DependsOn(gloveModel, function(v) return v == m end) end)
		end

		-- the skin must exist in the game's schema or it builds no gloves at all: validate it, and fall
		-- back to the plain "Stock" finish ("Default" in the menu)
		local SkinsLib
		local function skinsLib()
			if SkinsLib ~= nil then return SkinsLib or nil end
			local ok, lib = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Database.Components.Libraries.Skins)
			end)
			fixId()
			SkinsLib = (ok and type(lib) == "table") and lib or false
			return SkinsLib or nil
		end

		local function chosen()
			local m = gloveModel:Get()
			local pick = gloveSkin[m]
			local s = pick and pick:Get() or "Default"
			if s == "Default" then s = "Stock" end
			local lib = skinsLib()
			if lib then
				local function valid(sk)
					local okI, info = pcall(lib.GetSkinInformation, m, sk)
					return okI and info ~= nil
				end
				if not valid(s) then
					-- "Stock" only exists for models the game has weapon properties for; a glove model without
					-- it builds NO gloves (so a new model looked like it never refreshed). Use the model's
					-- first real skin instead.
					local fallback
					for _, sk in ipairs(skinLists[m] or {}) do
						if sk ~= "Default" and valid(sk) then fallback = sk break end
					end
					if fallback then s = fallback end
				end
			end
			return m, s
		end

		-- ── viewmodels ──
		local function findViewmodels()
			local out = {}
			if type(getgc) ~= "function" then return out end
			local okGc, gc = pcall(getgc, true)
			if not okGc or type(gc) ~= "table" then return out end
			-- every viewmodel of mine, equipped or not: a weapon that has never been drawn has its model
			-- unparented (it is only parented on equip), which the old camera-descendant filter skipped --
			-- that is why slots 2 and 3 kept the old gloves
			for _, v in ipairs(gc) do
				if type(v) == "table" then
					local ok, match = pcall(function()
						return type(v.construct) == "function"
							and v.Player == lp
							and typeof(v.Model) == "Instance"
							and v.WeaponComponent ~= nil
							and v.IsDestroyed ~= true
					end)
					if ok and match then out[#out + 1] = v end
				end
			end
			return out
		end

		local lastRebuild = -10
		local function rebuild()
			lastRebuild = os.clock()
			for _, vm in ipairs(findViewmodels()) do
				pcall(function()
					local wc = vm.WeaponComponent
					local char = (wc and wc.Character) or lp.Character
					if char then vm.construct(vm, char, wc) end
				end)
			end
		end

		-- ── apply ──
		local saved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- [character] = { v = original attribute }
		local function wantJson()
			local m, s = chosen()
			return HttpService:JSONEncode({ Name = m, Skin = s })
		end

		-- force = the user changed something: rebuild now. Otherwise (the watcher) only rebuild when the
		-- game reset the attribute, and not more than once every 1.5s so a server that keeps rewriting it
		-- can't make this scan the heap over and over.
		local function apply(force)
			local ch = lp.Character
			if not ch then return end
			if gloveOn:Get() then
				local want = wantJson()
				if ch:GetAttribute("EquippedGloves") ~= want then
					if saved[ch] == nil then saved[ch] = { v = ch:GetAttribute("EquippedGloves") } end
					ch:SetAttribute("EquippedGloves", want)
					if force or os.clock() - lastRebuild > 1.5 then rebuild() end
				elseif force then
					rebuild()
				end
			else
				local sv = saved[ch]
				if sv then
					saved[ch] = nil
					ch:SetAttribute("EquippedGloves", sv.v)
					rebuild()
				end
			end
		end

		local pending = false
		local function onChanged()
			if pending then return end
			pending = true
			task.defer(function()
				pending = false
				pcall(apply, true)
				-- once more shortly after: the per-model skin menu has switched by then, and a viewmodel that was
				-- busy equipping on the first pass gets rebuilt too
				task.delay(0.35, function() pcall(apply, true) end)
			end)
		end
		gloveOn:OnChanged(onChanged)
		gloveModel:OnChanged(onChanged)
		for _, dd in pairs(gloveSkin) do dd:OnChanged(onChanged) end

		gloveSec:AddButton("Refresh", function()
			pcall(apply, true)
			-- button callbacks run without the elevated identity: restore it before Notify
			fixId()
			pcall(Window.Notify, Window, "Gloves refreshed")
		end)

		-- the moment a new character gets its EquippedGloves from the server, replace it -- before the
		-- viewmodels are built from it -- then rebuild a few times as the loadout appears after a respawn
		local charConns = {}
		local function hookCharacter(ch)
			if not ch then return end
			for _, c in ipairs(charConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			table.clear(charConns)
			charConns[1] = ch:GetAttributeChangedSignal("EquippedGloves"):Connect(function()
				if gloveOn:Get() and ch:GetAttribute("EquippedGloves") ~= wantJson() then
					pcall(apply, false)
				end
			end)
			if gloveOn:Get() then pcall(apply, false) end
		end
		local function afterRespawn()
			hookCharacter(lp.Character)
			for _, d in ipairs({ 0.4, 1.2, 2.5 }) do
				task.delay(d, function()
					if gloveOn:Get() then pcall(apply, true) end
				end)
			end
		end
		local charAddedConn = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
			hookCharacter(ch)
			afterRespawn()
		end)
		local deadGloveConn = lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Dead"):Connect(function()
			if lp:GetAttribute("Dead") ~= true then afterRespawn() end
		end)
		hookCharacter(lp.Character)

		-- keeps the choice on a respawn or when the server rewrites the attribute
		local alive = true
		task.spawn(function()
			while alive do
				pcall(apply, false)
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
		end)

		env.__gsGloveCleanup = function()
			alive = false
			pcall(function() charAddedConn:Disconnect() end)
			pcall(function() deadGloveConn:Disconnect() end)
			for _, c in ipairs(charConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			pcall(function()
				local ch = lp.Character
				local sv = ch and saved[ch]
				if sv then
					saved[ch] = nil
					ch:SetAttribute("EquippedGloves", sv.v)
					rebuild()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

-- ───────────── debug state ─────────────
local dbg = {
	state = "starting",
	hit = "-",
	model = "-",
	how = "-",
	team = "-",
	dist = 0,
	clicks = 0,
	lastClick = 0,
	clickErr = nil,
	modelRef = nil,
	onEnemy = false,
	silentTarget = "None",
	silentDist = 0,
	silentStatus = "Searching...",
	ghostSource = "-",
	wallbangOn = false,
}

local aimOffset = Vector2.new(0, 1)
local firstPersonCF, thirdPersonCF = nil, nil

-- ───────────── crosshair + aim-point utilities (shared everywhere) ─────────────
local getCrosshair, menusOpen, aimPoint
do
	local cachedCrosshair, lastSearch = nil, 0

	local function findCrosshair()
		local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		if not pg then return nil end

		local mainGui = pg:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
		local gameplay = mainGui and mainGui:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
		local middle = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("Middle")
		local direct = middle and middle:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
		if direct and direct:IsA("GuiObject") then return direct end

		for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
			if d.Name == "Crosshair" and d:IsA("GuiObject") and d:FindFirstChild("Ticks") then
				return d
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	getCrosshair = function()
		if cachedCrosshair and cachedCrosshair:IsDescendantOf(game) then
			return cachedCrosshair
		end
		cachedCrosshair = nil
		if os.clock() - lastSearch < 1 then return nil end
		lastSearch = os.clock()
		cachedCrosshair = findCrosshair()
		return cachedCrosshair
	end

	local MENUS = { "TeamSelection", "Leaderboard", "EndScreen", "BuyMenu" }
	menusOpen = function()
		local ch = getCrosshair()
		local parent = ch and ch.Parent
		if not parent then return false end
		for _, n in ipairs(MENUS) do
			local m = parent:FindFirstChild(n)
			if m and m:IsA("GuiObject") and m.Visible then return true, n end
		end
		return false
	end

	aimPoint = function()
		local ch = getCrosshair()
		if ch then
			local target, src = ch, "outer frame"
			local inner = ch:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
			if inner and inner:IsA("GuiObject") and inner.Visible and inner.AbsoluteSize.Magnitude > 0 then
				target, src = inner, "inner image"
			end

			local p = target.AbsolutePosition + target.AbsoluteSize / 2
			p += GuiService:GetGuiInset()
			p += aimOffset
			return p, src
		end
		return UserInputService:GetMouseLocation(), "mouse fallback (crosshair not found)"
	end
end

-- Camera / Screen widgets (shared: used by silent aim and the main loop too)
local fovOn, fovVal, resOn, resVal
do
	local camSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Camera", "Left")
	fovOn = camSec:AddToggle("Custom FOV", false)
	fovVal = camSec:AddSlider("Field of view", 40, 120, 90, nil, "°", 1)
	local resSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Screen", "Left")
	resOn = resSec:AddToggle("Stretched resolution", false)
	resVal = resSec:AddSlider("Resolution", 30, 100, 65, nil, "%", 1)
end

-- Team / player shared utilities
local TEAM_FOLDERS = { ["Terrorists"] = true, ["Counter-Terrorists"] = true }

local playerFromCharacter

-- every team name ("Terrorists", "Counter-Terrorists", "T", "CT"...) becomes "T" or "CT" so
-- results from different sources can be compared
local function normTeam(v)
	if v == nil then return nil end
	local t = string.lower(tostring(v))
	if t == "ct" or string.find(t, "counter", 1, true) then return "CT" end
	if t == "t" or string.find(t, "terror", 1, true) then return "T" end
	return tostring(v)
end

-- the team the game itself assigned (Player attribute "Team", the same one the game's own
-- scripts read); far more reliable than guessing from the armor a model wears
local function assignedTeam(model, plr)
	plr = plr or (model and playerFromCharacter(model))
	if plr then
		local a = plr:GetAttribute("Team")
		if a ~= nil then return normTeam(a) end
		if plr.Team then return normTeam(plr.Team.Name) end
	end
	if model then
		local a = model:GetAttribute("Team")
		if a ~= nil then return normTeam(a) end
	end
	return nil
end

local function armorGuess(model)
	local armor = model and model:FindFirstChild("CharacterArmor")
	if not armor then return nil end
	if armor:FindFirstChild("Balaclava") then return "T" end
	if armor:FindFirstChild("Helmet")
		or armor:FindFirstChild("HeadCover")
		or armor:FindFirstChild("VestDetails") then
		return "CT"
	end
	return nil
end

-- first the assigned team, the armor guess only when the game gives no team at all
local function armorTeam(model)
	return assignedTeam(model) or armorGuess(model)
end

playerFromCharacter = function(model)
	local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
	if plr then return plr end
	local byName = Players:FindFirstChild(model.Name)
	if byName and byName:IsA("Player") then return byName end
	return nil
end

local function backupTeam(model, plr)
	local par = model and model.Parent
	if par and TEAM_FOLDERS[par.Name] then return normTeam(par.Name) end
	return assignedTeam(model, plr)
end

local function myTeam()
	local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
	local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
	local a = lp:GetAttribute("Team")
	if a ~= nil then return normTeam(a) end
	return armorTeam(lp.Character) or armorTeam(mine) or backupTeam(lp.Character, lp)
end

local function characterRoots()
	local roots = {}
	if lp.Character then roots[#roots + 1] = lp.Character end
	local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
	local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
	if mine and mine ~= lp.Character then roots[#roots + 1] = mine end
	return roots
end

local function isSpectating()
	local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local subj = cam and cam.CameraSubject
	if subj then
		local m = subj
		if subj:IsA("Humanoid") or subj:IsA("BasePart") then m = subj.Parent end
		if m and m:IsA("Model") and m.Name ~= lp.Name then return true end
	end
	if lp:GetAttribute("Dead") == true then return true end
	local char = lp.Character
	if not char or char:GetAttribute("Dead") == true then return true end
	return false
end

local ghostFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
ghostFolder.Name = "GhostProxyFolder"
ghostFolder.Parent = workspace

local tpGun = { on = false, active = false, chams = nil }
local isCharacterDead, cleanupAllGhosts, ghostConn, pickMyModel

-- ───────────── GHOST PROXIES ─────────────
do
	local lagSec = RageTab:AddSection("Ghost Proxies", "Left")
	local lagOn = lagSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local lagTargetMode = lagSec:AddDropdown("Ghost Targets", { "Only Me", "Other Players" }, "Only Me")
	local lagTeamCheck = lagSec:AddToggle("Ghost Team check", false)
	local lagDelay = lagSec:AddSlider("Lag Ticks", 1, 15, 6, nil, " ticks", 1)
	local lagMaterial = lagSec:AddDropdown("Ghost Material", {
		"ForceField", "Neon", "Glass", "Plastic", "SmoothPlastic", "Metal", "Ice",
	}, "ForceField")
	local lagColorOn = lagSec:AddToggle("Ghost custom colour", false)
	local lagColor = lagColorOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), false)
	local lagTransVal = lagSec:AddSlider("Ghost Transparency", 0, 100, 35, nil, "%", 5)

	local ghostProxies = {}

	local GHOST_HIDDEN = { HumanoidRootPart = true, CameraPart = true }
	local GHOST_BODY = {
		Head = true, Torso = true, UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
		["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true, ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
		LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
		RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
		LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
		RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
	}

	local function destroyGhost(char)
		local data = ghostProxies[char]
		if not data then return end
		ghostProxies[char] = nil
		for _, c in ipairs(data.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		if data.model then pcall(function() data.model:Destroy() end) end
	end

	cleanupAllGhosts = function()
		for char in pairs(ghostProxies) do destroyGhost(char) end
	end

	isCharacterDead = function(character)
		if not character or not character.Parent then return true end

		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return true
		end

		if character:GetAttribute("Dead") == true or character:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true then
			return true
		end

		local healthAttr = character:GetAttribute("Health")
		if type(healthAttr) == "number" and healthAttr <= 0 then
			return true
		end

		local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not rootPart or not rootPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
			return true
		end

		return false
	end

	local function localDead()
		if lp:GetAttribute("Dead") == true then return true end
		local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
		if mine then return isCharacterDead(mine) end
		return lp.Character == nil or isCharacterDead(lp.Character)
	end

	local function countParts(m)
		local n = 0
		for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then n += 1 end
		end
		return n
	end

	local myPick, myPickAt = nil, 0
	pickMyModel = function()
		if myPick and myPick.Parent and os.clock() - myPickAt < 0.5 then return myPick end
		myPickAt = os.clock()

		local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local folderModel = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)

		local best, bestN = nil, -1
		for _, m in ipairs({ folderModel, lp.Character }) do
			if m and m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local n = countParts(m)
				if n > bestN then best, bestN = m, n end
			end
		end

		myPick = best
		return best
	end

	local otherPick = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function pickFor(plr)
		local c = otherPick[plr]
		if c and c.model and c.model.Parent and os.clock() - c.at < 0.5 then return c.model end

		local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local folderModel = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)
		if folderModel and not folderModel:IsA("Model") then folderModel = nil end

		local best, bestN = nil, -1
		for _, m in ipairs({ folderModel, plr.Character }) do
			if m and m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				local n = countParts(m)
				if n > bestN then best, bestN = m, n end
			end
		end

		otherPick[plr] = { model = best, at = os.clock() }
		return best
	end

	local function pairTree(o, g, out)
		local used = {}
		for _, oc in ipairs(o:GetChildren()) do
			for _, gc in ipairs(g:GetChildren()) do
				if not used[gc] and gc.Name == oc.Name and gc.ClassName == oc.ClassName then
					used[gc] = true
					if oc:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = { oc, gc } end
					pairTree(oc, gc, out)
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function ghostAlpha(p)
		local n = string.lower(p.Name)
		if GHOST_HIDDEN[p.Name] or string.find(n, "hitbox", 1, true) or string.find(n, "muzzle", 1, true) then
			return 1
		end
		if p.Transparency >= 0.99 and not (p:IsA("MeshPart") or GHOST_BODY[p.Name]) then
			return 1
		end
		return math.clamp(lagTransVal:Get() / 100, 0, 1)
	end

	local function buildGhost(char, data)
		if data.model then pcall(function() data.model:Destroy() end) data.model = nil end
		table.clear(data.pairs)
		table.clear(data.history)
		data.lastBuild = os.clock()

		local wasArchivable = char.Archivable
		char.Archivable = true
		local ok, model = pcall(function() return char:Clone() end)
		char.Archivable = wasArchivable
		if not ok or not model then return end

		pairTree(char, model, data.pairs)

		local armor = model:FindFirstChild("CharacterArmor")
		if armor then pcall(function() armor:Destroy() end) end

		for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("JointInstance") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") or d:IsA("Constraint")
				or d:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or d:IsA("Humanoid") or d:IsA("Sound")
				or d:IsA("ForceField") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture")
				or d:IsA("SpecialMesh") or d:IsA("BlockMesh") or d:IsA("CylinderMesh")
				or d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Beam")
				or d:IsA("Light") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Sparkles")
				or d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
				pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
			end
		end

		local matName = lagMaterial:Get()
		local matEnum = Enum.Material[matName] or Enum.Material.ForceField
		local useColor = lagColorOn:Get()
		local color = lagColor:Get()
		data.baseColors = {}

		for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then
				data.baseColors[d] = d.Color
				d.Anchored = true
				d.CanCollide = false
				d.CanQuery = false
				d.CanTouch = false
				d.CastShadow = false
				d.Transparency = ghostAlpha(d)
				d.Material = matEnum
				if useColor then d.Color = color end
			end
		end

		model.Name = "Ghost_" .. char.Name
		model.Parent = ghostFolder
		data.model = model
	end

	local function refreshGhostAppearance(data)
		if not data.model or not data.model.Parent then return end

		local useColor = lagColorOn:Get()
		local selectedColor = lagColor:Get()
		local material = Enum.Material[lagMaterial:Get()] or Enum.Material.ForceField

		for _, pair in ipairs(data.pairs) do
			local source, proxy = pair[1], pair[2]
			if source and source.Parent and proxy and proxy.Parent then
				proxy.Material = material
				proxy.Transparency = ghostAlpha(source)
				if useColor then
					proxy.Color = selectedColor
				elseif data.baseColors and data.baseColors[proxy] then
					proxy.Color = data.baseColors[proxy]
				end
			end
		end
	end

	lagMaterial:OnChanged(function()
		for _, data in pairs(ghostProxies) do data.dirty = true end
	end)
	lagColorOn:OnChanged(function()
		for _, data in pairs(ghostProxies) do data.dirty = true end
	end)
	lagColor.OnChanged = function()
		for _, data in pairs(ghostProxies) do data.dirty = true end
	end
	lagTransVal:OnChanged(function()
		for _, data in pairs(ghostProxies) do data.dirty = true end
	end)

	local function collectGhostTargets()
		local list = {}

		if lagTargetMode:Get() == "Only Me" then
			local m = pickMyModel()
			if m then list[1] = m end
			return list
		end

		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= lp then
				local m = pickFor(plr)
				if m then
					local isEnemy = true
					if lagTeamCheck:Get() then
						local mine = myTeam()
						local theirs = armorTeam(m) or backupTeam(m, plr)
						if mine and theirs and mine == theirs then isEnemy = false end
					end
					if isEnemy then list[#list + 1] = m end
				end
			end
		end

		return list
	end

	ghostConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not lagOn:Get() or localDead() then
			if next(ghostProxies) then cleanupAllGhosts() end
			dbg.ghostSource = lagOn:Get() and "none (you are dead)" or "off"
			return
		end

		local maxTicks = math.max(1, math.floor(lagDelay:Get()))
		local active = {}
		local count = 0

		for _, char in ipairs(collectGhostTargets()) do
			if char.Parent and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not isCharacterDead(char) then
				active[char] = true

				local data = ghostProxies[char]
				if not data then
					data = { model = nil, pairs = {}, history = {}, dirty = true, conns = {}, lastBuild = 0 }
					ghostProxies[char] = data
					local function mark(d)
						if d:IsA("BasePart") then data.dirty = true end
					end
					data.conns[#data.conns + 1] = char.DescendantAdded:Connect(mark)
					data.conns[#data.conns + 1] = char.DescendantRemoving:Connect(mark)
				end

				if not data.model or not data.model.Parent
					or (data.dirty and os.clock() - data.lastBuild > 0.25) then
					data.dirty = false
					buildGhost(char, data)
				end

				if data.model then
					count += 1
					refreshGhostAppearance(data)

					local snap = table.create(#data.pairs)
					for i, pr in ipairs(data.pairs) do
						snap[i] = pr[1].Parent and pr[1].CFrame or false
					end
					table.insert(data.history, 1, snap)
					while #data.history > maxTicks do
						table.remove(data.history)
					end

					local old = data.history[#data.history]
					for i, pr in ipairs(data.pairs) do
						local cf = old[i]
						if cf and pr[2].Parent then pr[2].CFrame = cf end
					end

					if lagTargetMode:Get() == "Only Me" then
						dbg.ghostSource = char:GetFullName() .. " (" .. #data.pairs .. " parts)"
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if lagTargetMode:Get() ~= "Only Me" then
			dbg.ghostSource = "other players (" .. count .. " ghosts)"
		elseif count == 0 then
			dbg.ghostSource = "none found"
		end

		for char in pairs(ghostProxies) do
			if not active[char] then destroyGhost(char) end
		end
	end)
end

local fovCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
fovCircle.Visible = false
fovCircle.Thickness = 1
fovCircle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
fovCircle.Filled = false
fovCircle.Transparency = 0.7

local spawnBulletTracer = function(rayData, redirectedPart) end
local getClosestPlayerPartInFov
local registerOwnHit = function(userId, partName) end -- reassigned by the hit-sound block below

-- ───────────── SILENT AIM ─────────────
do
	local rageSec = RageTab:AddSection("Silent Aim", "Left")
	local silentOn = rageSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local silentFovVal = rageSec:AddSlider("Silent FOV", 10, 500, 300, nil, "px", 5)
	local silentFovTrans = rageSec:AddSlider("FOV Transparency", 0, 100, 30, nil, "%", 5)
	local silentIgnoreFov = rageSec:AddToggle("Ignore FOV", false)
	local silentTargetPart = rageSec:AddDropdown("Silent Target Part", { "Head", "Body", "Any" }, "Head")
	local silentPriority = rageSec:AddDropdown("Target priority", {
		"Closest to crosshair", "Closest distance", "Lowest health", "Looking at me", "Killable first",
	}, "Closest to crosshair")
	-- HvH-style kill logic: if one body shot already kills, shoot the (bigger, safer) body instead of
	-- gambling on the head; targets that one shot can finish are picked first with "Killable first"
	local silentSmartKill = rageSec:AddToggle("Smart hitbox (go for the kill)", true)
	local silentTeamCheck = rageSec:AddToggle("Team check", false)
	local silentIgnoreHostages = rageSec:AddToggle("Ignore hostages", false)
	local silentIgnoreHighlight = rageSec:AddToggle("Ignore highlighted", false)
	local silentWallbang = rageSec:AddToggle("Wallbang / Penetration", false)
	local silentAutoPen = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto penetration (weapon)", false)
	silentAutoPen:DependsOn(silentWallbang, function(v) return v end)
	local silentPenPower = rageSec:AddSlider("Penetration power (manual)", 0, 150, 40, nil, "", 1)
	silentPenPower:DependsOn(silentWallbang, function(v) return v end)
	local silentLethal = rageSec:AddToggle("Wallbang: only lethal shots", false)
	silentLethal:DependsOn(silentWallbang, function(v) return v end)
	local silentWallLoss = rageSec:AddSlider("Est. damage lost in walls", 0, 90, 40, nil, "%", 5)
	silentWallLoss:DependsOn(silentWallbang, function(v) return v end)
	-- studs of penetration budget held back, so borderline shots (the ones the server rejects) are skipped
	local silentWallSafety = rageSec:AddSlider("Wallbang safety margin", 0, 3, 0.6, nil, " studs", 0.1)
	silentWallSafety:DependsOn(silentWallbang, function(v) return v end)
	-- auto shoot only takes a wallbang that uses at most this much of the bullet's penetration budget: a
	-- shot that barely squeaks through by the estimate is the one the server rejects or deals nothing for
	local silentAutoPenUse = rageSec:AddSlider("Auto wallbang max pen use", 10, 100, 65, nil, "%", 5)
	local silentMultipoint = rageSec:AddToggle("Multipoint", false)
	local silentMaxDist = rageSec:AddSlider("Max distance", 50, 3000, 3000, nil, " studs", 50)
	local silentHitChance = rageSec:AddSlider("Hit chance", 0, 100, 100, nil, "%", 5)
	local silentSticky = rageSec:AddToggle("Sticky target", false)
	-- lead moving targets: the server sees them a little ahead of what you render
	local silentPredict = rageSec:AddSlider("Prediction", 0, 200, 0, nil, "ms", 5)
	local silentAutoShoot = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot", false)
	local silentAutoDelay = rageSec:AddSlider("Auto shoot delay", 0, 500, 60, nil, "ms", 10)
	silentAutoDelay:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	local silentAutoVisible = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot: visible only", false)
	silentAutoVisible:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- fire at any wallbangable enemy (through walls) even when the shot isn't a confirmed kill
	local silentAutoWall = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot: wallbang any", true)
	silentAutoWall:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- fire as soon as ANY body part is visible (arms / legs count too), not just the preferred part
	local silentAutoAny = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot: any visible part", false)
	silentAutoAny:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- every shot (auto AND manual) respects the Target part setting instead of falling back to the body
	local silentAutoPart = rageSec:AddToggle("Respect target part", false)
	silentAutoPart:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- HvH-style: only fire when the estimated damage (after walls) reaches this, or it kills them outright
	local silentAutoMinDmg = rageSec:AddSlider("Auto shoot min damage", 0, 100, 0, nil, "", 1)
	silentAutoMinDmg:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- the server checks a shot against the real look direction: turn onto the best spot, then fire
	local silentAutoFace = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot: face best spot", true)
	silentAutoFace:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- HvH reaction speed: scan every frame, no delay, short spawn grace, quick turn onto the target
	local silentAutoFast = rageSec:AddToggle("Auto shoot: HvH fast mode", true)
	silentAutoFast:DependsOn(silentAutoShoot, function(v) return v end)
	-- read by the main loop, which fires the click (it owns the click helper)
	dbg.silentAuto = { enabled = silentOn, on = silentAutoShoot, delay = silentAutoDelay, visibleOnly = silentAutoVisible, minDmg = silentAutoMinDmg, wallAny = silentAutoWall, anyPart = silentAutoAny, respectPart = silentAutoPart, face = silentAutoFace, fast = silentAutoFast }

	-- ── material penetration data, copied from ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Raycast ──
	local MAP_MIN_PEN = table.freeze({ Reactor = -1 })
	local VARIANT_PEN = table.freeze({ ["Sandy Brick"] = 0, IndoorWall = 0 })
	local MATERIAL_PEN = table.freeze({
		[Enum.Material.Asphalt] = 0, [Enum.Material.Basalt] = 0, [Enum.Material.Brick] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Cobblestone] = 0, [Enum.Material.Concrete] = 0, [Enum.Material.CrackedLava] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.DiamondPlate] = 0, [Enum.Material.Foil] = 0, [Enum.Material.Glacier] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Granite] = 0, [Enum.Material.Grass] = 0, [Enum.Material.Ground] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Ice] = 0, [Enum.Material.LeafyGrass] = 0, [Enum.Material.Limestone] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Marble] = 0, [Enum.Material.Metal] = 0, [Enum.Material.Mud] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Pavement] = 0, [Enum.Material.Rock] = 0, [Enum.Material.Salt] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Sand] = 0, [Enum.Material.Sandstone] = 0, [Enum.Material.Slate] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Snow] = 0, [Enum.Material.ForceField] = 0, [Enum.Material.Neon] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.CorrodedMetal] = 0, [Enum.Material.Pebble] = 0, [Enum.Material.CeramicTiles] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Plaster] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Plastic] = 10, [Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic] = 10, [Enum.Material.Wood] = 10,
		[Enum.Material.WoodPlanks] = 10, [Enum.Material.Cardboard] = 10,
		[Enum.Material.Glass] = 25, [Enum.Material.Fabric] = 25,
	})

	local function applyMapMinPenetration(v)
		if v ~= 0 then return v end
		local mapName = workspace:GetAttribute("Map")
		if typeof(mapName) ~= "string" then return v end
		return MAP_MIN_PEN[mapName] or v
	end

	-- parts that never count as an obstruction (accessories, ragdoll capsules, roots)
	local function penIsFiltered(part)
		if part:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Accessory") then return true end
		local ok, tagged = pcall(function() return part:HasTag("CharacterAccessory") end)
		if ok and tagged then return true end
		if part.Name == "CollisionCapsule" then return true end
		if part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then return true end
		return false
	end

	local function penMaterialOf(part)
		local p = part.Parent
		local ok, door = pcall(function() return p and p:HasTag("BreakableDoor") end)
		if ok and door then return Enum.Material.Metal end
		return part.Material
	end

	-- depth (studs) the bullet travels through `part`: cast from far along the ray
	-- back toward the entry point and find the exit surface
	local depthParams = RaycastParams.new()
	depthParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	depthParams.CollisionGroup = "Bullet"

	local function penetrationDepth(entryPos, dirUnit, part)
		depthParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { part }
		local far = entryPos + dirUnit * 1000
		local hit = workspace:Raycast(far, entryPos - far, depthParams)
		if not hit then return nil end
		return hit.Position, (entryPos - hit.Position).Magnitude
	end

	-- Walks the shot from origin to the aim point the same way the game's Shared.Raycast does.
	-- Returns: canReach, totalStudsThroughWalls, fractionOfPenetrationBudgetUsed (0..1)
	local function traceShot(origin, targetPart, ignoreList, penPower, aimPos, conservative, safety)
		local toTarget = (aimPos or targetPart.Position) - origin
		local dist = toTarget.Magnitude
		if dist < 0.001 then return true, 0, 0 end
		local dirUnit = toTarget.Unit

		local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
		rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		rayParams.CollisionGroup = "Bullet"
		local filterList = table.clone(ignoreList)
		rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList

		local matDepth, variantDepth = {}, {}
		local pos, traveled = origin, 0
		local totalDepth, worstFrac = 0, 0

		for _ = 1, 25 do
			local remaining = dist - traveled
			if remaining <= 0 then return true, totalDepth, worstFrac end

			local hit = workspace:Raycast(pos, dirUnit * remaining, rayParams)
			if not hit then return true, totalDepth, worstFrac end -- nothing left in the way

			if hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent) then
				return true, totalDepth, worstFrac -- reached the target's own body
			end

			if penIsFiltered(hit.Instance) then
				filterList[#filterList + 1] = hit.Instance
				rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList
				pos = hit.Position
				traveled = (pos - origin):Dot(dirUnit)
			else
				local exitPos, depth = penetrationDepth(hit.Position, dirUnit, hit.Instance)
				if not exitPos then
					-- couldn't measure it: normal aim trusts it, auto shoot (conservative) does not
					dbg.penBlock = "exit unmeasurable: " .. hit.Instance.Name .. " (" .. tostring(hit.Instance.Material) .. ")"
					return not conservative, totalDepth, worstFrac
				end

				local variant = hit.Instance.MaterialVariant
				local tbl, key, base
				if variant == "" or VARIANT_PEN[variant] == nil then
					local mat = penMaterialOf(hit.Instance)
					tbl, key, base = matDepth, mat, MATERIAL_PEN[mat]
				else
					tbl, key, base = variantDepth, variant, VARIANT_PEN[variant]
				end

				tbl[key] = (tbl[key] or 0) + depth
				local budget = applyMapMinPenetration(base or 0) + penPower
				if budget - (safety or 0) < tbl[key] then
					dbg.penBlock = ("%s depth %.1f > budget %.1f (weapon pen %.1f)"):format(tostring(key), tbl[key], budget, penPower)
					return false -- this material used up the whole penetration budget (minus the safety margin)
				end

				local f = budget > 0 and (tbl[key] / budget) or 1
				if f > worstFrac then worstFrac = f end
				totalDepth += depth

				filterList[#filterList + 1] = hit.Instance
				rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterList
				pos = exitPos
				traveled = (pos - origin):Dot(dirUnit)
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	-- a Highlight tagged GSChams is our own ESP's, not a sign the target was flagged
	-- by something else, so it must never cause "Ignore highlighted" to skip them
	local function hasActiveHighlight(character)
		for _, c in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("Highlight") and c.Enabled and not c:GetAttribute("GSChams") then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function silentFovRadius()
		local currentCamFov = (fovOn:Get() and not env.__gsScopedNow and lp:GetAttribute("IsSniperScoped") ~= true) and fovVal:Get() or camera.FieldOfView
		return silentFovVal:Get() * math.clamp(currentCamFov / 70, 0.5, 2)
	end

	-- penetration the equipped weapon really has (recorded from the game's Bullet class)
	local function currentPen()
		if silentAutoPen:Get() and type(dbg.activePen) == "number" then return dbg.activePen end
		return silentPenPower:Get()
	end

	-- plain line of sight from origin to the aim point; a hit within `tolerance` studs of
	-- the point (grazing a corner / the target's own gear) still counts as visible
	local function directVisible(origin, part, ignoreList, aimPos, tolerance)
		local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
		rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList
		rayParams.CollisionGroup = "Bullet"

		local direction = (aimPos or part.Position) - origin
		local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, rayParams)
		if not result then return true end
		if result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(part.Parent) then return true end
		return direction.Magnitude - result.Distance <= tolerance
	end

	-- best-effort health of a target (Humanoid, then the Health attribute)
	local function targetHealth(character)
		local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then return hum.Health end
		local hp = character:GetAttribute("Health")
		if type(hp) == "number" then return hp end
		local plr = playerFromCharacter(character)
		if plr then
			local v = plr:GetAttribute("Health")
			if type(v) == "number" then return v end
		end
		return nil
	end

	RunService:BindToRenderStep("SilentAimFovCircle", 3055, function()
		local on = silentOn:Get() and not silentIgnoreFov:Get()
		fovCircle.Visible = on
		if on then
			fovCircle.Position = camera.ViewportSize * 0.5
			fovCircle.Radius = silentFovRadius()
			fovCircle.Transparency = math.clamp(1 - (silentFovTrans:Get() / 100), 0, 1)
		end
		dbg.wallbangOn = silentWallbang:Get()
	end)

	local SILENT_BODY_PARTS = {
		["Torso"] = true,
		["UpperTorso"] = true,
		["LowerTorso"] = true,
		["HumanoidRootPart"] = true,
	}
	local LIMB_PARTS = {
		["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true, ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
		LeftUpperArm = true, RightUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, RightLowerArm = true,
		LeftUpperLeg = true, RightUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true,
	}

	-- horizontal velocity of a target, smoothed (the game moves bodies by CFrame, so no physics velocity)
	local velTrack = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function targetVelocity(m)
		local root = m and (m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m:FindFirstChild("Torso") or m:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
		if not root then return Vector3.zero end
		local now = os.clock()
		local r = velTrack[m]
		if not r then
			velTrack[m] = { pos = root.Position, t = now, vel = Vector3.zero }
			return Vector3.zero
		end
		local dt = now - r.t
		if dt >= 0.05 then
			local nv = (root.Position - r.pos) / dt
			nv = Vector3.new(nv.X, 0, nv.Z)
			if nv.Magnitude > 60 then nv = Vector3.zero end -- a teleport, not movement
			r.vel = r.vel:Lerp(nv, 0.6)
			r.pos, r.t = root.Position, now
		end
		return r.vel
	end

	local function silentParts(character, mode)
		local head, body = nil, {}

		if mode ~= "Body" then
			local h = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if h and h:IsA("BasePart") and h.Transparency < 0.99 and h.Size.Magnitude > 0 then
