				head = h
			end
		end

		if mode ~= "Head" then
			for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Transparency < 0.99 and part.Size.Magnitude > 0
					and (SILENT_BODY_PARTS[part.Name] or ((mode == "Any" or mode == "Head > Any") and LIMB_PARTS[part.Name])) then
					body[#body + 1] = part
				end
			end
		end

		return head, body
	end

	local function adjustForStretchedRes(screenVector2)
		if not resOn:Get() then return screenVector2 end
		local viewportSize = camera.ViewportSize
		local center = viewportSize * 0.5
		local resFactor = resVal:Get() / 100

		local dx = (screenVector2.X - center.X) / resFactor
		return Vector2.new(center.X + dx, screenVector2.Y)
	end

	local stickyChar, stickyAt = nil, 0
	local STICKY_BIAS = 0.6 -- lower = harder to switch away from the current target
	local MAX_TRACES = 90   -- penetration traces allowed per target search (keeps it fast)

	local SPAWN_GRACE = 0.4
	local seenSince = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function validSince(character)
		local t = seenSince[character]
		if not t then
			t = os.clock()
			seenSince[character] = t
		end
		return t
	end

	local function forgetValid(character)
		seenSince[character] = nil
	end

	local function pickSilentTarget(origin, cheap, strict, auto)
		local mode = silentTargetPart:Get()
		-- while auto shooting (or hunting wallbangs) a body shot is acceptable too,
		-- so the bullet must be allowed to land on the body instead of waiting for the head --
		-- unless "respect target part" is on: then EVERY shot holds out for the chosen part only
		local respectPart = dbg.silentAuto and dbg.silentAuto.respectPart and dbg.silentAuto.respectPart:Get()
		if mode == "Head" and not respectPart
			and (strict or auto or os.clock() - (dbg.autoAt or 0) < 0.35 or silentWallbang:Get()) then
			mode = "Head > Body"
		end
		-- "any visible part": auto shoot fires as soon as any hitbox is exposed -- head and
		-- torso are still preferred (they come first), arms and legs are a valid fallback
		if auto and dbg.silentAuto and dbg.silentAuto.anyPart and dbg.silentAuto.anyPart:Get() then
			mode = "Head > Any"
		end
		local priority = silentPriority:Get()
		local ignoreFov = silentIgnoreFov:Get()
		local saCfg = dbg.silentAuto
		local autoMin = auto and saCfg and saCfg.minDmg and saCfg.minDmg:Get() or nil
		local fovRadius = silentFovRadius()
		local viewportCenter = camera.ViewportSize * 0.5
		local aimCF = camera.CFrame
		origin = origin or aimCF.Position

		local charactersFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		if not charactersFolder then
			dbg.silentStatus = "No Characters Folder"
			return nil
		end

		local mine = {}
		local ignore = { camera, ghostFolder }
		if lp.Character then
			mine[lp.Character] = true
			ignore[#ignore + 1] = lp.Character
		end
		local mineModel = charactersFolder:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
		if mineModel then
			mine[mineModel] = true
			ignore[#ignore + 1] = mineModel
		end

		local best, bestScore, bestPos = nil, math.huge, nil
		local blocked, traceCount = 0, 0

		-- the shot leaves from the eye / camera, but a corner can hide the target from the head: for
		-- strict (visible only) checks the line must be clear from BOTH
		local eye2
		do
			local mm = pickMyModel and pickMyModel()
			local hd = mm and mm:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if hd and (hd.Position - origin).Magnitude > 0.3 then eye2 = hd.Position end
		end
		local maxStuds = math.min(silentMaxDist:Get(), dbg.activeRange or 3000)

		--will find wallbangs
		local function candidatePoints(part)
			local pts = { part.Position }

			if silentMultipoint:Get() or auto or silentWallbang:Get() then
				local cf, sz = part.CFrame, part.Size
				local dense = part.Name ~= "Head"
				-- inner points only: a point near the edge of the part falls outside the server's slightly
				-- different hitbox (positions are a little behind what you see), which showed up as misses
				local ox, oy = sz.X * 0.22, sz.Y * 0.22
				pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(0, oy, 0)).Position
				pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(0, -oy, 0)).Position
				pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(ox, 0, 0)).Position
				pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(-ox, 0, 0)).Position
				if dense then
					pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(ox, oy, 0)).Position
					pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(-ox, oy, 0)).Position
					pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(ox, -oy, 0)).Position
					pts[#pts + 1] = (cf * CFrame.new(-ox, -oy, 0)).Position
				end
			end
			return pts
		end

		-- can the shot reach this point? returns canReach, visible, penetrationFraction, wallStuds
		local function reachInfo(part, pt)
			-- 2.5 studs of tolerance counted a wall right in front of the target as "visible" and the
			-- shot was never checked against the wall; with wallbang on the tolerance is kept tight
			if directVisible(origin, part, ignore, pt, strict and 0.4 or ((silentWallbang:Get() or auto) and 0.35 or 2.5))
				and (not strict or not eye2 or directVisible(eye2, part, ignore, pt, 0.4)) then
				return true, true, 0, 0
			end
			-- a target behind a wall is still fair game whenever Wallbang is on, even with
			-- "Auto shoot: visible only" checked — that box only makes a clear shot preferred
			-- over a wallbang (see the scoring in `evaluate`), it never blocks wallbangs outright
			-- auto shoot hunts wallbangs on its own, even with the Wallbang toggle off
			if not (silentWallbang:Get() or auto) then return false end
			if traceCount >= (auto and 240 or MAX_TRACES) then return false end
			traceCount += 1
			local safety = silentWallSafety:Get()
			local ok, can, depth, frac = pcall(traceShot, origin, part, ignore, currentPen(), pt, auto, safety)
			if not ok then
				if auto then return false end -- auto shoot never fires on a guess
				return true, false, 1, 0 -- couldn't trace: assume possible
			end
			if not can then return false end
			if auto and (frac or 0) * 100 > silentAutoPenUse:Get() then
				dbg.penBlock = ("too much of the pen budget: %.0f%% > %d%%"):format((frac or 0) * 100, silentAutoPenUse:Get())
				return false
			end
			-- the server may start the shot from the head instead of the camera: that line has to work too
			if eye2 then
				local ok2, can2, depth2, frac2 = pcall(traceShot, eye2, part, ignore, currentPen(), pt, auto, safety)
				if ok2 then
					if not can2 then return false end
					depth = math.max(depth or 0, depth2 or 0)
					frac = math.max(frac or 0, frac2 or 0)
					if auto and frac * 100 > silentAutoPenUse:Get() then
						dbg.penBlock = ("head line uses too much of the pen budget: %.0f%%"):format(frac * 100)
						return false
					end
				end
			end
			return true, false, frac or 0, depth or 0
		end

		-- returns score (lower is better) and the point to aim at, or nil
		local function evaluate(part, hp)
			local toPart = part.Position - aimCF.Position
			local studs = toPart.Magnitude
			if studs < 0.001 then return nil end
			if studs > maxStuds then return nil end

			local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
			local screenVector2 = adjustForStretchedRes(Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y))
			local pixels = (screenVector2 - viewportCenter).Magnitude

			if not ignoreFov and not (onScreen and pixels <= fovRadius) then return nil end

			local aimPos, visible, aimEst = part.Position, true, nil
			local guess = false
			if not cheap then
				-- pick the best point on the part: a visible point beats a wallbang point,
				-- and among the rest the thinnest wall wins
				local found, bestRank = nil, math.huge
				for _, pt in ipairs(candidatePoints(part)) do
					local can, vis, frac, depth = reachInfo(part, pt)
					if can then
						-- estimated damage of a shot at this point: head multiplier, minus what walls take
						local base = dbg.activeDamage
						local est = nil
						if base then
							local hm = (part.Name == "Head") and math.max(1, dbg.activeHeadMult or 1) or 1
							est = base * hm
							if not vis then
								est = est * (1 - (silentWallLoss:Get() / 100) * math.clamp(frac or 0, 0, 1))
							end
						end
						local ok = true
						if est then
							-- wallbang: only lethal shots (unless "wallbang any" auto shoot is on)
							local wallAnyAuto = auto and saCfg and saCfg.wallAny and saCfg.wallAny:Get()
							if not vis and silentLethal:Get() and hp and est < hp and not wallAnyAuto then ok = false end
							-- auto shoot: minimum damage, unless the shot kills outright
							if autoMin and autoMin > 0 and est < autoMin and not (hp and est >= hp) then ok = false end
						end
						if ok then
							-- a point that kills outranks one that only chips: prefer lethal placements
							local notLethal = (hp and est and est < hp) and 500 or 0
							local rank = (vis and 0 or 1000) + notLethal + (frac or 0) * 10 + (depth or 0) * 0.1
															+ (pt - part.Position).Magnitude * 2
							if rank < bestRank then
								bestRank = rank
								found = { pos = pt, vis = vis, est = est }
							end
							if vis then break end -- a visible point can't be beaten
						end
					end
				end

				if not found then
					-- no reachable point (no line of sight and the wall can't be penetrated):
					-- instead of skipping the target entirely, gamble the shot at the candidate
					-- point closest to the crosshair -- a redirected shot still beats no redirect.
					-- auto shoot and strict (visible only) checks never fire on a guess
					if auto or strict then
						blocked += 1
						return nil
					end
					guess = true
					local gamblePos, gamblePixels = part.Position, math.huge
					for _, pt in ipairs(candidatePoints(part)) do
						local sp2, on2 = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pt)
						if on2 then
							local sv2 = adjustForStretchedRes(Vector2.new(sp2.X, sp2.Y))
							local px = (sv2 - viewportCenter).Magnitude
							if px < gamblePixels then
								gamblePixels, gamblePos = px, pt
							end
						end
					end
					aimPos, visible, aimEst = gamblePos, false, nil
				else
					-- use the point the search actually found (it used to be dropped, so every shot was aimed at the
					-- part's centre even when only another point was reachable)
					aimPos, visible, aimEst = found.pos, found.vis, found.est
				end
			end

			-- lead the aim point by the chosen prediction (the velocity is tracked every call so it is warm)
			do
				local v = targetVelocity(part.Parent)
				local lead = silentPredict:Get() / 1000
				if lead > 0 and v.Magnitude > 0.5 then aimPos = aimPos + v * lead end
			end

			local score
			if priority == "Closest distance" then
				score = studs
			elseif priority == "Lowest health" then
				local h = hp or 100
				score = h + studs / 10000
			elseif priority == "Killable first" then
				local dmgNow = dbg.activeDamage
				local hm = math.max(1, dbg.activeHeadMult or 1)
				local killable = hp and dmgNow and dmgNow * hm >= hp
				local aimScore = ignoreFov and math.acos(math.clamp(aimCF.LookVector:Dot(toPart.Unit), -1, 1)) * 1000 or pixels
				score = (killable and 0 or 5000) + aimScore
			elseif priority == "Looking at me" then
				local root = part.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or part
				local toMe = aimCF.Position - root.Position
				if toMe.Magnitude < 0.001 then
					score = studs
				else
					-- 0 = facing straight at me, 2 = facing directly away
					score = (1 - root.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toMe.Unit)) * 100 + studs / 10000
				end
			elseif ignoreFov then
				score = math.acos(math.clamp(aimCF.LookVector:Dot(toPart.Unit), -1, 1))
			else
				score = pixels
			end

			-- anything actually visible beats a wallbang
			if not visible then score += 1e6 end
			-- a confirmed wallbang beats a pure guess
			if guess then score += 1e6 end
			-- a placement that kills beats one that only damages
			if hp and aimEst and aimEst < hp then score += 2500 end
			return score, aimPos, aimEst
		end

		local function consider(character)
			if not character:IsA("Model") or mine[character] or character.Name == lp.Name then return end
			if silentIgnoreHostages:Get() and character:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then return end
			if isCharacterDead(character) then
				forgetValid(character)
				return
			end
			if silentIgnoreHighlight:Get() and hasActiveHighlight(character) then return end

			if silentTeamCheck:Get() then
				local mineTeam = myTeam()
				local theirs = armorTeam(character) or backupTeam(character, playerFromCharacter(character))
				if mineTeam and theirs and mineTeam == theirs then return end
			end

			if auto then
				if not (character:FindFirstChild("Head") and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					and (character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso"))) then
					forgetValid(character)
					return
				end
				if os.clock() - validSince(character) < ((saCfg and saCfg.fast and saCfg.fast:Get()) and 0.12 or SPAWN_GRACE) then return end
			else
				validSince(character) -- keep the timer warm even when not auto-firing
			end

			local hp = targetHealth(character)
			local head, body = silentParts(character, mode)
			local dmgNow = dbg.activeDamage

			-- Every reachable point on this target is gathered as a candidate (score + whether it is
			-- a CONFIRMED kill). The best candidate is then picked with "confirmed kill always beats a
			-- non-kill" as the top-level rule, and aim score only breaks ties inside the same tier.
			--
			-- The old code picked by score alone: a head shot that needed a wallbang but wasn't fully
			-- lethal could out-score a body shot that WAS a guaranteed kill, just by being closer to the
			-- crosshair. Auto shoot then discarded the whole target because that (non-lethal) head pick
			-- wasn't a confirmed kill -- even though a killable body shot was sitting right there. That
			-- is why auto shoot sometimes skipped killable enemies, or fired a shot that didn't finish
			-- them.
			local candidates = {}
			local function addCandidate(part, forceKillsure)
				if not part then return end
				local s, pos, est = evaluate(part, hp)
				if not s then return end
				local killsure
				if forceKillsure ~= nil then
					killsure = forceKillsure
				elseif hp == nil then
					killsure = true
				elseif est ~= nil then
					-- a shot through a wall must clear the health with headroom (armor / falloff are not modelled)
					killsure = est >= ((auto and s >= 1e6) and hp * 1.15 or hp)
				elseif part == head then
					killsure = (dmgNow and dmgNow * math.max(1, dbg.activeHeadMult or 1) >= hp) or false
				else
					killsure = (dmgNow and dmgNow >= hp) or false
				end
				candidates[#candidates + 1] = { part = part, score = s, pos = pos, killsure = killsure }
			end

			-- one body shot already kills them outright: the big, safe body hitboxes are offered
			-- as guaranteed kills up front -- but never when "respect target part" is on: the
			-- chosen part must win on its own, even against a guaranteed body kill
			if silentSmartKill:Get() and mode ~= "Body" and not respectPart
				and hp and dmgNow and dmgNow >= hp then
				local _, bodyOnly = silentParts(character, "Body")
				for _, part in ipairs(bodyOnly) do
					local s, pos, est = evaluate(part, hp)
					if s and (est == nil or est >= ((auto and s >= 1e6) and hp * 1.15 or hp)) then
						candidates[#candidates + 1] = { part = part, score = s, pos = pos, killsure = true }
					end
				end
			end

			if mode == "Head > Body" or mode == "Head > Any" then
				addCandidate(head)
				local headOk = false
				for _, c in ipairs(candidates) do
					if c.part == head and c.killsure then headOk = true end
				end
				-- only pay for the body traces when the head alone isn't already a confirmed kill
				-- (or couldn't be reached at all) -- keeps this from scanning every hitbox on every
				-- enemy every single frame
				if not headOk then
					for _, part in ipairs(body) do addCandidate(part) end
				end
			else
				addCandidate(head)
				for _, part in ipairs(body) do addCandidate(part) end
			end

			if #candidates == 0 then return end

			-- "any visible part" is a TRIGGER condition, not an aim override: auto shoot may
			-- fire as soon as any hitbox is exposed, but the head always wins whenever it's a
			-- reachable candidate -- a lethal torso never outranks it
			local anyPartNow = auto and saCfg and saCfg.anyPart and saCfg.anyPart:Get()
			table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
				if anyPartNow and (a.part == head) ~= (b.part == head) then
					return a.part == head
				end
				if a.killsure ~= b.killsure then return a.killsure end
				-- same tier: take the head whenever it is reachable (a body point that merely sits closer to the
				-- crosshair used to win, which is why auto shoot rarely went for the head)
				if mode ~= "Body" and (a.part == head) ~= (b.part == head) then
					return a.part == head
				end
				return a.score < b.score
			end)
			local pick = candidates[1]
			local chosen, chosenScore, chosenPos, chosenKillsure = pick.part, pick.score, pick.pos, pick.killsure

			-- auto shoot never fires a shot that isn't a confirmed kill once HP/damage are known,
			-- unless "wallbang any" is on, or "any visible part" picked a reachable head: a head
			-- shot is always worth taking even when it wouldn't kill outright
						-- A shot the bullet can travel to in a straight line (chosenScore < 1e6, meaning
			-- evaluate() found a clear line of sight) now always fires for auto shoot once it
			-- passed evaluate()'s own "Auto shoot min damage" filter -- it no longer has to be a
			-- guaranteed kill. That requirement used to apply to EVERY shot, including a fully
			-- visible target, which is why auto shoot sometimes refused to fire at someone standing
			-- right in front of you.
			--
			-- Only a WALLBANG pick (chosenScore >= 1e6: evaluate() could only place it through a
			-- wall) still needs extra permission, since firing blind through a wall is riskier:
			-- either the shot is a confirmed kill, or "Auto shoot: wallbang any" is ticked.
			local isWallbang = chosenScore >= 1e6
			if auto and chosen and isWallbang then
				local wallAnyOn = saCfg and saCfg.wallAny and saCfg.wallAny:Get()
				if not wallAnyOn then
					if dmgNow and hp and not chosenKillsure then return end
					if not (dmgNow and hp) then return end -- unknown stats: never guess through a wall
				end
			end

			if chosen and silentSticky:Get() and character == stickyChar
				and os.clock() - stickyAt < 0.75 then
				chosenScore = chosenScore * STICKY_BIAS
			end

			if chosen and chosenScore < bestScore then
				best, bestScore, bestPos = chosen, chosenScore, chosenPos
			end
		end

		for _, categoryFolder in ipairs(charactersFolder:GetChildren()) do
			if categoryFolder:IsA("Folder") then
				for _, character in ipairs(categoryFolder:GetChildren()) do consider(character) end
			elseif categoryFolder:IsA("Model") then
				consider(categoryFolder)
			end
		end

		if best then
			stickyChar, stickyAt = best.Parent, os.clock()
			dbg.silentTarget = best.Parent.Name .. ":" .. best.Name
			dbg.silentDist = math.floor((best.Position - origin).Magnitude)
			dbg.silentStatus = (ignoreFov and "Locked (Global)" or "Locked")
			.. (bestScore >= 2e6 and " (guess)" or bestScore >= 1e6 and " (wallbang)" or "")
		else
			dbg.silentTarget = "None"
			dbg.silentDist = 0
			dbg.silentStatus = blocked > 0 and "Blocked (no line of sight)" or "Searching..."
		end

		return best, bestPos
	end

	getClosestPlayerPartInFov = function(origin, cheap, strict, auto)
		if not silentOn:Get() then
			dbg.silentStatus = "Disabled"
			return nil
		end

		local saved = camera.CFrame
		local swapped = false
		if firstPersonCF and thirdPersonCF and saved == thirdPersonCF then
			camera.CFrame = firstPersonCF
			swapped = true
		end

		local ok, result, aimPos = pcall(pickSilentTarget, origin, cheap, strict, auto)

		if swapped then camera.CFrame = saved end

		if not ok then
			warn("[SilentAim Error]: " .. tostring(result))
			return nil
		end
		return result, aimPos
	end

	local bulletModulePath = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Components"):WaitForChild("Weapon"):WaitForChild("Classes"):WaitForChild("Bullet", 5)
	if bulletModulePath then
		local BulletModule = require(bulletModulePath)
		local oldPerformRaycast = env.__gsOrigRaycast or BulletModule._performRaycast
		env.__gsOrigRaycast = oldPerformRaycast

		-- remember the equipped weapon's real penetration, range and damage (from the game's Bullet class)
		local function noteWeapon(b)
			if type(b) == "table" and type(b.Properties) == "table" then
				local P = b.Properties
				dbg.activePen = tonumber(P.Penetration) or 0
				dbg.activeRange = tonumber(P.Range) or 500
				dbg.activeDamage = type(P.Damage) == "number" and P.Damage or nil
				local hm = P.HeadMultiplier or P.HeadshotMultiplier or P.HeadDamageMultiplier
				if type(hm) ~= "number" and type(P.Multipliers) == "table" then hm = P.Multipliers.Head end
				dbg.activeHeadMult = type(hm) == "number" and hm or nil
			end
		end
		local oldSetActive = env.__gsOrigSetActive or BulletModule.setActive
		env.__gsOrigSetActive = oldSetActive
		if type(oldSetActive) == "function" then
			BulletModule.setActive = function(self, a2, ...)
				if a2 then pcall(noteWeapon, self) end
				return oldSetActive(self, a2, ...)
			end
		end

		BulletModule._performRaycast = function(self, spreadFactor)
			pcall(noteWeapon, self)
			dbg.shotAt = os.clock()
			if dbg.spreadMod then spreadFactor = dbg.spreadMod(spreadFactor) end

			-- third person: the shot must start from the real first-person eye, not from the
			-- pulled-back camera, otherwise it can start behind walls / inside your own body
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local savedCF, swapped = cam and cam.CFrame, false
			if cam and firstPersonCF and thirdPersonCF and savedCF == thirdPersonCF then
				cam.CFrame = firstPersonCF
				swapped = true
			end
			local okRay, rayData = pcall(oldPerformRaycast, self, spreadFactor)
			if swapped then cam.CFrame = savedCF end
			if not okRay then error(rayData, 0) end
			dbg.lastShot = { t = os.clock(), origin = rayData.Origin, dir = rayData.Direction }

			local redirected = nil

			if silentOn:Get() and math.random(1, 100) <= silentHitChance:Get() then
				local success, err = pcall(function()
					local origin = rayData.Origin
					if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return end

					local autoWin = os.clock() - (dbg.autoAt or 0) < 0.35
					local saCfg = dbg.silentAuto
					local targetPartObj, aimPos = getClosestPlayerPartInFov(
						origin, false,
						autoWin and saCfg and saCfg.visibleOnly and saCfg.visibleOnly:Get() or false,
						autoWin
					)

					-- the shot-time pick can fail (state changed in the last frames): keep the shot on the
					-- target auto shoot validated instead of letting it fly at the crosshair
					if not targetPartObj and autoWin and dbg.autoPick then
						local ap = dbg.autoPick
						if os.clock() - ap.t < 0.25 and ap.part and ap.part.Parent then
							targetPartObj, aimPos = ap.part, ap.pos
						end
					end

					if targetPartObj then
						local finalTargetPos = aimPos or targetPartObj.Position
						-- Re-run the game's own raycast aimed at the point. A fabricated hit list (one "Flesh" hit,
						-- no walls) is what the game never produces for a wallbang: the real list has the wall
						-- entry/exit hits before the target hit, and the server uses it for the penetration damage /
						-- validation. So let the game build it (Shared.Raycast through its own Bullet code).
						local recast
						do
							local cam2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
							if cam2 then
								local savedCF2 = cam2.CFrame
								cam2.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(origin, finalTargetPos)
								local okR, r2 = pcall(oldPerformRaycast, self, 0)
								cam2.CFrame = savedCF2
								if okR and type(r2) == "table" and type(r2.Hits) == "table" then
									for _, h in ipairs(r2.Hits) do
										local inst = type(h) == "table" and h.Instance
										if typeof(inst) == "Instance" and inst:IsDescendantOf(targetPartObj.Parent) then
											recast = r2
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if recast then
							rayData = recast
							dbg.shotMode = "game cast (" .. tostring(#recast.Hits) .. " hits)"
							if dbg.lastShot then dbg.lastShot.dir = recast.Direction end
						else
							-- the game's own cast did not reach the target (our trace disagreed): fall back
							dbg.shotMode = "fabricated hit (game cast missed)"
							local adjustedDirection = (finalTargetPos - origin).Unit

							rayData.Direction = adjustedDirection
							if dbg.lastShot then dbg.lastShot.dir = adjustedDirection end
							rayData.Distance = (finalTargetPos - origin).Magnitude

							rayData.Hits = {
								{
									Position = finalTargetPos,
									Instance = targetPartObj,
									Material = "Flesh",
									Normal = (origin - finalTargetPos).Unit,
									Exit = false,
								}
							}
						end

						redirected = targetPartObj
					end
				end)

				if not success then
					warn("[SilentAim Error]: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			end

			-- Second hit source for the hit/kill sounds: read whatever this bullet
			-- actually hit (redirected or not) straight from rayData.Hits, so the
			-- sounds don't depend solely on PlayerHitPrediction.Record firing.
			pcall(function()
				if type(rayData) == "table" and type(rayData.Hits) == "table" then
					for _, h in ipairs(rayData.Hits) do
						local inst = type(h) == "table" and h.Instance
						if typeof(inst) == "Instance" then
							local m = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
							while m do
								local plr = playerFromCharacter(m)
								if plr and plr ~= lp then
									registerOwnHit(plr.UserId, inst.Name)
									break
								elseif not plr and m:GetAttribute("Bot") == true then
									registerOwnHit(m, inst.Name) -- bots are keyed by their model
									break
								end
								m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			pcall(spawnBulletTracer, rayData, redirected)
			if dbg.bulletImpact then pcall(dbg.bulletImpact, rayData, redirected) end

			return rayData
		end
	end
end

-- Weapon mods
do
	local modSec = MiscTab:AddSection("Weapon mods", "Right")
	local recoilOn = modSec:AddToggle("No recoil", false)
	local recoilPct = modSec:AddSlider("Recoil amount", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)
	recoilPct:DependsOn(recoilOn, function(v) return v end)
	local spreadOn = modSec:AddToggle("No spread", false)
	local spreadPct = modSec:AddSlider("Spread amount", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)
	spreadPct:DependsOn(spreadOn, function(v) return v end)
	local instantOn = modSec:AddToggle("Instant reload", false)

	env.__gsOrig = env.__gsOrig or {}
	local orig = env.__gsOrig

	dbg.spreadMod = function(sf)
		if type(sf) == "number" and spreadOn:Get() then return sf * spreadPct:Get() / 100 end
		return sf
	end

	local CamCtl
	pcall(function() CamCtl = require(ReplicatedStorage.Controllers.CameraController) end)
	if CamCtl then
		pcall(function() if setreadonly then setreadonly(CamCtl, false) end end)
		if type(CamCtl.setWeaponRecoil) == "function" then
			orig.setWeaponRecoil = orig.setWeaponRecoil or CamCtl.setWeaponRecoil
			local base = orig.setWeaponRecoil
			CamCtl.setWeaponRecoil = function(t, ...)
				if recoilOn:Get() and type(t) == "table" and typeof(t.Value) == "Vector3" then
					t = { Value = t.Value * (recoilPct:Get() / 100), Damper = t.Damper, Speed = t.Speed }
				end
				return base(t, ...)
			end
		end
		if type(CamCtl.weaponKick) == "function" then
			orig.weaponKick = orig.weaponKick or CamCtl.weaponKick
			local base = orig.weaponKick
			CamCtl.weaponKick = function(k, ...)
				if recoilOn:Get() then
					local pct = recoilPct:Get() / 100
					if pct <= 0 then return end
					local tk = typeof(k)
					if tk == "number" or tk == "Vector3" or tk == "Vector2" then k = k * pct end
				end
				return base(k, ...)
			end
		end
	else
		Window:Notify("Recoil: CameraController not found", 5)
	end

	local RELOAD_WORDS = { "reload" }
	local RELOAD_SPEED = 1000

	local function looksLikeReload(name)
		if type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
		local n = string.lower(name)
		for _, w in ipairs(RELOAD_WORDS) do
			if string.find(n, w, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function speedUpTrack(track)
		if not instantOn:Get() then return end
		if typeof(track) ~= "Instance" then return end
		pcall(function()
			if track.AdjustSpeed then track:AdjustSpeed(RELOAD_SPEED) end
		end)
	end

	local AnimMod, origPlay
	do
		local ok, mod = pcall(function()
			return require(ReplicatedStorage.Classes.WeaponComponent.Classes.Viewmodel.Classes.Animation)
		end)
		if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.play) == "function" then
			AnimMod = mod
			origPlay = mod.play
			pcall(function()
				if setreadonly then setreadonly(mod, false) end
				mod.play = function(self_anim, animName, ...)
					local track = origPlay(self_anim, animName, ...)
					if track and looksLikeReload(animName) then
						pcall(speedUpTrack, track)
					end
					return track
				end
			end)
		else
			Window:Notify("Instant reload: Animation class not found", 5)
		end
	end

	env.__gsWeaponRestore = function()
		pcall(function() if CamCtl and orig.setWeaponRecoil then CamCtl.setWeaponRecoil = orig.setWeaponRecoil end end)
		pcall(function() if CamCtl and orig.weaponKick then CamCtl.weaponKick = orig.weaponKick end end)
		pcall(function()
			local B = require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Weapon.Classes.Bullet)
			if env.__gsOrigRaycast then B._performRaycast = env.__gsOrigRaycast end
			if env.__gsOrigSetActive then B.setActive = env.__gsOrigSetActive end
		end)
		pcall(function() if AnimMod and origPlay then AnimMod.play = origPlay end end)
		dbg.spreadMod = nil
		dbg.silentAuto = nil
	end
end

-- Self chams
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Self chams", "Left")
	local selfOn = sec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local selfCol = selfOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
	local selfFill = sec:AddSlider("Fill", 0, 100, 100, nil, "%", 5)
	selfFill:DependsOn(selfOn, function(v) return v end)

	local selfFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	selfFolder.Name = "GSSelfChams"
	selfFolder.Parent = workspace

	local hls = {} -- [model] = Highlight

	local function myModels()
		local list = {}
		if lp.Character then list[#list + 1] = lp.Character end
		local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
		if mine and mine ~= lp.Character then list[#list + 1] = mine end
		return list
	end

	local function clearAll()
		for m, hl in pairs(hls) do
			pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
			hls[m] = nil
		end
	end

	local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not selfOn:Get() then
			if next(hls) then clearAll() end
			return
		end
		if selfFolder.Parent ~= workspace then selfFolder.Parent = workspace end

		local want = {}
		local col, fillT = selfCol:Get(), 1 - selfFill:Get() / 100
		for _, m in ipairs(myModels()) do
			want[m] = true
			local hl = hls[m]
			if not hl or not hl.Parent then
				if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
				hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
				hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
				hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
				hl.Parent = selfFolder
				hls[m] = hl
			end
			if hl.Adornee ~= m then hl.Adornee = m end
			hl.FillColor = col
			hl.FillTransparency = fillT
			hl.Enabled = true
		end
		for m, hl in pairs(hls) do
			if not want[m] then
				pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
				hls[m] = nil
			end
		end
	end)

	env.__gsSelfChamsCleanup = function()
		pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(clearAll)
		pcall(function() selfFolder:Destroy() end)
	end
end

--enemy esp
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Extra Sensory Perception", "Right")

	local espBox = sec:AddToggle("Box", false)
	local espBoxCol = espBox:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
	local espBoxType = sec:AddDropdown("Box type", { "Full box", "Corner box", "Filled box" }, "Full box")
	espBoxType:DependsOn(espBox, function(v) return v end)

	local espName = sec:AddToggle("Name", false)
	local espNameCol = espName:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local espDistOn = sec:AddToggle("Distance", false)
	local espDistCol = espDistOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local espHp = sec:AddToggle("Health bar", false)
	local espHpCol = espHp:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 80), false)
	local espHpGrad = sec:AddToggle("Health bar: color by health", false)
	local espHpText = sec:AddToggle("Health number", false)
	local espHpTextCol = espHpText:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local espSkel = sec:AddToggle("Skeleton", false)
	local espSkelCol = espSkel:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local espWep = sec:AddToggle("Weapon", false)
	local espWepCol = espWep:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local espChams = sec:AddToggle("Chams", false)
	local espFillCol = espChams:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
	local espFill = sec:AddSlider("Chams fill", 0, 100, 100, nil, "%", 5)
	espFill:DependsOn(espChams, function(v) return v end)


	local espTeam = sec:AddToggle("Team check", false)
	local espDist = sec:AddSlider("Max distance", 50, 5000, 3000, nil, " studs", 50)


	local espFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	espFolder.Name = "GSESP"
	espFolder.Parent = workspace

	local pool = {}
	local BLACK = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	local LOW = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
	local FOOT_NAMES = { "LeftFoot", "RightFoot", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }

	-- joints to connect for the skeleton (R15 names first, then R6 names): a bone is only
	-- drawn when both of its parts exist on the model
	local SKEL = {
		{ "Head", "UpperTorso" }, { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
		{ "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" }, { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" }, { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
		{ "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" }, { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" }, { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
		{ "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" }, { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" }, { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
		{ "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" }, { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" }, { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
		{ "Head", "Torso" }, { "Torso", "Left Arm" }, { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
		{ "Torso", "Left Leg" }, { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
	}

	local function setZ(obj, z) pcall(function() obj.ZIndex = z end) end

	local function mkSquare(filled, thick, col, z)
		local s = Drawing.new("Square")
		s.Filled = filled
		s.Thickness = thick
		s.Color = col
		s.Transparency = 1
		s.Visible = false
		setZ(s, z)
		return s
	end

	local function mkLine(thick, col, z)
		local l = Drawing.new("Line")
		l.Thickness = thick
		l.Color = col
		l.Transparency = 1
		l.Visible = false
		setZ(l, z)
		return l
	end

	local function mkText()
		local t = Drawing.new("Text")
		t.Size = 13
		t.Center = true
		t.Outline = true
		t.Transparency = 1
		t.Visible = false
		pcall(function() t.Font = 2 end)
		setZ(t, 3)
		return t
	end

	-- lines are created lazily (corner boxes / skeleton only cost anything when used)
	local function ensure(list, n, thick, col, z)
		while #list < n do list[#list + 1] = mkLine(thick, col, z) end
	end

	local function newEntry()
		local e = {}
		e.fill = mkSquare(true, 1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0)
		e.boxOut = mkSquare(false, 3, BLACK, 1)
		e.box = mkSquare(false, 1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 2)
		e.hpBg = mkSquare(true, 1, BLACK, 1)
		e.hpFill = mkSquare(true, 1, Color3.new(0, 1, 0), 2)
		e.name = mkText()
		e.dist = mkText()
		e.hpText = mkText()
		e.wep = mkText()
		e.cOut, e.c, e.skel = {}, {}, {}
		local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
		hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		hl.Enabled = false
		hl:SetAttribute("GSChams", true) -- marks this as our own ESP highlight (see Silent Aim's "Ignore highlighted")
		hl.Parent = espFolder
		e.hl = hl
		return e
	end

	local function hideList(list)
		for i = 1, #list do list[i].Visible = false end
	end

	local function hideDrawings(e)
		e.fill.Visible = false
		e.boxOut.Visible = false
		e.box.Visible = false
		e.hpBg.Visible = false
		e.hpFill.Visible = false
		e.name.Visible = false
		e.dist.Visible = false
		e.hpText.Visible = false
		e.wep.Visible = false
		hideList(e.cOut)
		hideList(e.c)
		hideList(e.skel)
	end

	local function removeList(list)
		for i = 1, #list do pcall(function() list[i]:Remove() end) end
	end

	local function dropEntry(key)
		local e = pool[key]
		if not e then return end
		pool[key] = nil
		for _, n in ipairs({ "fill", "boxOut", "box", "hpBg", "hpFill", "name", "dist", "hpText", "wep" }) do
			pcall(function() e[n]:Remove() end)
		end
		removeList(e.cOut)
		removeList(e.c)
		removeList(e.skel)
		pcall(function() e.hl:Destroy() end)
	end

	local function dropAll()
		for key in pairs(pool) do dropEntry(key) end
	end

	local partCount = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function partsOf(m)
		local c = partCount[m]
		if c and os.clock() - c.at < 0.5 then return c.n end
		local n = 0
		for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then n += 1 end
		end
		partCount[m] = { n = n, at = os.clock() }
		return n
	end

	-- the player's current ALIVE body (Player.Character and the workspace.Characters model
	-- can both exist; only living ones count, so a dead leftover can never hide a respawn)
	local function bestModel(plr)
		local cands = { plr.Character }
		local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local fm = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)
		if fm and fm:IsA("Model") then cands[#cands + 1] = fm end

		local best, bestN
		for _, m in ipairs(cands) do
			if m and m:IsA("Model") and m.Parent and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				and not isCharacterDead(m) then
				local n = partsOf(m)
				if not bestN or n > bestN then best, bestN = m, n end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	local function collect()
		local list = {}
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= lp then
				local m = bestModel(plr)
				if m and not m:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then
					list[#list + 1] = { key = plr, model = m, plr = plr }
				end
			end
		end

		local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		if folder then
			local function offerBot(m)
				if not m:IsA("Model") or m.Name == lp.Name then return end
				if playerFromCharacter(m) then return end -- players are handled above
				if m:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then return end
				if not m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or isCharacterDead(m) then return end
				list[#list + 1] = { key = m, model = m }
			end
			for _, c in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Folder") then
					for _, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do offerBot(m) end
				else
					offerBot(c)
				end
			end
		end
		return list
	end

	-- cached per body so the per-frame ESP loop doesn't rescan the whole model
	local wepCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local HttpSvc2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
	-- container / placeholder names that are never a real weapon name
	local GENERIC_WEP = {
		Weapon = true, Equipped = true, Gun = true, Model = true, Tool = true,
		Viewmodel = true, ViewModel = true, Handle = true, WeaponModel = true,
	}
	local WEP_NAME_KEYS = { "Name", "WeaponName", "DisplayName", "Weapon", "ItemName" }

	-- pull a real weapon name out of the CurrentEquipped attribute (JSON or plain text)
	local function nameFromAttr(a)
		if typeof(a) ~= "string" or #a == 0 then return nil end
		if string.find(a, "{", 1, true) then
			-- proper JSON decode: the name can sit at the top level or inside a nested table
			local ok, d = pcall(HttpSvc2.JSONDecode, HttpSvc2, a)
			if ok and type(d) == "table" then
				local function dig(t, depth)
					if depth > 3 then return nil end
					for _, k in ipairs(WEP_NAME_KEYS) do
						local v = t[k]
						if typeof(v) == "string" and #v > 0 and not GENERIC_WEP[v] then return v end
					end
					for _, v in pairs(t) do
						if type(v) == "table" then
							local r = dig(v, depth + 1)
							if r then return r end
						end
					end
					return nil
				end
				local n = dig(d, 0)
				if n then return n end
			end
			-- not valid JSON: fall back to raw field matching
			for _, k in ipairs(WEP_NAME_KEYS) do
				local v = string.match(a, "\"" .. k .. "\"%s*:%s*\"([^\"]+)\"")
				if v and not GENERIC_WEP[v] then return v end
			end
			return nil
		end
		return a -- plain text, not JSON
	end

	local function weaponNameOfBody(m, plr)
		local now = os.clock()
		local rec = wepCache[m]
		if rec and now - rec.at < 0.25 then return rec.n end

		local n
		-- 1) the CurrentEquipped attribute carries the real weapon name (JSON or plain text)
		n = nameFromAttr((plr and plr:GetAttribute("CurrentEquipped")) or m:GetAttribute("CurrentEquipped"))

		-- 2) the game builds WeaponModel > Equipped on every character; its child is named
		--    generically ("Weapon"), so only accept a non-generic name: attributes first,
		--    then deeper Model / Tool children
		if not n then
			local wm = m:FindFirstChild("WeaponModel")
			local eq = wm and wm:FindFirstChild("Equipped")
			if eq then
				for _, k in ipairs(WEP_NAME_KEYS) do
					local v = eq:GetAttribute(k)
					if typeof(v) == "string" and #v > 0 and not GENERIC_WEP[v] then
						n = v
						break
					end
				end
				if not n then
					for _, d in ipairs(eq:GetDescendants()) do
						if (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("Tool")) and not GENERIC_WEP[d.Name] then
							n = d.Name
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- (the attribute and the weapon model are handled above)

		-- 3) a Tool anywhere in the body (equipped tools can be nested inside a hand)
		if not n then
			local tool = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
			if not tool then
				for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("Tool") then
						tool = d
						break
					end
				end
			end
			if tool and not GENERIC_WEP[tool.Name] then n = tool.Name end
		end

		-- 4) other attribute spellings on the player / body
		if not n then
			for _, k in ipairs({ "EquippedWeapon", "CurrentWeapon", "Weapon", "Equipped", "Held", "ActiveWeapon" }) do
				local r = nameFromAttr((plr and plr:GetAttribute(k)) or m:GetAttribute(k))
				if r and not GENERIC_WEP[r] then n = r break end
			end
		end

		-- 5) last resort: first meaningfully named object inside the weapon model
		if not n then
			local wm = m:FindFirstChild("WeaponModel")
			if wm then
				for _, d in ipairs(wm:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("MeshPart") or d:IsA("Folder")) and not GENERIC_WEP[d.Name] and #d.Name > 1 then
						n = d.Name
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end

		wepCache[m] = { n = n, at = now }
		return n
	end

	local HEALTH_ATTRS = { "Health", "HP", "health" }
	local function healthOf(m, plr)
		local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then return hum.Health, math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1) end
		local mx = tonumber(m:GetAttribute("MaxHealth")) or 100
		for _, n in ipairs(HEALTH_ATTRS) do
			local v = m:GetAttribute(n)
			if type(v) == "number" then return v, mx end
		end
		if plr then
			for _, n in ipairs(HEALTH_ATTRS) do
				local v = plr:GetAttribute(n)
				if type(v) == "number" then return v, mx end
			end
		end
		return nil, mx
	end

	local function draw(item, cam, camPos, mineTeam, maxD, seen)
		local m, plr = item.model, item.plr

		if mineTeam then
			local theirs = armorTeam(m) or backupTeam(m, plr)
			if theirs and theirs == mineTeam then return end
		end

		local root = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return end
		local p = root.Position
		local dist = (p - camPos).Magnitude
		if dist > maxD then return end

		seen[item.key] = true
		local e = pool[item.key]
		if not e then
			e = newEntry()
			pool[item.key] = e
		end

		-- chams are handled by their own loop below; this highlight stays off
		local hl = e.hl
		if hl.Parent ~= espFolder then hl.Parent = espFolder end
		hl.Enabled = false

		hideDrawings(e)

		-- box geometry: head top to feet, centred on the root
		local head = m:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local topY = (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) and (head.Position.Y + head.Size.Y / 2 + 0.15) or (p.Y + 2.6)
		local botY = p.Y - 3
		for _, n in ipairs(FOOT_NAMES) do
			local f = m:FindFirstChild(n)
			if f and f:IsA("BasePart") then botY = math.min(botY, f.Position.Y - f.Size.Y / 2) end
		end
		local top = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(Vector3.new(p.X, topY, p.Z))
		local bot = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(Vector3.new(p.X, botY, p.Z))
		if top.Z <= 0 or bot.Z <= 0 then return end

		local h = math.floor(math.abs(bot.Y - top.Y) + 0.5)
		if h < 4 then return end
		local w = math.floor(h * 0.5 + 0.5)
		local x = math.floor(top.X - w / 2 + 0.5)
		local y = math.floor(math.min(top.Y, bot.Y) + 0.5)

		-- box
		if espBox:Get() then
			local bt = espBoxType:Get()
			local col = espBoxCol:Get()

			if bt == "Corner box" then
				ensure(e.cOut, 8, 3, BLACK, 1)
				ensure(e.c, 8, 1, col, 2)
				local cw = math.max(4, math.floor(w * 0.3))
				local ch = math.max(4, math.floor(h * 0.2))
				local segs = {
					{ x, y, x + cw, y },             { x, y, x, y + ch },
					{ x + w, y, x + w - cw, y },     { x + w, y, x + w, y + ch },
					{ x, y + h, x + cw, y + h },     { x, y + h, x, y + h - ch },
					{ x + w, y + h, x + w - cw, y + h }, { x + w, y + h, x + w, y + h - ch },
				}
				for i, s in ipairs(segs) do
					local a, b = Vector2.new(s[1], s[2]), Vector2.new(s[3], s[4])
					local o, c = e.cOut[i], e.c[i]
					o.From, o.To, o.Visible = a, b, true
					c.From, c.To, c.Color, c.Visible = a, b, col, true
				end
			else
				if bt == "Filled box" then
					e.fill.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
					e.fill.Size = Vector2.new(w, h)
					e.fill.Color = col
					e.fill.Transparency = 0.25
					e.fill.Visible = true
				end
				e.boxOut.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
				e.boxOut.Size = Vector2.new(w, h)
				e.boxOut.Visible = true
				e.box.Position = Vector2.new(x, y)
				e.box.Size = Vector2.new(w, h)
				e.box.Color = col
				e.box.Visible = true
			end
		end

		-- skeleton
		if espSkel:Get() then
			local col = espSkelCol:Get()
			local n = 0
			for _, pr in ipairs(SKEL) do
				local a, b = m:FindFirstChild(pr[1]), m:FindFirstChild(pr[2])
				if a and b and a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") then
					local pa = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(a.Position)
					local pb = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(b.Position)
					if pa.Z > 0 and pb.Z > 0 then
						n += 1
						ensure(e.skel, n, 1, col, 2)
						local l = e.skel[n]
						l.From = Vector2.new(pa.X, pa.Y)
						l.To = Vector2.new(pb.X, pb.Y)
						l.Color = col
						l.Visible = true
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- name (top)
		if espName:Get() then
			e.name.Text = plr and plr.Name or m.Name
			e.name.Position = Vector2.new(x + w / 2, y - 15)
			e.name.Color = espNameCol:Get()
			e.name.Visible = true
		end

		-- distance (bottom)
		if espDistOn:Get() then
			e.dist.Text = tostring(math.floor(dist)) .. "m"
			e.dist.Position = Vector2.new(x + w / 2, y + h + 2)
			e.dist.Color = espDistCol:Get()
			e.dist.Visible = true
		end

		-- weapon
		if espWep:Get() then
			local wn = weaponNameOfBody(m, plr)
			if wn then
				e.wep.Text = wn
				e.wep.Position = Vector2.new(x + w / 2, y + h + (espDistOn:Get() and 15 or 2))
				e.wep.Color = espWepCol:Get()
				e.wep.Visible = true
			end
		end

		-- health bar (left) + number
		local hp, mx = healthOf(m, plr)
		if hp and (espHp:Get() or espHpText:Get()) then
			local pct = math.clamp(hp / mx, 0, 1)
			local bx = x - 6
			local fh = math.max(1, math.floor(h * pct + 0.5))

			if espHp:Get() then
				e.hpBg.Position = Vector2.new(bx, y - 1)
				e.hpBg.Size = Vector2.new(4, h + 2)
				e.hpFill.Position = Vector2.new(bx + 1, y + h - fh)
				e.hpFill.Size = Vector2.new(2, fh)
				local base = espHpCol:Get()
				e.hpFill.Color = espHpGrad:Get() and LOW:Lerp(base, pct) or base
				e.hpBg.Visible = true
				e.hpFill.Visible = true
			end

			if espHpText:Get() then
				e.hpText.Text = tostring(math.floor(hp + 0.5))
				e.hpText.Position = Vector2.new(bx - 9, math.clamp(y + h - fh - 6, y - 6, y + h - 6))
				e.hpText.Color = espHpTextCol:Get()
				e.hpText.Visible = true
			end
		end
	end

	local function anyOn()
		return espBox:Get() or espName:Get() or espDistOn:Get() or espHp:Get()
			or espHpText:Get() or espSkel:Get() or espChams:Get() or espWep:Get()
	end

	local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not anyOn() then
			if next(pool) then dropAll() end
			return
		end
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return end
		if espFolder.Parent ~= workspace then espFolder.Parent = workspace end

		local mineTeam = espTeam:Get() and myTeam() or nil
		local maxD = espDist:Get()
		local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
		local seen = {}

		for _, item in ipairs(collect()) do
			pcall(draw, item, cam, camPos, mineTeam, maxD, seen)
		end

		for key, e in pairs(pool) do
			if not seen[key] then
				hideDrawings(e)
				e.hl.Enabled = false
				-- forget entries of players who left / bots that were removed
				local gone
				if typeof(key) == "Instance" and key:IsA("Player") then
					gone = key.Parent == nil
				else
					gone = typeof(key) == "Instance" and not key:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
				end
				if gone then dropEntry(key) end
			end
		end
	end)

	-- ───── CHAMS: own loop, independent of box / name / health drawing ─────
	-- Why chams used to drop out:
	--  * Roblox draws at most 31 Highlights in total and the game keeps some of its own, so ours
	--    silently stopped rendering. Game highlights are now counted, and anything past the budget gets
	--    through-wall boxes instead (those have no limit).
	--  * bodies were only found by scanning; every model that appears under workspace.Characters is now
	--    registered the moment it is added (ChildAdded), at any folder depth.
	--  * a body whose Health attribute was still 0 while spawning was treated as dead.
	local chamsFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	chamsFolder.Name = "GSChams"
	chamsFolder.Parent = workspace

	local MAX_HL = 24
	local hlPool = {}      -- [key] = Highlight
	local adPool = {}      -- [key] = { model, parts = { [part] = adornment }, at }
	local CH_SKIP = { HumanoidRootPart = true, CameraPart = true }
	local hlWatch = {}     -- [key] = { conn, dirty, at }

	-- count the Highlights the game itself has alive
	local gameHls, gameHlCount = {}, 0
	local function trackHl(d)
		if d:IsA("Highlight") and not gameHls[d] and not d:GetAttribute("GSChams") then
			local p = d.Parent
			if not (p and string.sub(p.Name, 1, 2) == "GS") then
				gameHls[d] = true
				gameHlCount += 1
			end
		end
	end
	local hlAddConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) pcall(trackHl, d) end)
	local hlRemConn = workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d)
		if gameHls[d] then
			gameHls[d] = nil
			gameHlCount -= 1
		end
	end)
	task.defer(function()
		for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do pcall(trackHl, d) end
	end)

	local function killHl(key)
		local hl = hlPool[key]
		if hl then
			hlPool[key] = nil
			pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
		end
		local w = hlWatch[key]
		if w then
			hlWatch[key] = nil
			pcall(function() w.conn:Disconnect() end)
		end
	end

	local function killAd(key)
		local data = adPool[key]
		if data then
			adPool[key] = nil
			for _, a in pairs(data.parts) do pcall(function() a:Destroy() end) end
		end
	end

	local function ensureHighlight(key, model, col, fillT)
		local hl = hlPool[key]
		local w = hlWatch[key]
		if not w then
			w = { dirty = false, at = os.clock() }
			w.conn = model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function() w.dirty = true end)
			hlWatch[key] = w
		end
		local stale = w.dirty and os.clock() - w.at > 0.1
		if stale or os.clock() - w.at > 1.5 then
			-- re-bind in place so parts that streamed in later are picked up
			w.dirty = false
			w.at = os.clock()
			if hl and hl.Parent then pcall(function() hl.Adornee = nil end) end
		end
		if stale or not hl then
			-- hide the game's invisible oversized hitbox slabs so the Highlight doesn't paint them
			pcall(function()
				for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then
						local z = d.Size
						if z.X > 6 or z.Y > 6 or z.Z > 6 then d.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		if not hl or not hl.Parent then
			if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
			hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
			hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
			hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
			hl:SetAttribute("GSChams", true)
			hlPool[key] = hl
		end
		if hl.Parent ~= chamsFolder then hl.Parent = chamsFolder end
		if hl.Adornee ~= model then hl.Adornee = model end
		hl.FillColor = col
		hl.FillTransparency = fillT
		hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
		hl.Enabled = true
	end

	-- fallback for targets beyond the Highlight budget: a through-wall box on every part
	local function ensureAdorn(key, model, col, fillT)
		local data = adPool[key]
		if not data or data.model ~= model then
			killAd(key)
			data = { model = model, parts = {}, at = 0 }
			adPool[key] = data
		end

		if os.clock() - data.at > 0.5 then
			data.at = os.clock()
			local seenParts = {}
			for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") and not CH_SKIP[d.Name] and d.Transparency < 0.99 and d.Size.Magnitude > 0 then
					seenParts[d] = true
					if not data.parts[d] then
						local a = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
						a.AlwaysOnTop = true
						a.ZIndex = 5
						a.Adornee = d
						a.Parent = chamsFolder
						data.parts[d] = a
					end
				end
			end
			for p, a in pairs(data.parts) do
				if not seenParts[p] or not p.Parent then
					pcall(function() a:Destroy() end)
					data.parts[p] = nil
				end
			end
		end

		for p, a in pairs(data.parts) do
			if a.Parent ~= chamsFolder then a.Parent = chamsFolder end
			a.Size = p.Size
			a.Color3 = col
			a.Transparency = fillT
		end
	end

	local function clearChams()
		for k in pairs(hlPool) do killHl(k) end
		for k in pairs(adPool) do killAd(k) end
	end

	-- registry of every model that appears under workspace.Characters (any folder depth)
	local tracked, charConns = {}, {}
	local function track(m)
		if m:IsA("Model") and m.Name ~= lp.Name then tracked[m] = true end
	end
	local function watchFolder(f)
		if charConns[f] then return end
		local c = {}
		charConns[f] = c
		for _, ch in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("Folder") then watchFolder(ch) else track(ch) end
		end
		c[1] = f.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
			if ch:IsA("Folder") then watchFolder(ch) else track(ch) end
		end)
		c[2] = f.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(ch) tracked[ch] = nil end)
	end
	local function attachCharacters()
		local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		if f then watchFolder(f) end
	end
	attachCharacters()
	local wsConn = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
		if c.Name == "Characters" then attachCharacters() end
	end)

	-- ignore floating / ragdolled characters
	local FLOAT_SPEED = 35
	local RAGDOLL_ATTRS = {
		"Ragdoll", "Ragdolled", "IsRagdoll", "IsRagdolled", "Ragdolling", "IsFlying", "Flying",
		"Knocked", "KnockedOut", "Knockout", "Stunned", "Unconscious", "Downed", "IsDown", "Died",
	}
	local motion = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- [model] = { pos, t, speed }
	local function measuredSpeed(m)
		local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
		if not ok then return 0 end
		local now = os.clock()
		local rec = motion[m]
		if not rec then
			motion[m] = { pos = pivot, t = now, speed = 0 }
			return 0
		end
		local dt = now - rec.t
		if dt >= 0.1 then
			rec.speed = (pivot - rec.pos).Magnitude / dt
			rec.pos, rec.t = pivot, now
		end
		return rec.speed
	end
	-- structural ragdoll signs, cached so the per-frame loop doesn't rescan the model
	local ragScan = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- [model] = { at, rag }
	local function looksRagdolled(m)
		local now = os.clock()
		local rec = ragScan[m]
		if rec and now - rec.at < 0.5 then return rec.rag end
		local rag = false
		-- no Humanoid and no HumanoidRootPart: a ragdoll corpse the game stripped down
		if not m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and not m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			rag = true
		else
			for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
				-- ragdoll systems disable the stock Motor6D joints...
				if d:IsA("Motor6D") and not d.Enabled then
					rag = true
					break
					-- ...and add constraints / folders tagged with the ragdoll's name
				elseif string.find(string.lower(d.Name), "ragdoll", 1, true) then
					rag = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
		ragScan[m] = { at = now, rag = rag }
		return rag
	end

	local function isFloating(m, plr)
		for _, n in ipairs(RAGDOLL_ATTRS) do
			if m:GetAttribute(n) == true or (plr and plr:GetAttribute(n) == true) then return true end
		end
		-- the game's own ragdoll state, if it uses the stock Humanoid Ragdoll state
		local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then
			local ok, state = pcall(function() return hum:GetState() end)
			if ok and (state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
				or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
				or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
				or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown) then
				return true
			end
			-- PlatformStand is how most custom ragdoll systems disable the Humanoid's own control
			local okPs, ps = pcall(function() return hum.PlatformStand end)
			if okPs and ps then return true end
		end
		-- NOTE: the old structural checks (disabled Motor6D, "ragdoll" in a descendant's name, Anchored root)
		-- matched ordinary players in this game (bodies are CFrame-driven and anchored), which hid every chams
		-- target. Only explicit game flags, Humanoid states and a sustained very high speed count now.
		local sp = measuredSpeed(m)
		local rec = motion[m]
		if rec then
			if sp > FLOAT_SPEED then
				rec.fast = rec.fast or os.clock()
				if os.clock() - rec.fast > 0.6 then return true end
			else
				rec.fast = nil
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function bodyDead(m)
		if not m.Parent then return true end
		local dead = m:GetAttribute("Dead")
		if dead == true or m:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true then return true end
		if dead == false then return false end -- the game says alive: trust it over a Health attribute that is still 0
		local ha = m:GetAttribute("Health")
		if type(ha) == "number" and ha <= 0 then return true end
		local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		return hum ~= nil and hum.Health <= 0
	end

	local lastMineTeam
	local chamsLastErr
	espTeam:OnChanged(function()
		lastMineTeam = nil
		clearChams()
	end)

	local chamsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not espChams:Get() then
			if next(hlPool) or next(adPool) then clearChams() end
			return
		end
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return end
		if chamsFolder.Parent ~= workspace then chamsFolder.Parent = workspace end
		if not next(charConns) then attachCharacters() end

		local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
		local mineTeam = nil
		if espTeam:Get() then
			mineTeam = myTeam() or lastMineTeam
			if mineTeam then lastMineTeam = mineTeam end
		end
		local maxD = espDist:Get()
		local col = espFillCol:Get()
		local fillT = 1 - espFill:Get() / 100

		local list, seenModel = {}, {}

		local function offer(m, plr)
			if not m or seenModel[m] or not m:IsA("Model") or not m:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return end
			seenModel[m] = true
			if plr == lp or m.Name == lp.Name or m == lp.Character then return end
			if m:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then return end
			if bodyDead(m) then return end
			if isFloating(m, plr) then return end
			if mineTeam then
				local theirs = armorTeam(m) or backupTeam(m, plr)
				if theirs and theirs == mineTeam then return end
			end
			local d = 0 -- a model still streaming in has no pivot yet: show it anyway
			local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
			if ok then d = (pivot - camPos).Magnitude end
			if d <= maxD then list[#list + 1] = { key = m, model = m, dist = d } end
		end

		for m in pairs(tracked) do
			if m.Parent then offer(m, playerFromCharacter(m)) else tracked[m] = nil end
		end
		local charsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= lp then
				-- every place a player's body can live: Player.Character, a model named after the player
				-- directly in workspace.Characters, inside a team folder there, or straight in workspace
				offer(plr.Character, plr)
				if charsFolder then
					offer(charsFolder:FindFirstChild(plr.Name), plr)
					for _, sub in ipairs(charsFolder:GetChildren()) do
						if sub:IsA("Folder") then offer(sub:FindFirstChild(plr.Name), plr) end
					end
				end
				local direct = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name)
				if direct and direct:IsA("Model") then offer(direct, plr) end
			end
		end
		table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.dist < b.dist end)

		local budget = math.clamp(31 - gameHlCount - 8, 0, MAX_HL)

		local function report(ok, err)
			if not ok and err ~= chamsLastErr then
				chamsLastErr = err
				warn("[GS chams] " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end

		local want = {}
		for i, it in ipairs(list) do
			want[it.key] = true
			if i <= budget then
				killAd(it.key)
				report(pcall(ensureHighlight, it.key, it.model, col, fillT))
			else
				killHl(it.key)
				report(pcall(ensureAdorn, it.key, it.model, col, fillT))
			end
		end
		for k in pairs(hlPool) do if not want[k] then killHl(k) end end
		for k in pairs(adPool) do if not want[k] then killAd(k) end end
	end)

	env.__gsEspCleanup = function()
		pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() chamsConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() wsConn:Disconnect() end)
		for _, c in pairs(charConns) do
			for _, cn in ipairs(c) do pcall(function() cn:Disconnect() end) end
		end
		pcall(function() hlAddConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() hlRemConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(clearChams)
		dropAll()
		pcall(function() chamsFolder:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() espFolder:Destroy() end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── VIEWMODEL: gun chams ─────────────
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Viewmodel", "Left")

	local vmOn = sec:AddToggle("Gun chams", false)
	local vmCol = vmOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
	local vmPart = sec:AddDropdown("Chams part", { "Weapon", "Arms", "Weapon + arms" }, "Weapon")
	local vmMat = sec:AddDropdown("Chams material", { "Highlight", "Neon", "Foil", "ForceField" }, "Highlight")
	local vmFill = sec:AddSlider("Chams fill", 0, 100, 100, nil, "%", 5)
	local vmNoTex = sec:AddToggle("Chams: remove textures", false)
	vmPart:DependsOn(vmOn, function(v) return v end)
	vmMat:DependsOn(vmOn, function(v) return v end)
	vmFill:DependsOn(vmOn, function(v) return v end)
	vmNoTex:DependsOn(vmOn, function(v) return v end)
	tpGun.chams = { on = vmOn, color = vmCol, part = vmPart, fill = vmFill }

	local orig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })       -- [part] = original look
	local texStash = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })   -- [part] = SurfaceAppearance pushed aside
	local hls = {}                                        -- [adornee] = Highlight (Highlight material)

	local vmFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	vmFolder.Name = "GSVM"
	vmFolder.Parent = workspace

	local WEAPON_ROOTS = { Weapon = true, WeaponL = true, WeaponR = true, CharmBase = true }
	local WEAPON_ONLY = { "Weapon", "WeaponL", "WeaponR" }
	local ARM_ONLY = { "Right Arm", "Left Arm" }
	local SKIP_NAMES = { KillTrak = true, KillTrackKnife = true, NamePlate = true }

	local function isWeaponPart(part, model)
		if WEAPON_ROOTS[part.Name] then return true end
		local p = part.Parent
		while p and p ~= model do
			if WEAPON_ROOTS[p.Name] then return true end
			p = p.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	-- screens (kill counter / name tag) keep their text readable
	local function isScreen(part, model)
		if part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui") then return true end
		local p = part
		while p and p ~= model do
			if SKIP_NAMES[p.Name] then return true end
			p = p.Parent
		end
		return false
	end

	local function restorePart(d)
		local o = orig[d]
		if o then
			orig[d] = nil
			pcall(function()
				d.Material = o.Material
				d.Color = o.Color
				d.Transparency = o.Transparency
				d.Reflectance = o.Reflectance
			end)
		end
		local sa = texStash[d]
		if sa then
			texStash[d] = nil
			local cur = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")
			if cur then cur:Destroy() end
			sa.Parent = d
		end
	end

	local function restoreParts()
		local list = {}
		for d in pairs(orig) do list[#list + 1] = d end
		for d in pairs(texStash) do list[#list + 1] = d end
		for _, d in ipairs(list) do
			if d.Parent then restorePart(d) else orig[d] = nil texStash[d] = nil end
		end
		-- make the skin changer re-check the viewmodel textures
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if cam then
			for _, c in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do pcall(function() c:SetAttribute("GSSkin", nil) end) end
		end
	end

	local function destroyHls()
		for k, hl in pairs(hls) do
			pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
			hls[k] = nil
		end
	end

	local function clearAll()
		destroyHls()
		restoreParts()
	end

	vmOn:OnChanged(function(on) if not on then clearAll() end end)
	vmPart:OnChanged(function() clearAll() end)
	vmMat:OnChanged(function() clearAll() end)

	-- a viewmodel is any Model inside the camera that looks like one (Stats child, arms or weapon)
	local function isVM(model)
		if not model:IsA("Model") then return false end
		if string.find(string.lower(model.Name), "charm", 1, true) then return false end
		return model:FindFirstChild("Stats") ~= nil
			or model:FindFirstChild("Right Arm", true) ~= nil
			or model:FindFirstChild("Weapon", true) ~= nil
	end

	local function chamTargets(model, mode)
		if mode == "Weapon + arms" then return { model } end
		local out = {}
		for _, n in ipairs(mode == "Weapon" and WEAPON_ONLY or ARM_ONLY) do
			local c = model:FindFirstChild(n, true)
			if c then out[#out + 1] = c end
		end
		-- nothing matched: highlight the whole viewmodel instead of showing nothing
		if #out == 0 then out[1] = model end
		return out
	end

	RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSViewmodelFx", 9050, function()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		-- while the third-person gun is shown, the real viewmodel is parked far away,
		-- so the chams are drawn on the third-person gun instead (see "Third person gun")
		if not cam or not vmOn:Get() or isSpectating() or tpGun.active then
			if next(orig) or next(texStash) or next(hls) then clearAll() end
			return
		end

		local mode = vmPart:Get()
		local matName = vmMat:Get()
		local fill = vmFill:Get() / 100
		local col = vmCol:Get()

		local models = {}
		for _, model in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
			if isVM(model) then models[#models + 1] = model end
		end

		if matName == "Highlight" then
			if next(orig) or next(texStash) then restoreParts() end
			if vmFolder.Parent ~= workspace then vmFolder.Parent = workspace end

			local want = {}
			for _, model in ipairs(models) do
				for _, t in ipairs(chamTargets(model, mode)) do want[t] = true end
			end

			for inst in pairs(want) do
				local hl = hls[inst]
				if not hl or not hl.Parent then
					if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
					hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
					hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
					hl.Parent = vmFolder
					hls[inst] = hl
				end
				if hl.Adornee ~= inst then hl.Adornee = inst end
				hl.Enabled = true
				hl.FillColor = col
				hl.FillTransparency = 1 - fill
				hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
			end
			for inst, hl in pairs(hls) do
				if not want[inst] or not inst.Parent then
					pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
					hls[inst] = nil
				end
			end
			return
		end

		-- Neon / Foil / ForceField: change the parts themselves
		if next(hls) then destroyHls() end

		local wantWeapon = mode ~= "Arms"
		local wantArms = mode ~= "Weapon"
		local mat = Enum.Material[matName] or Enum.Material.ForceField
		local tr = 1 - fill
		local noTex = vmNoTex:Get()

		for _, model in ipairs(models) do
			for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then
					local weapon = isWeaponPart(d, model)
					local apply = (weapon and wantWeapon) or ((not weapon) and wantArms)
					if apply and isScreen(d, model) then apply = false end

					if apply then
						local o = orig[d]
						if not o and d.Transparency < 0.99 then
							o = {
								Material = d.Material, Color = d.Color,
								Transparency = d.Transparency, Reflectance = d.Reflectance,
							}
							orig[d] = o
						end
						if o then
							d.Material = mat
							d.Color = col
							d.Transparency = tr
							d.Reflectance = 0
							if noTex then
								local sa = d:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")
								if sa then
									if texStash[d] == nil then
										sa.Parent = nil
										texStash[d] = sa
									else
										sa:Destroy()
									end
								end
							end
						end
					elseif orig[d] or texStash[d] then
						restorePart(d)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	env.__gsVmFxCleanup = function()
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSViewmodelFx") end)
		pcall(clearAll)
		pcall(function() vmFolder:Destroy() end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── VIEWMODEL: custom hands offsets ─────────────
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Custom Hands", "Left")

	local handsOn = sec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local handsX = sec:AddSlider("X", -2, 2, 0.2, nil, " studs", 0.005)
	local handsY = sec:AddSlider("Y", -2, 2, -0.155, nil, " studs", 0.005)
	local handsZ = sec:AddSlider("Z", -2, 2, 0.075, nil, " studs", 0.005)
	handsX:DependsOn(handsOn, function(v) return v end)
	handsY:DependsOn(handsOn, function(v) return v end)
	handsZ:DependsOn(handsOn, function(v) return v end)

	-- viewmodels are Models inside the camera; the game reads Stats.Default
	-- (a Vector3Value) as the hands position offset
	RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSCustomHands", 9060, function()
		if not handsOn:Get() then return end
		local offset = Vector3.new(handsX:Get(), handsY:Get(), handsZ:Get())
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return end
		for _, child in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Model") then
				local statsFolder = child:FindFirstChild("Stats")
				if statsFolder then
					local defaultVal = statsFolder:FindFirstChild("Default")
					if defaultVal and defaultVal:IsA("Vector3Value") then
						defaultVal.Value = offset
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	env.__gsCustomHandsCleanup = function()
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSCustomHands") end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── VISUALS TAB: GUN TRACER CONTROLS ─────────────
local tracerCleanup = function() end
do
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

	local tracerSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Gun Tracers", "Right")
	local tracerOn = tracerSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local tracerColor = tracerOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
	local tracerStyle = tracerSec:AddDropdown("Tracer Style", {
		"Trail (Solid Beam)",
		"Line (Neon Beam)",
	}, "Trail (Solid Beam)")
	local tracerWidth = tracerSec:AddSlider("Thickness", 1, 10, 3, nil, "", 1)
	local tracerTrans = tracerSec:AddSlider("Transparency", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)
	local tracerSpeed = tracerSec:AddSlider("Bullet speed", 200, 3000, 1000, nil, " studs/s", 50)
	local tracerLife = tracerSec:AddSlider("Lifetime", 0.1, 5, 2, nil, "s", 0.1)
	local tracerHideGame = tracerSec:AddToggle("Hide game tracer", false)
	tracerSpeed:DependsOn(tracerStyle, function(v) return v ~= "Line (Neon Beam)" end)

	local tracerFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	tracerFolder.Name = "GSTracers"
	tracerFolder.Parent = workspace

	-- Where the shot really leaves the gun: the muzzle of the third-person gun (when it is shown) or of
	-- the first-person viewmodel.
	local function findMuzzle()
		local function scan(root)
			if not root then return nil end
			local loose
			for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BasePart") then
					local n = string.lower(d.Name)
					if n == "muzzlepart" or n == "muzzle" or n == "muzzlepartr" then return d end
					if not loose and string.find(n, "muzzle", 1, true) and not string.find(n, "flash", 1, true) then
						loose = d
					end
				end
			end
			return loose
		end

		if firstPersonCF then -- third person: use the weapon on my character
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local mine = (chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)) or lp.Character
			local wm = mine and mine:FindFirstChild("WeaponModel")
			return scan(wm and wm:FindFirstChild("Equipped"))
		end

		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if cam then
			for _, c in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Model") then
					local m = scan(c)
					if m then return m end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function tracerEndpoints(rayData, target)
		local origin = rayData.Origin
		if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return end

		local stop
		if target and target.Parent then
			stop = target.Position
			local h1 = type(rayData.Hits) == "table" and rayData.Hits[1]
			if type(h1) == "table" and typeof(h1.Position) == "Vector3" then stop = h1.Position end
		else
			local dir = rayData.Direction
			if typeof(dir) ~= "Vector3" or dir.Magnitude == 0 then return end
			local dist = tonumber(rayData.Distance) or 500
			stop = origin + dir.Unit * math.min(dist, 1000)

			if type(rayData.Hits) == "table" then
				local far = 0
				for _, h in ipairs(rayData.Hits) do
					local pos = type(h) == "table" and h.Position
					if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
						local d = (pos - origin).Magnitude
						if d > far then far, stop = d, pos end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- start at the muzzle when it is close to the shot origin (a wrong match can't throw the tracer away)
		local start = origin
		local mz = findMuzzle()
		if mz and mz.Parent and (mz.Position - origin).Magnitude < 12 then
			start = mz.Position
		else
			local base = firstPersonCF or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
			if base then
				start = base.Position + base:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0.6, -0.5, -1.2))
			end
		end
		-- silent aim: the recorded hit is the aim point INSIDE the body part. End the tracer where it
		-- actually enters the enemy (the surface facing you) instead of floating in the middle of them
		if target and target.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				local model = target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				local toward = stop - start
				if model and toward.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					local params = RaycastParams.new()
					params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
					params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { model }
					local r = workspace:Raycast(start, toward.Unit * (toward.Magnitude + 4), params)
					if r then stop = r.Position end
				end
			end)
		end
		return start, stop
	end

	local function newNeonPart(color, thick, length)
		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Anchored = true
		p.CanCollide = false
		p.CanQuery = false
		p.CanTouch = false
		p.CastShadow = false
		p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		p.Color = color
		p.Size = Vector3.new(thick, thick, length)
		p.Parent = tracerFolder
		return p
	end

	local function spawnBeam(a, b, color, thick, life, trans)
		local total = (b - a).Magnitude
		if total < 0.5 then return end
		local p = newNeonPart(color, thick, total)
		p.Transparency = trans
		p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b)
		local tw = TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Transparency = 1 })
		tw.Completed:Once(function() p:Destroy() end)
		tw:Play()
	end

	local function spawnTrail(a, b, color, thick, speed, life, trans)
		local total = (b - a).Magnitude
		if total < 0.5 then return end
		local dir = (b - a).Unit

		local p = Instance.new("Part")
		p.Anchored = true
		p.CanCollide = false
		p.CanQuery = false
		p.CanTouch = false
		p.CastShadow = false
		p.Transparency = 1
		p.Size = Vector3.new(thick, thick, thick)
		p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(a, b)
		p.Parent = tracerFolder

		local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, thick / 2, 0)
		a0.Parent = p
		local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -thick / 2, 0)
		a1.Parent = p

		local tr = Instance.new("Trail")
		tr.Attachment0 = a0
		tr.Attachment1 = a1
		tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
		tr.LightEmission = 1
		tr.Lifetime = life
		tr.FaceCamera = true
		tr.MinLength = 0
		tr.MaxLength = 0
		tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(trans)
		tr.Parent = p

		local t0 = os.clock()
		local conn
		conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if not p.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
			local d = (os.clock() - t0) * speed
			if d >= total then
				conn:Disconnect()
				p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(b, b + dir)
				task.delay(life + 0.3, function() p:Destroy() end)
				return
			end
			p.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(a + dir * d, a + dir * (d + 1))
		end)
	end

	spawnBulletTracer = function(rayData, redirectedPart)
		-- the bullet hook runs at a low thread identity and Instance.new fails there
		-- ("lacking capability Plugin"); the pcall around this call used to hide that error
		if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
		if not tracerOn:Get() then return end

		local a, b = tracerEndpoints(rayData, redirectedPart)
		if not a then return end

		local style = tracerStyle:Get()
		local color = tracerColor:Get()
		local thick = tracerWidth:Get() * 0.05
		local trans = tracerTrans:Get() / 100

		if style == "Trail (Solid Beam)" then
			spawnTrail(a, b, color, thick, tracerSpeed:Get(), tracerLife:Get(), trans)
		else
			spawnBeam(a, b, color, thick, tracerLife:Get(), trans)
