		end
	end

	-- hides the game's own tracer: its Trails / Beams, and the Debris bullet part right after a shot.
	-- (The old loop also rotated every Debris part toward the silent-aim target every frame; that is gone.)
	local function applyTracerSettings(item)
		if not tracerOn:Get() or not tracerHideGame:Get() then return end
		if item:IsDescendantOf(tracerFolder) then return end
		if item:IsA("Trail") then
			item.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
			item.Enabled = false
		elseif item:IsA("Beam") then
			item.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
			item.Enabled = false
		elseif item:IsA("BasePart") and item.Parent and item.Parent.Name == "Debris"
			and os.clock() - (dbg.shotAt or 0) < 0.25 then
			item.Transparency = 1
		end
	end

	local tracerConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
		if d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("BasePart") then
			task.defer(applyTracerSettings, d)
		end
	end)

	tracerCleanup = function()
		pcall(function() tracerConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() tracerFolder:Destroy() end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── HIT SOUND + KILL SOUND ─────────────
local soundCleanup = function() end
do
	local SKEET_ID = "rbxassetid://80461265049096"

	local soundSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Hit Sounds", "Right")
	local hitOn = soundSec:AddToggle("Hit sound", false)
	local hitChoice = soundSec:AddDropdown("Hit sound type", { "Skeet", "Custom ID" }, "Skeet")
	local hitCustomId = soundSec:AddTextbox("Hit Custom ID", "rbxassetid://0", function() end)
	local hitVol = soundSec:AddSlider("Hit volume", 1, 100, 50, nil, "%", 1)

	local killOn = soundSec:AddToggle("Kill sound", false)
	local killChoice = soundSec:AddDropdown("Kill sound type", { "Skeet", "Custom ID" }, "Skeet")
	local killCustomId = soundSec:AddTextbox("Kill Custom ID", "rbxassetid://0", function() end)
	local killVol = soundSec:AddSlider("Kill volume", 1, 100, 50, nil, "%", 1)

	local soundCache = {}
	local function getBase(id)
		if not id or id == "" then return nil end
		local base = soundCache[id]
		if base then return base end
		base = Instance.new("Sound")
		base.SoundId = id
		base.Volume = 1
		-- the base must live in the DataModel or the audio never actually loads
		base.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
		soundCache[id] = base
		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({ base }) end)
		end)
		return base
	end
	getBase(SKEET_ID)

	local alive = true

	local function soundIdFor(choiceDropdown, textboxObj)
		if choiceDropdown:Get() == "Custom ID" then
			local digits = tostring(textboxObj:Get() or ""):match("%d+")
			if digits and tonumber(digits) and tonumber(digits) > 0 then
				local id = "rbxassetid://" .. digits
				getBase(id) -- preload so the first kill isn't late
				return id
			end
		end
		return SKEET_ID
	end

	local function playSoundId(id, volume)
		local base = getBase(id)
		if not base then return end
		if env.__gsElevate then pcall(env.__gsElevate) end
		-- a fresh clone per play: rapid hits overlap instead of cutting each other off, and Play() on an
		-- unloaded sound simply starts once it has loaded (no waiting on IsLoaded / Loaded)
		local ok = pcall(function()
			local snd = base:Clone()
			snd.Volume = math.clamp(volume / 50, 0, 3)
			snd.Looped = false
			snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
			snd.Ended:Once(function() snd:Destroy() end)
			snd:Play()
			task.delay(10, function() if snd.Parent then snd:Destroy() end end)
		end)
		if not ok then
			base.Volume = math.clamp(volume / 50, 0, 3)
			pcall(function() base:Play() end)
		end
	end

	local hitHeadOnly = soundSec:AddToggle("Hit sound: headshots only", false)
	local lastKillPlay = 0

	local function playKill()
		if not alive or not killOn:Get() then return end
		if os.clock() - lastKillPlay < 0.15 then return end
		lastKillPlay = os.clock()
		playSoundId(soundIdFor(killChoice, killCustomId), killVol:Get())
	end

	-- ── how a hit is confirmed ──
	-- A hit is "opened" when one of MY shots lands on someone (PlayerHitPrediction.Record, or the bullet's
	-- own rayData.Hits read in the Silent Aim hook; either one is enough). It is confirmed when that
	-- victim's health really drops, they get the Dead flag, or their model vanishes.
	--
	-- Why the damage numbers / hit sounds used to skip hits:
	--  * there was ONE pending slot per victim and a confirmation used it up. With fast fire the second
	--    shot's health drop arrived with nothing pending and was ignored. Now a hit stays valid for a
	--    short window and every health drop inside it is shown.
	--  * bots were never watched (only Players:GetPlayers()), so nothing showed on them.
	--  * a kill by "model vanished" had no body to attach the number to, so no number appeared.
	-- Keys: a player's UserId, or the model itself for bots.
	local PENDING_WINDOW = 0.5   -- seconds a hit stays valid
	local FALLBACK_DELAY = 0.25  -- a registered hit with no health proof after this long is trusted anyway
	local lastMine = {}          -- [key] = time of my last registered hit (kills may land well after it)
	local hits = {}              -- [key] = { t, part, fb }
	local canRead = {}           -- [key] = true once any health value was readable
	local states = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- [model] = { key, hp, dead, pos, seen }
	local lastDrop = {}          -- [key] = { t, amount }  (stops the two bodies of one player counting twice)

	local function onOwnHit(key, partName)
		if not alive then return end
		if key == nil or not partName or partName == "" then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		lastMine[key] = now
		local cur = hits[key]
		if cur and now - cur.t < 0.03 then
			-- same shot reported by both sources
			if partName == "Head" then cur.part = "Head" end
			return
		end
		local keepHead = cur and cur.part == "Head" and now - cur.t < PENDING_WINDOW
		local left = (cur and now - cur.t < PENDING_WINDOW) and math.max(cur.n or 0, 0) or 0
		hits[key] = { t = now, part = keepHead and "Head" or partName, fb = false, n = left + 1 }
	end
	registerOwnHit = onOwnHit

	local origPredictionRecord
	local function hookPrediction()
		local PlayerHitPrediction = require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Common.PlayerHitPrediction)
		if type(PlayerHitPrediction) == "table" and type(PlayerHitPrediction.Record) == "function" and not origPredictionRecord then
			origPredictionRecord = PlayerHitPrediction.Record
			if setreadonly then setreadonly(PlayerHitPrediction, false) end
			PlayerHitPrediction.Record = function(shotSeq, userId, partName, position, ...)
				pcall(onOwnHit, userId, partName)
				return origPredictionRecord(shotSeq, userId, partName, position, ...)
			end
		end
		return origPredictionRecord ~= nil
	end
	pcall(hookPrediction)
	-- game content loads at runtime, so the module may not exist yet; retry until it does
	task.spawn(function()
		for _ = 1, 120 do
			if not alive then return end
			if pcall(hookPrediction) and origPredictionRecord then return end
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	local function confirmHit(key, kill, dmg, model, pos)
		local h = hits[key]
		if kill then
			-- death replicates late: a kill counts if my last hit on them was recent, even when that hit
			-- was already spent on an earlier health drop or has expired
			local lm = lastMine[key]
			if not lm or os.clock() - lm > 1.2 then return end
			lastMine[key] = nil
			h = h or { part = "Body", n = 0 }
		else
			if not h or os.clock() - h.t > PENDING_WINDOW then return end
			-- every one of my hits explains exactly one health drop; a teammate's damage after it is ignored
			if (h.n or 1) <= 0 then return end
		end
		h.n = (h.n or 1) - 1
		local part = h.part
		h.fb = true -- consumed: the expiry fallback must not replay this hit
		if kill then hits[key] = nil end

		if dbg.hitmarker then pcall(dbg.hitmarker, kill and "kill" or "hit", key) end
		if dbg.damageNumber then
			local shown = tonumber(dmg)
			if not shown and kill then

				shown = nil
			end
			if shown then
				pcall(dbg.damageNumber, key, shown, part == "Head", kill, model, pos)
			end
		end

		if kill then
			playKill()
		elseif hitOn:Get() and (not hitHeadOnly:Get() or part == "Head") then
			playSoundId(soundIdFor(hitChoice, hitCustomId), hitVol:Get())
		end
	end

	local HEALTH_ATTRS = { "Health", "HP", "health" }
	local function readHealth(plr, model)
		local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then return hum.Health end
		for _, n in ipairs(HEALTH_ATTRS) do
			local v = model:GetAttribute(n)
			if type(v) == "number" then return v end
		end
		if plr then
			for _, n in ipairs(HEALTH_ATTRS) do
				local v = plr:GetAttribute(n)
				if type(v) == "number" then return v end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function deadFlag(model)
		return model:GetAttribute("Dead") == true or model:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true
	end

	local function candidates(plr)
		local list = {}
		local c = plr.Character
		if c and c.Parent then list[#list + 1] = c end
		local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local m = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)
		if m and m:IsA("Model") and m ~= c then list[#list + 1] = m end
		return list
	end

	-- Is the victim behind this hit key dead / gone right now? Used when a fast
	-- kill happens between scan ticks and no alive→dead transition was ever seen.
	local function victimDeadNow(key)
		if typeof(key) == "Instance" then
			if not key:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return true end
			local hp = readHealth(nil, key)
			return deadFlag(key) or (hp ~= nil and hp <= 0)
		end
		local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(key)
		if not plr then return true end -- left the game mid-fight
		local list = candidates(plr)
		if #list == 0 then return true end -- character model gone = died
		for _, m in ipairs(list) do
			local hp = readHealth(plr, m)
			if not (deadFlag(m) or (hp ~= nil and hp <= 0)) then return false end
		end
		return true
	end

	local function step(key, plr, model, now)
		local hp = readHealth(plr, model)
		local dead = deadFlag(model) or (hp ~= nil and hp <= 0)
		if hp ~= nil then canRead[key] = true end

		local st = states[model]
		-- did my last shot actually point at this victim (and happen just now)?
		local function aimedAt(mdl, pos)
			local ls = dbg.lastShot
			if not ls or now - ls.t > 0.5 or typeof(ls.origin) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(ls.dir) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
			local root = (mdl and (mdl:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or mdl:FindFirstChild("Head"))) 
			local p = (root and root:IsA("BasePart") and root.Position) or pos
			if not p or ls.dir.Magnitude < 0.001 then return false end
			local d = ls.dir.Unit
			local rel = p - ls.origin
			local along = rel:Dot(d)
			if along < 0 then return false end
			return (rel - d * along).Magnitude < 7
		end
		-- safety net: Record / rayData never reported this hit, but I fired a moment ago and this
		-- victim just lost health or died -> count it as mine
		local function adopt()
			local h = hits[key]
			if (not h or now - h.t > PENDING_WINDOW) and aimedAt(model, st and st.pos) then
				hits[key] = { t = now, part = "Body", fb = false, n = 1 }
				lastMine[key] = now
			end
		end
		if not st then
			st = { key = key, hp = hp, dead = dead }
			states[model] = st
			-- fast kill: the first time we ever see this model it is already dead.
			-- If a fresh hit is pending on them, it was ours — confirm the kill.
			if dead then
				confirmHit(key, true, hp, model, nil)
			end
		else
			if dead and not st.dead then
				adopt()
				confirmHit(key, true, st.hp, model, st.pos)
			elseif hp ~= nil and st.hp ~= nil and hp < st.hp - 0.01 then
				local amount = st.hp - math.max(hp, 0)
				local ld = lastDrop[key]
				if not (ld and ld.t == now and math.abs(ld.amount - amount) < 0.5) then
					lastDrop[key] = { t = now, amount = amount }
					adopt()
					confirmHit(key, dead, amount, model, st.pos)
				end
			end
			if hp ~= nil then st.hp = hp end
			st.dead = dead
		end

		local base = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if base and base:IsA("BasePart") then st.pos = base.Position end
		if model:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then st.seen = true end
	end

	local function stepBot(m, now)
		if m:IsA("Model") and m.Name ~= lp.Name and m:GetAttribute("Bot") == true
			and not playerFromCharacter(m) then
			step(m, nil, m, now)
		end
	end

	local acc = 0
	local scanConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not alive then return end
		acc += dt
		if acc < 0.03 then return end
		acc = 0
		local now = os.clock()

		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= lp then
				for _, model in ipairs(candidates(plr)) do
					step(plr.UserId, plr, model, now)
				end
			end
		end

		local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		if folder then
			for _, c in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Folder") then
					for _, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do stepBot(m, now) end
				else
					stepBot(c, now)
				end
			end
		end

		-- a model that disappears right after my hit counts as a kill (only if it was in the world before)
		for model, st in pairs(states) do
			if st.seen and not model:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				if not st.dead then
					local h0 = hits[st.key]
					local ls = dbg.lastShot
					if (not h0 or now - h0.t > PENDING_WINDOW) and ls and now - ls.t < 0.5 and st.pos
						and typeof(ls.origin) == "Vector3" and typeof(ls.dir) == "Vector3" and ls.dir.Magnitude > 0.001 then
						local d = ls.dir.Unit
						local rel = st.pos - ls.origin
						local along = rel:Dot(d)
						if along > 0 and (rel - d * along).Magnitude < 7 then
							hits[st.key] = { t = now, part = "Body", fb = false, n = 1 }
							lastMine[st.key] = now
						end
					end
					confirmHit(st.key, true, st.hp, nil, st.pos)
				end
				states[model] = nil
			end
		end

		-- expire old hits; if health can't be read for that victim, trust the hit once
		for key, h in pairs(hits) do
			if now - h.t > PENDING_WINDOW then
				-- no proof ever arrived (lag / health not replicated / model swapped):
				-- trust the shot registration instead of staying silent
				if not h.fb then
					local wasKill = victimDeadNow(key)
					if dbg.hitmarker then pcall(dbg.hitmarker, wasKill and "kill" or "hit", key) end
					if wasKill then
						playKill()
					elseif hitOn:Get() and (not hitHeadOnly:Get() or h.part == "Head") then
						playSoundId(soundIdFor(hitChoice, hitCustomId), hitVol:Get())
					end
				end
				hits[key] = nil
			elseif not h.fb and now - h.t > FALLBACK_DELAY then
				h.fb = true
				confirmHit(key, victimDeadNow(key))
			end
		end
	end)

	soundCleanup = function()
		alive = false
		registerOwnHit = function() end
		pcall(function() scanConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function()
			local ok, PlayerHitPrediction = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Common.PlayerHitPrediction)
			end)
			if ok and origPredictionRecord and type(PlayerHitPrediction) == "table" then
				PlayerHitPrediction.Record = origPredictionRecord
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			for _, b in pairs(soundCache) do b:Destroy() end
		end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── DAMAGE INDICATOR ─────────────
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Damage indicator", "Right")
	local dmgOn = sec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local dmgCol = dmgOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)

	local FONT_NAMES = {
		"GothamBlack", "GothamBold", "Gotham", "Code", "Arcade", "Bangers", "Oswald",
		"PermanentMarker", "SourceSansBold", "Ubuntu", "Roboto", "RobotoMono", "Michroma",
		"LuckiestGuy", "SciFi", "FredokaOne", "Jura", "Nunito", "TitilliumWeb",
		"SpecialElite", "Cartoon", "Highway",
	}
	local fontOpts = {}
	for _, n in ipairs(FONT_NAMES) do
		if pcall(function() return Enum.Font[n] end) then fontOpts[#fontOpts + 1] = n end
	end
	local dmgFont = sec:AddDropdown("Font", fontOpts, fontOpts[1] or "GothamBold")
	local dmgSize = sec:AddSlider("Size", 10, 60, 22, nil, "px", 1)
	local dmgOutline = sec:AddSlider("Outline", 0, 4, 1, nil, "px", 0.5)
	local dmgTime = sec:AddSlider("Duration", 0.3, 3, 1.2, nil, "s", 0.1)

	if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name = "GSDamage"
	gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
	gui.DisplayOrder = 996
	do
		local okH, hui = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
		gui.Parent = (okH and hui) or CoreGui
	end

	local active = {}

	local function bodyOf(plr)
		local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
		local m = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)
		if m and m:IsA("Model") then return m end
		return plr.Character
	end

	-- key = a player's UserId, or the model itself for bots
	local function resolveBody(key)
		if typeof(key) == "Instance" then return key end
		if type(key) == "number" then
			local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(key)
			return plr and bodyOf(plr) or nil
		end
		return nil
	end

	dbg.damageNumber = function(key, dmg, head, kill, model, pos)
		if not dmgOn:Get() then return end
		if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end

		local m = model
		if not (m and m.Parent) then m = resolveBody(key) end
		local part = m and (m:FindFirstChild("Head") or m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		-- a body that is already gone (kill by "model vanished") still gets its number, at the last known spot
		if not part and typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return end

		dmg = tonumber(dmg)
		if not dmg then return end -- no fallback to the weapon's flat damage stat: that's what caused
		-- the same number ("49") to show on every kill
		local shown = math.floor(dmg + 0.5)
		if shown <= 0 then return end

		local size = dmgSize:Get()
		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, size + 12)
		lbl.Text = tostring(shown)
		lbl.TextSize = size
		lbl.TextColor3 = dmgCol:Get()
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		lbl.Visible = false
		local okF, fnt = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[dmgFont:Get()] end)
		lbl.Font = (okF and fnt) or Enum.Font.GothamBold

		local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
		stroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		stroke.Thickness = dmgOutline:Get()
		stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual
		stroke.Parent = lbl
		lbl.Parent = gui

		active[#active + 1] = {
			lbl = lbl, stroke = stroke, part = part, last = part and part.Position or pos,
			born = os.clock(), dur = dmgTime:Get(),
			ox = (math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2, rise = 2.5,
		}
	end

	local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		for i = #active, 1, -1 do
			local e = active[i]
			local k = (os.clock() - e.born) / e.dur
			if k >= 1 or not cam then
				pcall(function() e.lbl:Destroy() end)
				table.remove(active, i)
			else
				if e.part and e.part.Parent then e.last = e.part.Position end
				local world = e.last + Vector3.new(e.ox, 1.2 + e.rise * k, 0)
				local sp, on = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
				e.lbl.Visible = on
				e.lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.X, sp.Y)
				local a = k < 0.6 and 0 or (k - 0.6) / 0.4
				e.lbl.TextTransparency = a
				e.stroke.Transparency = a
			end
		end
	end)

	env.__gsDmgCleanup = function()
		dbg.damageNumber = nil
		pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
	end
end

-- ───────────── CUBE CHECKER ─────────────
do
	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Cube checker", "Right")
	local cubeOn = sec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	-- green cube = the part under the crosshair can be wallbanged, red = solid
	local cubeOkCol = cubeOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 80), false)
	local cubeBadCol = cubeOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80), false)
	local cubeDist = sec:AddSlider("Max ray distance", 50, 3000, 1000, nil, " studs", 50)
	local cubeSize = sec:AddSlider("Size", 0.5, 15, 3, nil, " studs", 0.1)
	local cubeThick = sec:AddSlider("Thickness", 0.05, 1, 0.1, nil, " studs", 0.05)
	local cubeTrans = sec:AddSlider("Transparency", 0, 100, 30, nil, "%", 5)
	local cubeText = sec:AddToggle("Show status text", true)

	-- ── material penetration data, same rules as the game's Shared.Raycast ──
	local MAP_MIN_PEN = table.freeze({ Reactor = -1 })
	local VARIANT_PEN = table.freeze({ ["Sandy Brick"] = 0, IndoorWall = 0 })
	local MATERIAL_PEN = table.freeze({
		[Enum.Material.Asphalt] = 0, [Enum.Material.Basalt] = 0, [Enum.Material.Brick] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Cobblestone] = 0, [Enum.Material.Concrete] = 0, [Enum.Material.CrackedLava] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.DiamondPlate] = 0, [Enum.Material.Foil] = 0, [Enum.Material.Glacier] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Granite] = 0, [Enum.Material.Grass] = 0, [Enum.Material.Ground] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Ice] = 0, [Enum.Material.LeafyGrass] = 0, [Enum.Material.Limestone] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Marble] = 0, [Enum.Material.Metal] = 0, [Enum.Material.Mud] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Pavement] = 0, [Enum.Material.Rock] = 0, [Enum.Material.Salt] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Sand] = 0, [Enum.Material.Sandstone] = 0, [Enum.Material.Slate] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Snow] = 0, [Enum.Material.ForceField] = 0, [Enum.Material.Neon] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.CorrodedMetal] = 0, [Enum.Material.Pebble] = 0, [Enum.Material.CeramicTiles] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Plaster] = 0,
		[Enum.Material.Plastic] = 10, [Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic] = 10, [Enum.Material.Wood] = 10,
		[Enum.Material.WoodPlanks] = 10, [Enum.Material.Cardboard] = 10,
		[Enum.Material.Glass] = 25, [Enum.Material.Fabric] = 25,
	})

	local function applyMapMinPenetration(v)
		if v ~= 0 then return v end
		local mapName = workspace:GetAttribute("Map")
		if typeof(mapName) ~= "string" then return v end
		return MAP_MIN_PEN[mapName] or v
	end

	-- breakable doors always behave like metal, like the game's raycast does
	local function penMaterialOf(part)
		local p = part.Parent
		local ok, door = pcall(function() return p and p:HasTag("BreakableDoor") end)
		if ok and door then return Enum.Material.Metal end
		return part.Material
	end

	local depthParams = RaycastParams.new()
	depthParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	depthParams.CollisionGroup = "Bullet"

	-- studs the bullet would travel through the part: cast from far along the ray
	-- back toward the entry point and find the exit surface
	local function penetrationDepth(entryPos, dirUnit, part)
		depthParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { part }
		local far = entryPos + dirUnit * 1000
		local hit = workspace:Raycast(far, entryPos - far, depthParams)
		if not hit then return nil end
		return (entryPos - hit.Position).Magnitude
	end

	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "GSCubeChecker"
	folder.Parent = workspace

	local cube = Instance.new("Part")
	cube.Name = "Cube"
	cube.Anchored = true
	cube.CanCollide = false
	cube.CanQuery = false
	cube.CanTouch = false
	cube.CastShadow = false
	cube.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
	cube.Transparency = 0.3
	cube.Size = Vector3.new(1, 0.1, 1)
	cube.Transparency = 1
	cube.Parent = folder

	local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	bb.Name = "Status"
	bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 28)
	bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
	bb.Enabled = false
	bb.Parent = cube

	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	lbl.TextSize = 16
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
	lbl.Text = ""
	lbl.Parent = bb

	local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
	rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	rayParams.CollisionGroup = "Bullet"

	-- hide the checker while spectating: the camera follows someone else's
	-- view, so the crosshair ray is not ours anymore
	local function spectatingOther()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local subj = cam and cam.CameraSubject
		if not subj then return false end
		local m = subj
		if subj:IsA("Humanoid") or subj:IsA("BasePart") then m = subj.Parent end
		if m and m:IsA("Model") and m.Name ~= lp.Name then
			local p = Players:FindFirstChild(m.Name)
			return p ~= nil and p ~= lp
		end
		return false
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		-- runs every frame so the marker tracks the crosshair with no visible delay
		-- (the old 0.05s polling loop made it lag up to 50ms behind the aim)
		local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
		local conn = rs.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				local on = cubeOn:Get()
				cube.Transparency = 1
				bb.Enabled = false
				if not on then return end
				if spectatingOther() then return end

				local char = lp.Character
				local filter = { camera, folder }
				if char then filter[#filter + 1] = char end
				rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter

				local p = aimPoint()
				local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(p.X, p.Y)
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * cubeDist:Get(), rayParams)

				-- nothing under the crosshair within range
				if not hit then return end

				-- a body is not a wall: don't check character parts
				local m = hit.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
				if m and m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return end

				local dirUnit = ray.Direction.Unit
				local depth = penetrationDepth(hit.Position, dirUnit, hit.Instance)

				-- penetration budget for this part, same rules as Shared.Raycast:
				-- material/variant base + the equipped weapon's real penetration
				local variant = hit.Instance.MaterialVariant
				local base
				if variant == "" or VARIANT_PEN[variant] == nil then
					base = MATERIAL_PEN[penMaterialOf(hit.Instance)]
				else
					base = VARIANT_PEN[variant]
				end
				local pen = (type(dbg.activePen) == "number" and dbg.activePen) or 0
				local budget = applyMapMinPenetration(base or 0) + pen

				local ok = depth ~= nil and depth <= budget
				cube.Color = ok and cubeOkCol:Get() or cubeBadCol:Get()
				-- HVH-style wall marker: a thin flat tag sitting flush on the wall
				-- surface at the impact point, like the penetration indicator in
				-- gamesense — it touches the wall, never clipping into it
				local th = cubeThick:Get()
				local s = cubeSize:Get()
				cube.Size = Vector3.new(s, s, th)
				-- flush against the surface: pushed out along the normal by half its thickness
				cube.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hit.Position + hit.Normal * (th / 2), hit.Position + hit.Normal)
				cube.Transparency = math.clamp(cubeTrans:Get() / 100, 0, 1)
				bb.Enabled = cubeText:Get()

				if depth == nil then
					lbl.Text = "SOLID (unmeasurable)"
				elseif ok then
					lbl.Text = ("WALLBANGABLE (%.1f / %.1f studs)"):format(depth, budget)
				else
					lbl.Text = ("SOLID (%.1f > %.1f studs)"):format(depth, budget)
				end
			end)
		end)
	end)
end

-- ───────────── BULLET IMPACTS ─────────────
do
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

	local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Bullet impacts", "Right")
	local impOn = sec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
	local impCol = impOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 200), false)
	local impSize = sec:AddSlider("Size", 1, 30, 8, nil, "", 1)
	local impTime = sec:AddSlider("Duration", 0.5, 10, 3, nil, "s", 0.5)
	local impTrans = sec:AddSlider("Transparency", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)
	local impFade = sec:AddToggle("Fade out", true)

	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "GSImpacts"
	folder.Parent = workspace

	dbg.bulletImpact = function(rayData, redirected)
		if not impOn:Get() then return end
		if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
		if not folder.Parent then folder.Parent = workspace end

		local origin = rayData.Origin
		if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return end

		local pos
		if redirected and redirected.Parent then
			pos = redirected.Position
			local h1 = type(rayData.Hits) == "table" and rayData.Hits[1]
			if type(h1) == "table" and typeof(h1.Position) == "Vector3" then pos = h1.Position end
		elseif type(rayData.Hits) == "table" and #rayData.Hits > 0 then
			-- the first entry is the actual impact point; the old code took the
			-- FARTHEST one, which is wrong and often picked nothing on a miss
			for _, h in ipairs(rayData.Hits) do
				local p = h
				if type(h) == "table" then p = h.Position or h.Pos or h.Point end
				if typeof(p) == "Instance" and p:IsA("BasePart") then p = p.Position end
				if typeof(p) == "Vector3" then pos = p break end
			end
		end

		if not pos then
			-- nothing hit (or Hits wasn't usable): draw it at the end of the ray anyway,
			-- so a miss still shows an impact instead of silently drawing nothing
			local dir = rayData.Direction
			if typeof(dir) == "Vector3" and dir.Magnitude > 0 then
				local dist = tonumber(rayData.Distance)
				if not dist or dist <= 0 then dist = 500 end
				pos = origin + dir.Unit * dist
			end
		end
		if not pos then return end

		local s = impSize:Get() * 0.05
		local initialTrans = math.clamp(impTrans:Get() / 100, 0, 1)

		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.CanQuery = false
		part.CanTouch = false
		part.CastShadow = false
		part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
		part.Color = impCol:Get()
		part.Size = Vector3.new(s, s, s)
		part.Transparency = initialTrans
		part.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
		part.Parent = folder

		local life = impTime:Get()
		if impFade:Get() then
			local tw = TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Transparency = 1 })
			tw.Completed:Once(function() part:Destroy() end)
			tw:Play()
		else
			task.delay(life, function() part:Destroy() end)
		end
	end

	-- ───────────── click helper ─────────────
	local clickMethod

	local function doClick()
		local m = clickMethod and clickMethod:Get() or "Auto"
		if m == "Auto" then
			if mouse1press and mouse1release then m = "Press/Release"
			elseif mouse1click then m = "mouse1click"
			else m = "VirtualInput" end
		end

		if m == "Press/Release" and mouse1press and mouse1release then
			mouse1press()
			task.delay(0.03, mouse1release)
		elseif m == "mouse1click" and mouse1click then
			mouse1click()
		else
			local pos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
			task.delay(0.03, function()
				VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function click()
		local ok, err = pcall(doClick)
		dbg.clickErr = (not ok) and tostring(err) or nil
		return ok
	end

	-- ───────────── menu visibility helpers ─────────────
	local isMenuVisible, setMenuVisible, mouseOverMenu
	do
		local menuVisible = true

		isMenuVisible = function()
			local ok, v = pcall(function() return Window:IsVisible() end)
			if ok and type(v) == "boolean" then menuVisible = v end
			return menuVisible
		end

		setMenuVisible = function(v)
			menuVisible = v
			pcall(function() Window:SetVisible(v) end)
		end

		mouseOverMenu = function()
			if not isMenuVisible() then return false end
			local ok, over = pcall(function()
				local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				local main = Window.Main
				local p, s = main.AbsolutePosition, main.AbsoluteSize
				return m.X >= p.X and m.X <= p.X + s.X and m.Y >= p.Y and m.Y <= p.Y + s.Y
			end)
			return ok and over == true
		end
	end

	-- ───────────── hotkeys (shared by every toggle with a keybind) ─────────────
	local hotkeys = {}
	local function addHotkey(toggle, keybind)
		local rec = { Toggle = toggle, Key = keybind, Bound = 0 }
		keybind.KeyChanged = function() rec.Bound = os.clock() end
		hotkeys[#hotkeys + 1] = rec
		return keybind
	end

	-- ───────────── LEGIT (triggerbot) ─────────────
	local getTrigTarget, trigOn, trigKey, trigDelay, targetPart
	do
		local trigSec = LegitTab:AddSection("Triggerbot", "Left")
		trigOn = trigSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
		trigKey = addHotkey(trigOn, trigOn:AddKeybind(nil, "Toggle"))
		trigDelay = trigSec:AddSlider("Click delay", 0, 500, 100, nil, "ms", 10)
		targetPart = trigSec:AddDropdown("Target part", { "Head", "Body", "Any" }, "Head")
		clickMethod = trigSec:AddDropdown("Click method", { "Auto", "Press/Release", "mouse1click", "VirtualInput" }, "Auto")

		local trigFilter = LegitTab:AddSection("Filters", "Right")
		local trigTeam = trigFilter:AddToggle("Team check", false)
		local trigHostage = trigFilter:AddToggle("Ignore hostages", false)
		local trigDist = trigFilter:AddSlider("Max distance", 50, 2000, 1000, nil, " studs", 50)

		local BODY_PARTS = {
			["Torso"] = true,
			["UpperTorso"] = true,
			["LowerTorso"] = true,
			["HumanoidRootPart"] = true,
		}

		local function insideFolder(inst, name)
			local p = inst.Parent
			while p do
				if p.Name == name then return true end
				p = p.Parent
			end
			return false
		end

		local function findCharacter(inst)
			local m = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			while m do
				if insideFolder(m, "Hostages") then
					local top = m
					while top.Parent and top.Parent:IsA("Model") do top = top.Parent end
					return top, "hostage", nil, "inside Hostages folder"
				end
				local plr = playerFromCharacter(m)
				if plr then return m, "player", plr, "Player.Character" end
				if m.Parent and TEAM_FOLDERS[m.Parent.Name] then
					return m, "bot", nil, "inside " .. m.Parent.Name .. " folder"
				end
				if m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return m, "npc", nil, "Humanoid" end
				m = m.Parent and m.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function partMatches(partName)
			local want = targetPart:Get()
			if want == "Any" then return true end
			if want == "Head" then return partName == "Head" end
			if want == "Body" then return BODY_PARTS[partName] == true end
			return false
		end

		getTrigTarget = function()
			dbg.hit, dbg.model, dbg.how, dbg.team, dbg.dist = "-", "-", "-", "-", 0
			dbg.onEnemy = false

			local char = lp.Character
			if not char then return nil, "no character" end

			local p = aimPoint()
			local savedCF = camera.CFrame
			local swapped = false
			if firstPersonCF and thirdPersonCF and savedCF == thirdPersonCF then
				camera.CFrame = firstPersonCF
				swapped = true
			end
			local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(p.X, p.Y)
			if swapped then camera.CFrame = savedCF end

			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char, camera }

			local result = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * trigDist:Get(), params)
			if not result then return nil, "ray hit nothing" end

			dbg.hit = result.Instance.Name
			dbg.dist = (result.Position - ray.Origin).Magnitude

			local nearest = result.Instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			local model, kind, plr, how = findCharacter(result.Instance)

			if not model then
				if nearest and nearest:FindFirstChild("Head") then
					model, kind, how = nearest, "rig", "any rig with Head"
				else
					return nil, "hit non-character: " .. (nearest and nearest.Name or result.Instance.Name)
				end
			end

			dbg.modelRef = model
			dbg.model = model.Name .. " (" .. kind .. ")"
			dbg.how = how

			if kind == "hostage" and trigHostage:Get() then
				return nil, "hostage (ignored): " .. model.Name
			end

			if kind == "bot" or kind == "npc" then
				return nil, "bot/NPC (ignored): " .. model.Name
			end

			if isCharacterDead(model) then
				return nil, "target is dead: " .. model.Name
			end

			if plr == lp then return nil, "hit yourself" end

			if trigTeam:Get() then
				local mine = myTeam()
				local theirs = armorTeam(model) or backupTeam(model, plr)
				dbg.team = "me=" .. tostring(mine) .. " them=" .. tostring(theirs)
				if mine and theirs and mine == theirs then
					return nil, "teammate: " .. model.Name
				end
			end

			dbg.onEnemy = true

			if not partMatches(result.Instance.Name) then
				return nil, "enemy, wrong part (hit " .. result.Instance.Name .. ", want " .. targetPart:Get() .. ")"
			end

			return model, "target ok: " .. model.Name
		end
	end

	-- ───────────── menu key + hotkey input handling ─────────────
	local beganConn, endedConn
	do
		local menuBind, menuBoundAt = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift, 0
		function Window:SetBind(k)
			menuBind = k
			menuBoundAt = os.clock()
		end

		local function keyMatches(input, key)
			if not key then return false end
			return input.KeyCode == key or input.UserInputType == key
		end

		beganConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end

			if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode == menuBind
				and os.clock() - menuBoundAt > 0.1 then
				setMenuVisible(not isMenuVisible())
			end

			for _, h in ipairs(hotkeys) do
				if os.clock() - h.Bound > 0.1 and keyMatches(input, h.Key:GetKey()) then
					local mode = h.Key:GetMode()
					if mode == "Toggle" then h.Toggle:Set(not h.Toggle:Get())
					elseif mode == "Hold" then h.Toggle:Set(true)
					elseif mode == "Off" then h.Toggle:Set(false) end
					h.Key:SetActive(h.Toggle:Get())
				end
			end
		end)

		endedConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			for _, h in ipairs(hotkeys) do
				if keyMatches(input, h.Key:GetKey()) then
					local mode = h.Key:GetMode()
					if mode == "Hold" then h.Toggle:Set(false)
					elseif mode == "Off" then h.Toggle:Set(true) end
					h.Key:SetActive(h.Toggle:Get())
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── THIRD PERSON + VIEWMODEL (Visuals) ─────────────
	local restoreCharacter, restoreViewmodel
	do
		local camSec2 = VisualTab:AddSection("Third person", "Left")
		local tpOn = camSec2:AddToggle("Third person", false)
		addHotkey(tpOn, tpOn:AddKeybind(nil, "Toggle"))
		local tpDist = camSec2:AddSlider("Camera distance", 3, 20, 8, nil, " studs", 1)
		local tpShow = camSec2:AddToggle("Show my character", false)
		tpGun.toggle = camSec2:AddToggle("Show my gun", false)
		local tpHideVM = camSec2:AddToggle("Hide viewmodel", false)
		local tpVMMode = camSec2:AddDropdown("Viewmodel hide mode", { "Arms only (keep gun)", "Everything" }, "Everything")

		local tpParams = RaycastParams.new()
		tpParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

		local HIDDEN_PARTS = { HumanoidRootPart = true, CameraPart = true }
		local BODY_NAMES = {
			Head = true, Torso = true, UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
			["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true, ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
			LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
			RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
			LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
			RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
		}

		local GUN_WORDS = { "weapon", "gun", "rifle", "pistol", "knife", "handle", "barrel", "magazine", "scope", "grip", "stock" }
		local HELPER_WORDS = { "muzzle", "light", "hitbox", "attachment", "camera" }

		local function hasWord(name, words)
			name = string.lower(name)
			for _, w in ipairs(words) do
				if string.find(name, w, 1, true) then return true end
			end
			return false
		end

		local function inWeapon(part, root)
			if hasWord(part.Name, HELPER_WORDS) then return false end
			local p = part
			while p and p ~= root do
				if hasWord(p.Name, GUN_WORDS) then return true end
				p = p.Parent
			end
			return false
		end

		-- parts the game itself keeps invisible (weapon rigging / helpers); revealing them draws white slabs
		local GAME_HELPERS = {
			MuzzlePart = true, MuzzlePartL = true, MuzzlePartR = true, RootPart = true,
			Hitbox = true, Insert = true, move = true, Silencer = true,
		}
		-- holstered / equipped weapon models: the game owns their visibility (the equipped weapon's holster
		-- copy is hidden on purpose), so "Show my character" must never un-hide anything in there
		local function inWeaponFolders(d)
			return d:FindFirstAncestor("WeaponAttachments") ~= nil or d:FindFirstAncestor("WeaponModel") ~= nil
		end
		local keepHidden = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local revealed = {}
		local ltmSaved = {}
		local weaponSet = {}
		local revealList = {}
		local lastRevealScan = 0

		restoreCharacter = function()
			for part, orig in pairs(revealed) do
				if part.Parent then part.Transparency = orig end
			end
			for part, orig in pairs(ltmSaved) do
				if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = orig end
			end
			table.clear(revealed)
			table.clear(ltmSaved)
			table.clear(revealList)
			table.clear(weaponSet)
		end

		local function revealCharacter()
			if os.clock() - lastRevealScan > 0.5 then
				lastRevealScan = os.clock()
				table.clear(revealList)
				table.clear(weaponSet)
				for _, root in ipairs(characterRoots()) do
					for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("BasePart")
							and not HIDDEN_PARTS[d.Name] and not GAME_HELPERS[d.Name]
							and not string.find(string.lower(d.Name), "hitbox", 1, true) then
							revealList[#revealList + 1] = d
							if inWeaponFolders(d) then keepHidden[d] = true end
							if inWeapon(d, root) then weaponSet[d] = true end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for _, part in ipairs(revealList) do
				if part.Parent then
					if ltmSaved[part] == nil then ltmSaved[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier end
					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
					if part.Transparency >= 0.99 and not keepHidden[part]
						and (part:IsA("MeshPart") or BODY_NAMES[part.Name] or weaponSet[part]) then
						if revealed[part] == nil then revealed[part] = part.Transparency end
						part.Transparency = 0
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local vmSaved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local vmList = {}
		local lastVmScan = 0

		local function isMyCharacter(d)
			if lp.Character and d:IsDescendantOf(lp.Character) then return true end
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
			return mine ~= nil and d:IsDescendantOf(mine)
		end

		local function isEffect(d)
			return d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Sparkles")
				or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Light")
				or d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui")
		end

		local function scanViewmodel()
			table.clear(vmList)
			local function consider(d, effectsOnly)
				if isEffect(d) then
					vmList[#vmList + 1] = { d, "Effect" }
				elseif not effectsOnly and not isMyCharacter(d)
					and (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture")) then
					vmList[#vmList + 1] = { d, "Transparency" }
				end
			end
			local function addRoot(root)
				consider(root)
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do consider(d) end
			end

			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if cam then
				for _, d in ipairs(cam:GetDescendants()) do consider(d) end
			end
			for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
				local n = string.lower(c.Name)
				if string.find(n, "viewmodel", 1, true) or string.find(n, "view_model", 1, true) then
					addRoot(c)
				end
			end
			for _, root in ipairs(characterRoots()) do
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do consider(d, true) end
			end
		end

		restoreViewmodel = function()
			for inst, list in pairs(vmSaved) do
				if inst.Parent then
					for _, e in ipairs(list) do pcall(function() inst[e[1]] = e[2] end) end
				end
			end
			table.clear(vmSaved)
			table.clear(vmList)
		end

		local function save(inst, prop)
			local list = vmSaved[inst]
			if not list then list = {}; vmSaved[inst] = list end
			for _, e in ipairs(list) do if e[1] == prop then return end end
			list[#list + 1] = { prop, inst[prop] }
		end

		local HIDDEN_SEQ = NumberSequence.new(1)

		local function hideViewmodel()
			if os.clock() - lastVmScan > 0.1 then
				lastVmScan = os.clock()
				scanViewmodel()
			end
			for _, e in ipairs(vmList) do
				local inst, kind = e[1], e[2]
				if inst.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						if kind == "Transparency" then
							save(inst, "Transparency")
							inst.Transparency = 1
						else
							save(inst, "Enabled")
							inst.Enabled = false
							if inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
								save(inst, "Transparency")
								inst.Transparency = HIDDEN_SEQ
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		local VMClass, origVMRender
		local VM_AWAY = CFrame.new(0, 10000, 0)
		local WEAPON_ROOTS = { Weapon = true, WeaponL = true, WeaponR = true, CharmBase = true }
		local armSaved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local armCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local function inWeaponRoot(d, model)
			local p = d.Parent
			while p and p ~= model do
				if WEAPON_ROOTS[p.Name] then return true end
				p = p.Parent
			end
			return false
		end

		local function hideArms(model)
			local c = armCache[model]
			if not c or os.clock() - c.at > 0.5 then
				local list = {}
				for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture"))
						and not WEAPON_ROOTS[d.Name] and not inWeaponRoot(d, model) then
						list[#list + 1] = d
					end
				end
				c = { list = list, at = os.clock() }
				armCache[model] = c
			end
			for _, d in ipairs(c.list) do
				if d.Parent then
					if armSaved[d] == nil then armSaved[d] = d.Transparency end
					d.Transparency = 1
				end
			end
		end

		local function restoreArms()
			for d, orig in pairs(armSaved) do
				if d.Parent then d.Transparency = orig end
			end
			table.clear(armSaved)
			table.clear(armCache)
		end

		local function viewmodelIsOther(vm)
			for _, key in ipairs({ "Player", "Owner", "Character", "Plr" }) do
				local v = rawget(vm, key)
				if v ~= nil then
					if typeof(v) == "Instance" then
						if v:IsA("Player") then return v ~= lp end
						if v:IsA("Model") then return v.Name ~= lp.Name end
					end
				end
			end
			return false
		end

		do
			local ok, mod = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Classes.WeaponComponent.Classes.Viewmodel)
			end)
			if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.render) == "function" then
				VMClass = mod
				origVMRender = mod.render
				pcall(function()
					if setreadonly then setreadonly(mod, false) end
					mod.render = function(self, ...)
						if tpOn:Get() and tpHideVM:Get() and not tpGun.on
							and not isSpectating() and not viewmodelIsOther(self) then
							local m = self.Model
							local mode = tpVMMode:Get()
							if mode == "Everything" then
								if next(armSaved) then pcall(restoreArms) end
								if m and m.Parent then pcall(m.PivotTo, m, VM_AWAY) end
								return
							end
							local r = origVMRender(self, ...)
							if m and m.Parent then pcall(hideArms, m) end
							return r
						end
						if next(armSaved) then pcall(restoreArms) end
						return origVMRender(self, ...)
					end
				end)
			else
				Window:Notify("Hide viewmodel: game Viewmodel class not found, using the fallback", 5)
			end
		end

		RunService:BindToRenderStep("TriggerThirdPersonReset", Enum.RenderPriority.First.Value, function()
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if cam and firstPersonCF and thirdPersonCF and cam.CFrame == thirdPersonCF then
				cam.CFrame = firstPersonCF
			end
		end)

		RunService:BindToRenderStep("TriggerThirdPerson", 3050, function()
			firstPersonCF, thirdPersonCF = nil, nil
			if not tpOn:Get() or isSpectating() then
				if next(revealed) or next(ltmSaved) then restoreCharacter() end
				if next(vmSaved) then restoreViewmodel() end
				if next(armSaved) then restoreArms() end
				return
			end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return end

			if tpShow:Get() then revealCharacter() elseif next(revealed) or next(ltmSaved) then restoreCharacter() end
			if VMClass then
				if next(vmSaved) then restoreViewmodel() end
			elseif tpHideVM:Get() then
				hideViewmodel()
			elseif next(vmSaved) then
				restoreViewmodel()
			end

			local cf = cam.CFrame
			local ignore = { cam }
			if lp.Character then ignore[#ignore + 1] = lp.Character end
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
			if mine then ignore[#ignore + 1] = mine end
			tpParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore

			local dist = tpDist:Get()
			local hit = workspace:Raycast(cf.Position, -cf.LookVector * dist, tpParams)
			local d = hit and math.max(hit.Distance - 0.4, 0) or dist

			firstPersonCF = cf
			thirdPersonCF = cf * CFrame.new(0, 0, d)
			cam.CFrame = thirdPersonCF
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── THIRD PERSON GUN (see your own gun on your character) ─────────────
	-- The game builds the third-person weapon (Characters > player > WeaponModel > Equipped) for every
	-- player EXCEPT you (its Observers.Players script returns early for LocalPlayer). So this uses the
	-- game's own CreateWeaponModel module to build it for you, from your CurrentEquipped / Slot
	-- attributes, and keeps it visible. Gun chams (Visuals > Viewmodel) are drawn on it too.
	do
		local gunOn = tpGun.toggle -- lives in the Third person section (Visuals)


		local SKIP = {
			MuzzlePart = true, MuzzlePartL = true, MuzzlePartR = true, RootPart = true,
			Hitbox = true, Insert = true, move = true, Silencer = true,
		}
		local saved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- part -> { Transparency, LocalTransparencyModifier }
		local list, listModel, lastScan = {}, nil, 0
		local hl = nil

		local function myCharacter()
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			return (chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)) or lp.Character
		end

		local function myEquipped()
			local roots = {}
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local mine = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
			if mine then roots[#roots + 1] = mine end
			if lp.Character then roots[#roots + 1] = lp.Character end
			for _, r in ipairs(roots) do
				local wm = r:FindFirstChild("WeaponModel")
				local eq = wm and wm:FindFirstChild("Equipped")
				if eq then return eq end
			end
			return nil
		end

		-- the game's own builder for third-person weapon models
		local HttpSvc = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local CWM, cwmErr
		do
			local ok, m = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Controllers.Observers.Players.Components.CreateWeaponModel)
			end)
			if ok and m then CWM = m else cwmErr = tostring(m) end
		end
		local builtSig, builtChar, lastBuild, building, noticed, onSince = nil, nil, 0, false, false, 0

		local function decodeAttr(name)
			local a = lp:GetAttribute(name)
			if typeof(a) ~= "string" then return nil end
			local ok, d = pcall(HttpSvc.JSONDecode, HttpSvc, a)
			if ok and type(d) == "table" then return d end
			return nil
		end

		local function clearBuilt()
			if CWM and builtSig then pcall(CWM.ClearPlayerCache, lp) end
			builtSig, builtChar = nil, nil
		end

		local function ensureWeapon(eq)
			if not CWM or building then return end
			local attr = lp:GetAttribute("CurrentEquipped")
			if typeof(attr) ~= "string" then
				if builtSig then clearBuilt() end
				return
			end
			local char = myCharacter()
			if not char or char:GetAttribute("Dead") == true then return end
			if attr == builtSig and char == builtChar and eq and eq.Parent then return end
			if os.clock() - lastBuild < 1 then return end
			lastBuild = os.clock()

			local data = decodeAttr("CurrentEquipped")
			if not data then return end
			local slots = {}
			for i = 1, 3 do slots[i] = decodeAttr("Slot" .. i) end

			building = true
			builtSig, builtChar = attr, char
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, err = pcall(CWM, lp, data, slots)
				if not ok then
					builtSig = nil
					warn("[GS] third person gun: " .. tostring(err))
				end
				building = false
			end)
		end

		local function restore()
			for part, o in pairs(saved) do
				if part.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						part.Transparency = o[1]
						part.LocalTransparencyModifier = o[2]
					end)
				end
			end
			table.clear(saved)
			table.clear(list)
			listModel = nil
			if hl then pcall(function() hl.Enabled = false end) end
		end

		RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSTPGun", 3051, function()
			if not gunOn:Get() or isSpectating() then
				if next(saved) or hl then restore() end
				if builtSig then clearBuilt() end
				onSince, noticed = 0, false
				return
			end
			if onSince == 0 then onSince = os.clock() end

			local eq = myEquipped()
			ensureWeapon(eq)
			if not eq then
				if next(saved) then restore() end
				if not noticed and os.clock() - onSince > 4 then
					noticed = true
					if not CWM then
						Window:Notify("Third person gun: game weapon builder not found", 5)
					elseif typeof(lp:GetAttribute("CurrentEquipped")) ~= "string" then
						Window:Notify("Third person gun: you have no weapon equipped", 5)
					else
						Window:Notify("Third person gun: could not build the weapon (see F9 console)", 5)
					end
				end
				return
			end
			noticed = false

			if eq ~= listModel or os.clock() - lastScan > 0.25 then
				lastScan = os.clock()
				listModel = eq
				table.clear(list)
				for _, d in ipairs(eq:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") and not string.find(string.lower(d.Name), "hitbox", 1, true) then
						if not SKIP[d.Name] then
							list[#list + 1] = d
						elseif d.Name == "Silencer" and d.Transparency < 0.99 then
							-- the game shows the silencer only when the weapon really is suppressed, so it is
							-- kept visible to you in that case (transparency is never forced on it)
							list[#list + 1] = d
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for _, part in ipairs(list) do
				if part.Parent then
					if not saved[part] then saved[part] = { part.Transparency, part.LocalTransparencyModifier } end
					-- fully transparent PLAIN parts are helpers (hitboxes / rigging, e.g. on the CT knife);
					-- un-hiding them shows a white slab, so only parts that carry a visual are revealed
					if part.Transparency >= 0.99 then
						if part:IsA("MeshPart") or part:IsA("UnionOperation")
							or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh") or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceAppearance")
							or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Texture") or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal") then
							part.Transparency = 0
						end
					end
					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end
			end

			-- gun chams (same toggle / colour / fill as the viewmodel chams)
			local ch = tpGun.chams
			if ch and ch.on:Get() and ch.part:Get() ~= "Arms" then
				if not hl or not hl.Parent then
					if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
					hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
					hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
					hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
					hl.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera or workspace
				end
				if hl.Adornee ~= eq then hl.Adornee = eq end
				hl.FillColor = ch.color:Get()
				hl.FillTransparency = 1 - ch.fill:Get() / 100
				hl.Enabled = true
			elseif hl then
				hl.Enabled = false
			end
		end)

		env.__gsTPGunCleanup = function()
			pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSTPGun") end)
			pcall(restore)
			pcall(clearBuilt)
			pcall(function() if hl then hl:Destroy() end end)
			hl = nil
		end
	end


	-- ───────────── EFFECTS (anti flash / smoke / fire) ─────────────
	local FlashEffect, SmokeFx, antiFlash, antiSmoke
	local lastSmokeSweep = 0
	local fireConn, fireSaved = nil, {}
	do
		local fxSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Effects", "Left")
		antiFlash = fxSec:AddToggle("Anti flash", false)
		antiSmoke = fxSec:AddToggle("Anti smoke", false)
		local antiFire = fxSec:AddToggle("Anti fire", false)

		local origFlash
		do
			local ok, mod = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Common.VFXLibary.FlashEffect)
			end)
			if ok and type(mod) == "table" then
				FlashEffect = mod
				origFlash = mod.Flash
				pcall(function()
					if setreadonly then setreadonly(mod, false) end
					mod.Flash = function(...)
						if antiFlash:Get() then return false end
						return origFlash(...)
					end
				end)
			else
				Window:Notify("Anti flash: FlashEffect module not found", 5)
			end
		end

		local origSmokeCreate
		do
			local ok, mod = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Common.VFXLibary.CreateVoxelSmoke)
			end)
			if ok and type(mod) == "table" then
				SmokeFx = mod
				origSmokeCreate = mod.Create
				pcall(function()
					if setreadonly then setreadonly(mod, false) end
					mod.Create = function(...)
						if antiSmoke:Get() then return end
						return origSmokeCreate(...)
					end
				end)
			else
				Window:Notify("Anti smoke: CreateVoxelSmoke module not found", 5)
			end
		end
		antiSmoke:OnChanged(function(on)
			if on and SmokeFx then pcall(SmokeFx.DestroyAll) end
		end)

		local FIRE_WORDS = { "fire", "flame", "molotov", "incendiary", "burn" }
		fireSaved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

		local function isFireEffect(d)
			if not (d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter")) then return false end
			if d:IsA("Fire") then return true end
			local n = string.lower(d.Name)
			local pn = d.Parent and string.lower(d.Parent.Name) or ""
			for _, w in ipairs(FIRE_WORDS) do
				if string.find(n, w, 1, true) or string.find(pn, w, 1, true) then return true end
			end
			return false
		end

		local function killFire(d)
			if isFireEffect(d) then
				if fireSaved[d] == nil then fireSaved[d] = d.Enabled end
				d.Enabled = false
			end
		end

		antiFire:OnChanged(function(on)
			if fireConn then fireConn:Disconnect() fireConn = nil end
			if on then
				for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do pcall(killFire, d) end
				fireConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
					task.defer(function() pcall(killFire, d) end)
				end)
			else
				for d, v in pairs(fireSaved) do
					if d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Enabled = v end) end
				end
				table.clear(fireSaved)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── LOW GFX (FPS boost) ─────────────
	do
		local perfSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Performance", "Left")
		local boardOn = perfSec:AddToggle("Low GFX", false)

		local savedMat = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local removed = {}
		local gen = 0
		local conn

		local function isMapPart(p)
			if p:IsA("Terrain") then return false end
			if camera and p:IsDescendantOf(camera) then return false end
			if p:IsDescendantOf(ghostFolder) then return false end
			if lp.Character and p:IsDescendantOf(lp.Character) then return false end
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			if chars and p:IsDescendantOf(chars) then return false end
			for _, n in ipairs({ "GSTracers", "Debris" }) do
				local f = workspace:FindFirstChild(n)
				if f and p:IsDescendantOf(f) then return false end
			end
			return true
		end

		local function process(d)
			if d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
				local p = d.Parent
				if p and p:IsA("BasePart") and isMapPart(p) then
					removed[#removed + 1] = { d, p }
					d.Parent = nil
				end
			elseif d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Transparency < 0.99 and isMapPart(d) then
				if savedMat[d] == nil then savedMat[d] = d.Material end
				d.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
			end
		end

		local function restore()
			gen += 1
			if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
			for part, mat in pairs(savedMat) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Material = mat end) end
			end
			table.clear(savedMat)
			for _, e in ipairs(removed) do
				if e[2].Parent then pcall(function() e[1].Parent = e[2] end) end
			end
			table.clear(removed)
		end
		env.__gsBoardRestore = restore

		boardOn:OnChanged(function(on)
			restore()
			if not on then return end
			local myGen = gen
			conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
				task.defer(function() pcall(process, d) end)
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				local n = 0
				for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
					if gen ~= myGen then return end
					pcall(process, d)
					n += 1
					if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
				end
				Window:Notify("Low GFX applied", 3)
			end)
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── WORLD: material / colour / textures ─────────────
	do
		local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("World", "Left")
		local matOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom map material", false)
		local matPick = sec:AddDropdown("Material", {
			"SmoothPlastic", "Plastic", "Neon", "ForceField", "Glass", "Metal", "Concrete", "Sand", "Grass", "Wood",
			"Cardboard",
		}, "SmoothPlastic")
		matPick:DependsOn(matOn, function(v) return v end)

		local colOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom map colour", false)
		local recolorHook
		local colPick = colOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120), false, function()
			if recolorHook then recolorHook() end
		end)

		local texOn = sec:AddToggle("Remove map textures", false)
		local transPick = sec:AddSlider("Map transparency", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)

		local savedMat, savedCol, removedTex, savedTrans, savedVar = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
		local gen = 0
		local scanConn

		local function isMapPart(p)
			if p:IsA("Terrain") then return false end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if cam and p:IsDescendantOf(cam) then return false end
			if p:IsDescendantOf(ghostFolder) then return false end
			if lp.Character and p:IsDescendantOf(lp.Character) then return false end
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			if chars and p:IsDescendantOf(chars) then return false end
			for _, n in ipairs({ "GSTracers", "Debris", "GSChams", "GSESP", "GSImpacts" }) do
				local f = workspace:FindFirstChild(n)
				if f and p:IsDescendantOf(f) then return false end
			end
			return true
		end

		local function applyPart(d)
			if d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
				-- a custom material / colour only shows once the SurfaceAppearance on top of it is gone
				local strip = texOn:Get() or ((matOn:Get() or colOn:Get()) and d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance"))
				if strip then
					local p = d.Parent
					if p and p:IsA("BasePart") and isMapPart(p) and removedTex[d] == nil then
						removedTex[d] = p
						d.Parent = nil
					end
				end
			elseif d:IsA("BasePart") and isMapPart(d) then
				if matOn:Get() then
					if savedMat[d] == nil then savedMat[d] = d.Material end
					local mat = Enum.Material[matPick:Get()]
					if mat then d.Material = mat end
					-- a MaterialVariant (the old wood / brick look) overrides the material, so drop it
					if d.MaterialVariant ~= "" then
						if savedVar[d] == nil then savedVar[d] = d.MaterialVariant end
						d.MaterialVariant = ""
					end
				end
				if colOn:Get() then
					if savedCol[d] == nil then savedCol[d] = d.Color end
					d.Color = colPick:Get()
				end
				local tv = transPick:Get()
				if tv > 0 then
					if savedTrans[d] == nil then savedTrans[d] = d.Transparency end
					local o = savedTrans[d]
					if o < 0.99 then d.Transparency = o + (1 - o) * tv / 100 end
				end
			end
		end

		local function restore()
			gen += 1
			if scanConn then scanConn:Disconnect() scanConn = nil end
			for part, mat in pairs(savedMat) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Material = mat end) end
			end
			table.clear(savedMat)
			for part, col in pairs(savedCol) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Color = col end) end
			end
			table.clear(savedCol)
			for part, t in pairs(savedTrans) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Transparency = t end) end
			end
			table.clear(savedTrans)
			for part, v in pairs(savedVar) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.MaterialVariant = v end) end
			end
			table.clear(savedVar)
			for tex, parent in pairs(removedTex) do
				if parent.Parent then pcall(function() tex.Parent = parent end) end
			end
			table.clear(removedTex)
		end

		local function rescan()
			restore()
			if not (matOn:Get() or colOn:Get() or texOn:Get() or transPick:Get() > 0) then return end
			local myGen = gen
			scanConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
				task.defer(function() if gen == myGen then pcall(applyPart, d) end end)
			end)
			task.spawn(function()
				local n = 0
				for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
					if gen ~= myGen then return end
					pcall(applyPart, d)
					n += 1
					if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end
				end
			end)
		end

		matOn:OnChanged(rescan)
		matPick:OnChanged(function() if matOn:Get() then rescan() end end)
		colOn:OnChanged(rescan)
		-- the colour updates live on every part already touched; nothing is restored or rescanned
		recolorHook = function()
			if not colOn:Get() then return end
			if next(savedCol) == nil then rescan() return end
			local c = colPick:Get()
			for part in pairs(savedCol) do
				if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.Color = c end) end
			end
		end
		texOn:OnChanged(rescan)
		transPick:OnChanged(rescan)

		env.__gsWorldCleanup = function()
			pcall(restore)
		end
	end

	-- ───────────── LIGHTING ─────────────
	do (function()
			local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

			local sec = VisualTab:AddSection("Lighting", "Left")
			local timeOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom time", false)
			local timeVal = sec:AddSlider("Clock time", 0, 24, 12, nil, "h", 0.1)
			local brightOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom brightness", false)
			local brightVal = sec:AddSlider("Brightness", 0, 10, 2, nil, "", 0.1)
			local atmOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom atmosphere", false)
			local atmColor = atmOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(199, 199, 199), false)
			local atmDecay = atmOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(106, 112, 125), false)
			local atmDensity = sec:AddSlider("Density", 0, 100, 35, nil, "%", 1)
			local atmOffset = sec:AddSlider("Offset", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 1)
			local atmGlare = sec:AddSlider("Glare", 0, 10, 0, nil, "", 0.1)
			local atmHaze = sec:AddSlider("Haze", 0, 10, 0, nil, "", 0.1)
			local skyOn = sec:AddToggle("Custom skybox", false)
			local skyId = sec:AddTextbox("Skybox ID", "")

			local savedTime, savedBright
			timeOn:OnChanged(function(on)
				if on then
					savedTime = savedTime or Lighting.ClockTime
				elseif savedTime then
					Lighting.ClockTime = savedTime
					savedTime = nil
				end
			end)
			brightOn:OnChanged(function(on)
				if on then
					savedBright = savedBright or Lighting.Brightness
				elseif savedBright then
					Lighting.Brightness = savedBright
					savedBright = nil
				end
			end)

			local atm, atmMade, atmOrig
			local function atmGet()
				if atm and atm.Parent then return atm end
				atm = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
				if atm then
					atmMade = false
					atmOrig = {
						Color = atm.Color, Decay = atm.Decay, Density = atm.Density,
						Offset = atm.Offset, Glare = atm.Glare, Haze = atm.Haze,
					}
				else
					atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
					atm.Name = "GSAtmosphere"
					atm.Parent = Lighting
					atmMade = true
				end
				return atm
			end
			local function atmRestore()
				if atm and atm.Parent then
					if atmMade then
						atm:Destroy()
					elseif atmOrig then
						for k, v in pairs(atmOrig) do pcall(function() atm[k] = v end) end
					end
				end
				atm, atmOrig, atmMade = nil, nil, nil
			end

			local FACES = { "SkyboxBk", "SkyboxDn", "SkyboxFt", "SkyboxLf", "SkyboxRt", "SkyboxUp" }
			local skySaved, skyMade, skyApplied = {}, nil, nil

			local function assetOf(text)
				text = (tostring(text or ""):gsub("%s", ""))
				if text == "" then return nil end
				if tonumber(text) then return "rbxassetid://" .. text end
				if text:find("rbxassetid://", 1, true) or text:find("http", 1, true) then return text end
				return nil
			end
			local function skyApply(asset)
				local skies = {}
				for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("Sky") then skies[#skies + 1] = c end
				end
				if #skies == 0 then
					skyMade = Instance.new("Sky")
					skyMade.Name = "GSSky"
					skyMade.Parent = Lighting
					skies[1] = skyMade
				end
				for _, s in ipairs(skies) do
					if not skySaved[s] then
						local t = {}
						for _, f in ipairs(FACES) do t[f] = s[f] end
						skySaved[s] = t
					end
					for _, f in ipairs(FACES) do s[f] = asset end
				end
				skyApplied = asset
			end
			local function skyRestore()
				for s, t in pairs(skySaved) do
					if s.Parent then
						for f, v in pairs(t) do pcall(function() s[f] = v end) end
					end
				end
				table.clear(skySaved)
				if skyMade then pcall(function() skyMade:Destroy() end) skyMade = nil end
				skyApplied = nil
			end

			local lastSky = 0
			local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if timeOn:Get() then Lighting.ClockTime = timeVal:Get() end
				if brightOn:Get() then Lighting.Brightness = brightVal:Get() end

				if atmOn:Get() then
					local a = atmGet()
					local c1 = atmColor:Get()
					local c2 = atmDecay:Get()
					a.Color = c1
					a.Decay = c2
					a.Density = atmDensity:Get() / 100
					a.Offset = atmOffset:Get() / 100
					a.Glare = atmGlare:Get()
					a.Haze = atmHaze:Get()
				elseif atm then
					atmRestore()
				end

				if os.clock() - lastSky > 0.3 then
					lastSky = os.clock()
					local asset = skyOn:Get() and assetOf(skyId:Get()) or nil
					if asset then
						if asset ~= skyApplied then skyApply(asset) end
					elseif skyApplied then
						skyRestore()
					end
				end
			end)

			env.__gsLightCleanup = function()
				pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
				pcall(function() if savedTime then Lighting.ClockTime = savedTime end end)
				pcall(function() if savedBright then Lighting.Brightness = savedBright end end)
				pcall(atmRestore)
				pcall(skyRestore)
			end
		end)() end

	-- ───────────── UTILITY ─────────────
	do
		local utilSec = MiscTab:AddSection("Utility", "Left")
		utilSec:AddButton("Rejoin", function()
			game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, lp)
		end)
		utilSec:AddButton("Copy game id", function()
			if setclipboard then
				setclipboard(tostring(game.PlaceId))
				Window:Notify("Copied place id")
			else
				Window:Notify("Failed: your executor has no setclipboard")
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── MOVEMENT (bunnyhop) + ANTI AIM ─────────────
	local CharClass, origSampleInput, jumpBeganConn, jumpEndedConn, bhopConn, bhopDbg, activeCfg
	do
		local moveSec = MiscTab:AddSection("Movement", "Left")
		local bhopOn = moveSec:AddToggle("Bunnyhop", false)
		addHotkey(bhopOn, bhopOn:AddKeybind(nil, "Toggle"))
		local bhopStrafe = moveSec:AddDropdown("Strafe Methods", { "Static", "Camera turn", "Off" }, "Static")
		local bhopWall = moveSec:AddToggle("Wall avoid (keep speed)", true)
		local bhopWallReach = moveSec:AddSlider("Wall look-ahead", 0.05, 0.4, 0.16, nil, " s", 0.01)
		bhopWallReach:DependsOn(bhopWall, function(v) return v end)

		do
			local ok, mod = pcall(function()
				return require(ReplicatedStorage.Classes.Character)
			end)
			if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.SampleInput) == "function" then
				CharClass = mod
				origSampleInput = mod.SampleInput
			else
				Window:Notify("Bunnyhop: game Character class not found, using key fallback", 5)
			end
		end

		local jumpHeld = false
		local pulsing = false
		local lastHop = 0

		jumpBeganConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space and not pulsing then jumpHeld = true end
		end)
		jumpEndedConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space and not pulsing then jumpHeld = false end
		end)

		local lastStrafeYaw = nil
		bhopDbg = { speed = 0, peak = 0, ground = false, stamina = 0 }

		local SimCfg, SimHz, SimConfigMod = nil, 64, nil
		pcall(function() SimConfigMod = require(ReplicatedStorage.MovementV2.Simulation.Config) end)
		pcall(function() SimCfg = SimConfigMod.Default end)
		pcall(function() SimHz = require(ReplicatedStorage.MovementV2.Config).DefaultSimulationHz or 64 end)

		activeCfg = function()
			if not SimConfigMod then return SimCfg end
			local gm = workspace:GetAttribute("ServerGamemode") or workspace:GetAttribute("Gamemode")
			local ok, cfg = pcall(SimConfigMod.resolveServerGamemode, gm, SimHz)
			if ok and type(cfg) == "table" then return cfg end
			return SimCfg
		end

		-- Wall avoid: hitting a wall head-on kills speed (the game clips your velocity). A few short rays are
		-- cast along the flight direction; if you are about to push INTO a wall, the movement input is slid
		-- along the wall instead, so you scrape past it and keep your speed.
		local wallParams = RaycastParams.new()
		wallParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		local function avoidWall(out, st)
			local gv = st and st.Velocity
			if not gv or typeof(out.Move) ~= "Vector2" or type(out.LookYaw) ~= "number" then return end
			local vel = Vector3.new(gv.X, 0, gv.Z)
			local sp = vel.Magnitude
			if sp < 8 then return end
			local mine = pickMyModel()
			local root = mine and mine:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not root then return end

			local ignore = {}
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			if chars then ignore[#ignore + 1] = chars end
			if lp.Character then ignore[#ignore + 1] = lp.Character end
			if workspace.CurrentCamera then ignore[#ignore + 1] = workspace.CurrentCamera end
			wallParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore

			local dir = vel / sp
			local side = dir:Cross(Vector3.yAxis)
			local reach = math.clamp(sp * bhopWallReach:Get(), 2.5, 12)
			local bestN, bestD
			for _, off in ipairs({ 0, 1.1, -1.1 }) do
				local r = workspace:Raycast(root.Position + side * off, dir * reach, wallParams)
				if r and r.Instance.CanCollide and math.abs(r.Normal.Y) < 0.6 then
					if not bestD or r.Distance < bestD then
						bestD = r.Distance
						bestN = Vector3.new(r.Normal.X, 0, r.Normal.Z)
					end
				end
			end
			if not bestN or bestN.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
			bestN = bestN.Unit

			local frame = CFrame.Angles(0, out.LookYaw, 0)
			local intent = frame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(out.Move.X, 0, out.Move.Y))
			if intent.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
			intent = intent.Unit
			local into = intent:Dot(bestN)
			if into >= 0 then return end -- already moving away from / along the wall

			local slide = intent - bestN * into
			if slide.Magnitude < 0.2 then slide = dir - bestN * dir:Dot(bestN) end -- head-on: follow the wall
			if slide.Magnitude < 0.05 then return end
			local o = frame:VectorToObjectSpace(slide.Unit)
			out.Move = Vector2.new(o.X, o.Z)
		end

		local function staticMove(self, out, st)
			local cfg = activeCfg()
			if not cfg then return end
			local gv = st and st.Velocity
			if not st or not gv then return end

			local m, yaw = out.Move, out.LookYaw
			local frame = CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
			local intent = frame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(m.X, 0, m.Y))
			if intent.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
			intent = intent.Unit

			local vel = Vector3.new(gv.X, 0, gv.Z)
			local speed = vel.Magnitude
			if speed < 0.5 then return end
			local velDir = vel / speed

			local stamina = math.clamp((st.Stamina or cfg.StaminaMax) / math.max(cfg.StaminaMax, 1), 0, 1)
			local base = cfg.MaxSpeed
			if type(st.BaseMoveSpeed) == "number" then
				base = math.clamp(st.BaseMoveSpeed * (type(st.VelocityModifier) == "number" and st.VelocityModifier or 1), 0, cfg.MaxBaseMoveSpeed)
			end
			local wishSpeed = base * stamina * stamina
			local accel = cfg.AirAccelerate * wishSpeed * (1 / SimHz) * (st.GroundSurfaceFriction or 1)
			local p = math.max(0, math.min(wishSpeed, cfg.AirSpeedCap) - accel)
			local theta = math.acos(math.clamp(p / speed, -1, 1))

			local cy = velDir.Z * intent.X - velDir.X * intent.Z
			local sign = cy >= 0 and 1 or -1

			if math.abs(cy) < 0.1 and lastStrafeYaw then
				local d = math.atan2(math.sin(yaw - lastStrafeYaw), math.cos(yaw - lastStrafeYaw))
				if math.abs(d) > 0.0002 then
					sign = d < 0 and -1 or 1
				end
			end

			local dir = CFrame.Angles(0, sign * theta, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(velDir)
			local o = frame:VectorToObjectSpace(dir)
			out.Move = Vector2.new(o.X, o.Z)
		end

		-- anti aim
		local aaSec = RageTab:AddSection("Anti aim", "Right")
		local aaOn = aaSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
		addHotkey(aaOn, aaOn:AddKeybind(nil, "Toggle"))
		local aaPitch = aaSec:AddDropdown("Pitch", { "Off", "Up", "Down", "Zero", "Jitter", "Random", "Custom" }, "Down")
		local aaPitchAmt = aaSec:AddSlider("Custom pitch", -90, 90, 0, nil, "°", 1)
		local aaBase = aaSec:AddDropdown("Yaw base", { "Local view", "At targets" }, "Local view")
		local aaYawMode = aaSec:AddDropdown("Yaw", { "Off", "Static", "Spin", "Sway", "Random" }, "Static")
		local aaYawBase = aaSec:AddSlider("Yaw offset", -180, 180, 180, nil, "°", 5)
		local aaSpin = aaSec:AddSlider("Spin speed", 1, 180, 90, nil, "°/tick", 1)
		local aaSway = aaSec:AddSlider("Sway range", 10, 180, 70, nil, "°", 5)
		local aaSwayT, aaStep3, aaPitchFlip = 0, 0, false
		local aaJitterMode = aaSec:AddDropdown("Yaw jitter", { "Off", "Offset", "Center", "3-way", "Random" }, "Off")
		local aaJitter = aaSec:AddSlider("Jitter range", 0, 180, 60, nil, "°", 5)
		local aaPause = aaSec:AddToggle("Pause while shooting", false)

		--peek
		local peekSet = aaSec:AddToggle("Peek assist", false)
		local peekOn = aaSec:AddToggle("Peek key", false)
		addHotkey(peekOn, peekOn:AddKeybind(nil, "Hold"))
		local peekCol = aaSec:AddLabel("Peek anchor colour"):AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
		local peekShow = aaSec:AddToggle("Show peek anchor", true)
		local peekMat = aaSec:AddDropdown("Anchor material", { "ForceField", "Neon", "Highlight" }, "ForceField")
		local peekSize = aaSec:AddSlider("Anchor size", 1, 12, 4, nil, " studs", 0.5)
		local peekHeight = aaSec:AddSlider("Anchor thickness", 0.02, 8, 0.06, nil, " studs", 0.02)
		local peekTrans = aaSec:AddSlider("Anchor transparency", 0, 100, 10, nil, "%", 5)
		local peekFill = aaSec:AddSlider("Anchor chams fill", 0, 100, 100, nil, "%", 5)

		for _, c in ipairs({ peekMat, peekSize, peekHeight, peekTrans }) do
			c:DependsOn(peekShow, function(v) return v end)
		end

		peekFill:DependsOn(peekMat, function(v) return v == "Highlight" end)
