		aaPitchAmt:DependsOn(aaPitch, function(v) return v == "Custom" end)
		aaSpin:DependsOn(aaYawMode, function(v) return v == "Spin" end)
		aaJitter:DependsOn(aaJitterMode, function(v) return v ~= "Off" end)

		local aaFlip, aaSpinAngle, aaPitchWarned = false, 0, false
		local aaSentPitch = nil
		local atYaw, atAt = nil, 0

		-- yaw (in the game's LookYaw convention) that faces the nearest enemy
		local function nearestEnemyYaw()
			local mine = pickMyModel()
			local root = mine and mine:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local from = root and root.Position
			local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			if not (from and folder) then return nil end

			local mineTeam = myTeam()
			local bestPos, bestD

			local function consider(m)
				if not m:IsA("Model") or m.Name == lp.Name then return end
				if m:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then return end
				if isCharacterDead(m) then return end
				local theirs = armorTeam(m) or backupTeam(m, playerFromCharacter(m))
				if mineTeam and theirs and theirs == mineTeam then return end
				local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not r then return end
				local d = (r.Position - from).Magnitude
				if not bestD or d < bestD then bestD, bestPos = d, r.Position end
			end

			for _, c in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("Folder") then
					for _, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do consider(m) end
				else
					consider(c)
				end
			end

			if not bestPos then return nil end
			local dx, dz = bestPos.X - from.X, bestPos.Z - from.Z
			if dx * dx + dz * dz < 0.01 then return nil end
			return math.atan2(-dx, -dz)
		end

		-- auto shoot: look straight at the validated aim point so the server sees the same direction
		local function applyAutoFace(out)
			local sa = dbg.silentAuto
			local ap = dbg.autoPick
			if not (sa and sa.face and sa.face:Get() and sa.on:Get() and sa.enabled:Get() and ap) then return end
			if os.clock() - ap.t > 0.2 or not (ap.part and ap.part.Parent) or typeof(ap.pos) ~= "Vector3" then return end
			if type(out) ~= "table" or type(out.LookYaw) ~= "number" then return end
			local eye = firstPersonCF and firstPersonCF.Position or workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
			local v = ap.pos - eye
			if v.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
			local dir = v.Unit
			local yaw = math.atan2(-dir.X, -dir.Z)
			local d = yaw - out.LookYaw
			out.LookYaw = yaw
			if type(out.VerticalLook) == "number" then out.VerticalLook = dir.Y end
			local m = out.Move
			if typeof(m) == "Vector2" then
				local o = CFrame.Angles(0, -d, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(m.X, 0, m.Y))
				out.Move = Vector2.new(o.X, o.Z)
			end
		end

		local function applyAntiAim(out)
			aaSentPitch = nil
			if type(out) ~= "table" or type(out.LookYaw) ~= "number" then return end

			-- shots may use the same look direction, so keep it real while you fire
			if aaPause:Get() and (UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
				or os.clock() - dbg.lastClick < 0.25) then
				return
			end

			-- ── yaw ──
			local mode = aaYawMode:Get()
			if mode ~= "Off" then
				local base = out.LookYaw
				if aaBase:Get() == "At targets" then
					if os.clock() - atAt > 0.1 then
						atAt = os.clock()
						atYaw = nearestEnemyYaw()
					end
					if atYaw then base = atYaw end
				end

				local off = math.rad(aaYawBase:Get())
				if mode == "Spin" then
					aaSpinAngle = (aaSpinAngle + aaSpin:Get()) % 360
					off = off + math.rad(aaSpinAngle)
				elseif mode == "Sway" then
					aaSwayT = aaSwayT + aaSpin:Get() * 0.06
					off = off + math.sin(aaSwayT) * math.rad(aaSway:Get())
				elseif mode == "Random" then
					off = math.rad(math.random(-180, 180))
				end

				local jm = aaJitterMode:Get()
				if jm ~= "Off" then
					aaFlip = not aaFlip
					local r = math.rad(aaJitter:Get())
					if jm == "Offset" then
						off = off + (aaFlip and r or 0)
					elseif jm == "Center" then
						off = off + (aaFlip and r or -r) / 2
					elseif jm == "3-way" then
						aaStep3 = (aaStep3 % 3) + 1
						off = off + (aaStep3 == 1 and -r or (aaStep3 == 2 and 0 or r))
					else
						off = off + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * r
					end
				end

				local d = (base + off) - out.LookYaw
				out.LookYaw = out.LookYaw + d

				-- keep the walking direction the same in the world
				local m = out.Move
				if typeof(m) == "Vector2" then
					local o = CFrame.Angles(0, -d, 0):VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(m.X, 0, m.Y))
					out.Move = Vector2.new(o.X, o.Z)
				end
			end

			-- ── pitch (VerticalLook = look direction Y, -1 down ... 1 up) ──
			local pm = aaPitch:Get()
			if pm ~= "Off" then
				if type(out.VerticalLook) == "number" then
					local v
					if pm == "Up" then v = 1
					elseif pm == "Down" then v = -1
					elseif pm == "Zero" then v = 0
					elseif pm == "Jitter" then
						aaPitchFlip = not aaPitchFlip
						v = aaPitchFlip and 1 or -1
					elseif pm == "Random" then v = math.random() * 2 - 1
					else v = math.sin(math.rad(aaPitchAmt:Get())) end
					out.VerticalLook = v
					aaSentPitch = v
				elseif not aaPitchWarned then
					aaPitchWarned = true
					Window:Notify("Anti aim: no VerticalLook in the game's input, pitch is not available", 5)
				end
			end
		end

		-- ── peek assist: TAP the key = drop the anchor here, HOLD the key = walk back to the anchor ──
		local peek = { fixed = nil, setWas = false, wasActive = false, pressT = nil }
		local PEEK_TAP = 0.25 -- seconds: a press shorter than this is a tap

		local function peekRoot()
			local m = pickMyModel()
			return m and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end

		local function applyPeek(out)
			if type(out) ~= "table" or type(out.LookYaw) ~= "number" or typeof(out.Move) ~= "Vector2" then return end
			local active = peekOn:Get()
			local root = peekRoot()
			if not root then
				peek.wasActive, peek.pressT = active, nil
				return
			end
			local pos = root.Position

			-- key edges (this runs every input tick, so release is always seen)
			if active and not peek.wasActive then
				peek.pressT = os.clock()
			elseif not active and peek.wasActive then
				if peek.pressT and os.clock() - peek.pressT < PEEK_TAP then
					peek.fixed = pos -- tap: anchor goes where you stand
					peekSet:Set(true)
				end
				peek.pressT = nil
			end
			peek.wasActive = active

			-- the checkbox mirrors the anchor (ticking by hand drops one here, unticking removes it)
			local setOn = peekSet:Get()
			if setOn and not peek.fixed then
				peek.fixed = pos
			elseif not setOn then
				peek.fixed = nil
			end
			peek.setWas = setOn

			-- hold: walk back to the anchor
			if active and peek.fixed and peek.pressT and os.clock() - peek.pressT >= PEEK_TAP then
				local flat = Vector3.new(peek.fixed.X - pos.X, 0, peek.fixed.Z - pos.Z)
				if flat.Magnitude > 0.4 then
					local o = CFrame.Angles(0, out.LookYaw, 0):VectorToObjectSpace(flat.Unit)
					out.Move = Vector2.new(o.X, o.Z)
				else
					out.Move = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				end
			end
		end

		-- ── peek anchor visuals ──
		local peekFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
		peekFolder.Name = "GSPeek"
		peekFolder.Parent = workspace

		local anchor = nil -- { part, hl }

		local function destroyAnchor()
			if anchor then
				pcall(function() anchor.part:Destroy() end)
				pcall(function() if anchor.hl then anchor.hl:Destroy() end end)
				anchor = nil
			end
		end

		-- the anchor is a plain cylinder Part (a flat circle): no mesh, no decal
		local function buildAnchor()
			local part = Instance.new("Part")
			part.Name = "PeekAnchor"
			part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
			part.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
			part.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
			part.Anchored = true
			part.CanCollide = false
			part.CanQuery = false
			part.CanTouch = false
			part.CastShadow = false
			part.Locked = true
			part.Parent = peekFolder
			return { part = part }
		end

		local function updateAnchor()
			local origin = peek.fixed
			if not (origin and peekShow:Get()) then
				if anchor then destroyAnchor() end
				return
			end
			if not anchor or not anchor.part.Parent then
				destroyAnchor()
				anchor = buildAnchor()
			end

			local part = anchor.part
			local d, h = peekSize:Get(), peekHeight:Get()

			-- a Cylinder part's own axis is X: X = thickness, Y / Z = diameter, rotated so it lies flat
			part.Size = Vector3.new(h, d, d)
			local rot = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))

			-- sits on the floor under where you started peeking
			local ground = origin - Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
			part.CFrame = CFrame.new(ground + Vector3.new(0, h / 2, 0)) * rot

			-- look
			local mode = peekMat:Get()
			local col = peekCol:Get()
			part.Color = col
			part.Reflectance = 0
			part.Transparency = peekTrans:Get() / 100
			if mode == "Neon" then
				part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
			elseif mode == "ForceField" then
				part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
			else
				part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
			end

			-- Highlight = same chams look as the ESP (also shows through walls)
			if mode == "Highlight" then
				if not anchor.hl or not anchor.hl.Parent then
					if anchor.hl then pcall(function() anchor.hl:Destroy() end) end
					local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
					hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
					hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
					hl.Parent = peekFolder
					anchor.hl = hl
				end
				anchor.hl.Adornee = part
				anchor.hl.FillColor = col
				anchor.hl.FillTransparency = 1 - peekFill:Get() / 100
				anchor.hl.OutlineTransparency = 1
				anchor.hl.Enabled = true
			elseif anchor.hl then
				anchor.hl.Enabled = false
			end
		end

		local peekConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(updateAnchor)
			if not ok and err ~= peek.lastErr then
				peek.lastErr = err
				warn("[Peek anchor] " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)

		env.__gsPeekCleanup = function()
			pcall(function() peekConn:Disconnect() end)
			pcall(destroyAnchor)
			pcall(function() peekFolder:Destroy() end)
		end

		if CharClass then
			pcall(function()
				if setreadonly then setreadonly(CharClass, false) end
				CharClass.SampleInput = function(self, ...)
					local active = bhopOn:Get() and jumpHeld and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
					local ctx = ...
					local st = (type(ctx) == "table" and ctx.State) or self.MovementState
					local onGround = st and st.OnGround
					if onGround == nil then onGround = self.OnGround end

					if active then
						if onGround then
							self.JumpInputDown = false
							self.JumpPulsePending = true
							self.JumpPulseNeedsRelease = false
						else
							self.JumpInputDown = false
							self.JumpPulsePending = false
						end
					end

					local out = origSampleInput(self, ...)

					do
						local gv = st and st.Velocity
						if gv then
							local sp = Vector3.new(gv.X, 0, gv.Z).Magnitude
							bhopDbg.speed = sp
							bhopDbg.peak = active and math.max(bhopDbg.peak, sp) or sp
						end
						bhopDbg.ground = onGround
						bhopDbg.stamina = (st and st.Stamina) or 0
					end

					if out then
						local yaw = out.LookYaw
						local mode = bhopStrafe:Get()
						if active and mode ~= "Off" and yaw
							and not onGround
							and st and st.MovementMode == "Walking" then
							if mode == "Static" then
								staticMove(self, out, st)
							elseif lastStrafeYaw and math.abs(out.Move.X) <= 0.2 then
								local d = math.atan2(math.sin(yaw - lastStrafeYaw), math.cos(yaw - lastStrafeYaw))
								if math.abs(d) > 0.00017 then
									out.Move = Vector2.new(d < 0 and 1 or -1, out.Move.Y)
								end
							end
						end
						lastStrafeYaw = yaw

						if active and bhopWall:Get() and not onGround
							and st and st.MovementMode == "Walking" then
							pcall(avoidWall, out, st)
						end
					end

					if out then pcall(applyPeek, out) end
					if out and aaOn:Get() then pcall(applyAntiAim, out) else aaSentPitch = nil end
					if out then pcall(applyAutoFace, out) end

					return out
				end
			end)
		end

		local CharacterPose, ownJoints, ownJointsChar
		pcall(function() CharacterPose = require(ReplicatedStorage.Components.Common.CharacterPose) end)
		RunService:BindToRenderStep("TriggerOwnHead", 3060, function()
			if not aaSentPitch or not CharacterPose then return end
			pcall(function()
				local char = lp.Character
				if not char then return end
				if ownJointsChar ~= char then
					ownJoints = CharacterPose.getJoints(char)
					ownJointsChar = char
				end
				if ownJoints then CharacterPose.applyVerticalLook(ownJoints, aaSentPitch, 0.003) end
			end)
		end)

		-- ── bunnyhop fallback (only used if the Character class could not be hooked) ──
		local function pulseSpace()
			if pulsing then return end
			pulsing = true
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(function() VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game) end)
				task.wait(0.045)
				pcall(function() VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game) end)
				task.wait(0.05)
				pulsing = false
			end)
		end

		local function getChar()
			local c = lp.Character
			if c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return c end
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local m = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)
			if m and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return m end
			return nil
		end

		local groundParams = RaycastParams.new()
		groundParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

		local function isGrounded(char, hrp)
			local reach = 4
			local foot = char:FindFirstChild("LeftFoot") or char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
			if foot then
				reach = (hrp.Position.Y - (foot.Position.Y - foot.Size.Y / 2)) + 0.6
			end
			groundParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char, camera }
			return workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0, -reach, 0), groundParams) ~= nil
		end

		bhopConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if CharClass then return end
			if not bhopOn:Get() or not jumpHeld then return end
			if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end

			local char = getChar()
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not hrp then return end

			if os.clock() - lastHop > 0.12 and isGrounded(char, hrp) then
				lastHop = os.clock()
				pulseSpace()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ───────────── HUD: crosshair + scope lines + enemy arrows + hitmarker ─────────────
	do (function()
			local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
			local SQ = math.sqrt(2)

			local dotGui2 = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			dotGui2.Name = "TriggerDotHud"
			dotGui2.ResetOnSpawn = false
			dotGui2.IgnoreGuiInset = true
			dotGui2.DisplayOrder = 997
			do
				local ok, hui = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
				dotGui2.Parent = (ok and hui) or CoreGui
			end
			env.__gsHudGui = dotGui2

			local dotSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Crosshair", "Right")
			local dotOn = dotSec:AddToggle("Custom crosshair", false)
			local dotSize = dotSec:AddSlider("Center dot", 1, 8, 2, nil, "px", 1)

			local scSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Scope lines", "Right")
			local scOn = scSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
			local scColor = scOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
			local scSniperOnly = scSec:AddToggle("Only sniper weapons", false)
			local keepGun = scSec:AddToggle("Keep gun visible when scoped", false)
			local keepSens = scSec:AddToggle("Keep sensitivity when scoped", false)
			local scHideNative = scSec:AddToggle("Hide game scope", false)
			local scLen = scSec:AddSlider("Line length", 20, 600, 140, nil, "px", 10)
			local scGap = scSec:AddSlider("Gap", 0, 200, 40, nil, "px", 5)
			local scThick = scSec:AddSlider("Thickness", 1, 3, 1, nil, "px", 1)
			local scTrans = scSec:AddSlider("Transparency", 0, 100, 0, nil, "%", 5)

			local arSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Enemy arrows", "Right")
			local arOn = arSec:AddToggle("Enabled", false)
			local arColor = arOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
			local arRadius = arSec:AddSlider("Distance from crosshair", 15, 150, 40, nil, "px", 1)
			local arSize = arSec:AddSlider("Size", 5, 150, 9, nil, "px", 1)
			local arDist = arSec:AddSlider("Max distance", 50, 2000, 1000, nil, " studs", 50)
			local arClosest = arSec:AddToggle("Closest enemy only", false)
			local arTeam = arSec:AddToggle("Team check", false)
			local arOffOnly = arSec:AddToggle("Only when off screen", false)

			local hmSec = VisualTab:AddSection("Hitmarker", "Right")
			local hmOn = hmSec:AddToggle("Hitmarker", false)
			local hmColor = hmOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
			local hmSize = hmSec:AddSlider("Size", 2, 20, 6, nil, "px", 1)
			local hmGap = hmSec:AddSlider("Gap", 0, 12, 3, nil, "px", 1)
			local hmThick = hmSec:AddSlider("Thickness", 1, 4, 1, nil, "px", 1)
			local hmOutline = hmSec:AddSlider("Outline", 0, 3, 1, nil, "px", 1)
			local hmTime = hmSec:AddSlider("Duration", 0.1, 1.5, 0.5, nil, "s", 0.05)

			local kmOn = hmSec:AddToggle("Kill marker", false)
			local kmColor = kmOn:AddColorPicker(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), false)
			local kmSize = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker size", 6, 40, 16, nil, "px", 1)
			local kmGap = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker gap", 0, 30, 6, nil, "px", 1)
			local kmOutline = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker outline", 0, 4, 1, nil, "px", 1)
			local kmThick = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker thickness", 1, 6, 2, nil, "px", 1)
			local kmSpin = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker spin", 0, 720, 120, nil, "°/s", 10)
			local kmTime = hmSec:AddSlider("Kill marker time", 0.3, 3, 1.2, nil, "s", 0.1)
			kmSize:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)
			kmGap:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)
			kmOutline:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)
			kmThick:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)
			kmSpin:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)
			kmTime:DependsOn(kmOn, function(v) return v end)

			-- bar: used by the hitmarker (keeps its outline)
			local function bar(parent, rot)
				local f = Instance.new("Frame")
				f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				f.BorderSizePixel = 0
				f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				f.Rotation = rot or 0
				f.Visible = false
				f.Parent = parent
				local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
				s.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
				s.Thickness = 0
				s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
				s.Parent = f
				return f
			end

			-- corner: used by the center dot and scope lines (no outline)
			local function corner(parent)
				local f = Instance.new("Frame")
				f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
				f.BorderSizePixel = 0
				f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				f.Visible = false
				f.Parent = parent
				return f
			end

			local xhDot = corner(dotGui2)
			local scF = { R = corner(dotGui2), L = corner(dotGui2), U = corner(dotGui2), D = corner(dotGui2) }
			-- HvH style: each line is opaque at the centre and fades to nothing at the far end
			local scGrad = {}
			for k, f in pairs(scF) do
				local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
				g.Parent = f
				scGrad[k] = g
			end
			local SC_ROT = { R = 0, L = 180, D = 90, U = 270 }
			local SC_FADE = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
			local SC_SOLID = NumberSequence.new(0)

			local function updateCrosshair(p, show)
				if not show then
					xhDot.Visible = false
					return
				end
				local ds = dotSize:Get()
				-- fixed colours: red by default, green the instant it's over an enemy
				local col = dbg.onEnemy and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
				local x, y = math.floor(p.X + 0.5), math.floor(p.Y + 0.5)

				xhDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ds, ds)
				xhDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x - math.floor(ds / 2), y - math.floor(ds / 2))
				xhDot.BackgroundColor3 = col
				xhDot.Visible = true
			end

			local SNIPERS = { "scout", "ssg", "awp", "g3sg1", "scar-20", "scar20", "sniper", "dragunov" }

			-- true only if a weapon MODEL inside MY OWN camera is a sniper, e.g.
			-- workspace.Camera.AWP or workspace.Camera."SSG 08". Other folders like
			-- ViewmodelStash are ignored, and other players never affect this.
			local function cameraHasSniper()
				if isSpectating() then return false, nil end
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam then return false, nil end
				for _, c in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("Model") then
						local n = string.lower(c.Name)
						for _, w in ipairs(SNIPERS) do
							if string.find(n, w, 1, true) then return true, c.Name end
						end
						return false, c.Name
					end
				end
				return false, nil
			end

			-- the game's real scope GUI: MainGui.Gameplay.Middle.SniperScope, the same
			-- object the Interface SniperScope module toggles .Visible on
			local gameScope, lastScopeFind = nil, 0
			local function findGameScope()
				if gameScope and gameScope.Parent then return gameScope end
				gameScope = nil
				if os.clock() - lastScopeFind < 0.1 then return nil end
				lastScopeFind = os.clock()

				local ch = getCrosshair()
				local middle = ch and ch.Parent
				local s = middle and middle:FindFirstChild("SniperScope")
				if not s then
					local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
					local main = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
					s = main and main:FindFirstChild("SniperScope", true)
				end
				gameScope = s
				return s
			end

			-- Hides the real scope WITHOUT touching .Visible: the game re-checks Visible every
			-- render step, so writing to it fights the game. Instead the frame is collapsed to
			-- 0x0 with clipping on. The game keeps rewriting Size / transparency (scope-in tween,
			-- re-equip), which used to let one frame of the real scope through (the blink), so:
			--  * property-changed signals put it back before the frame is drawn
			--  * a very late render step puts it back again after everything else has run
			--  * the hide is held for a moment so a one-frame gap never shows the real scope
			local scopeSaved, scopeConns, scopeHold = nil, {}, 0
			local ZERO = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

			local function scopeApply(s)
				if not (s and s.Parent) then return end
				if s.Size ~= ZERO then s.Size = ZERO end
				if not s.ClipsDescendants then s.ClipsDescendants = true end
				for _, n in ipairs({ "Blur", "Sharp" }) do
					local img = s:FindFirstChild(n)
					if img and img.ImageTransparency ~= 1 then img.ImageTransparency = 1 end
				end
			end

			local function scopeDisconnect()
				for _, c in ipairs(scopeConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
				table.clear(scopeConns)
			end

			local function setGameScopeHidden(s, hide)
				if hide and s then
					if not scopeSaved or scopeSaved.inst ~= s then
						scopeDisconnect()
						scopeSaved = { inst = s, size = s.Size, clips = s.ClipsDescendants }
						local function guard() if scopeSaved and scopeSaved.inst == s then scopeApply(s) end end
						scopeConns[#scopeConns + 1] = s:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(guard)
						scopeConns[#scopeConns + 1] = s:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ClipsDescendants"):Connect(guard)
						for _, n in ipairs({ "Blur", "Sharp" }) do
							local img = s:FindFirstChild(n)
							if img then
								scopeConns[#scopeConns + 1] = img:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ImageTransparency"):Connect(guard)
							end
						end
					end
					scopeApply(s)
				elseif scopeSaved then
					local o = scopeSaved
					scopeSaved = nil
					scopeDisconnect()
					if o.inst.Parent then
						o.inst.Size = o.size
						o.inst.ClipsDescendants = o.clips
					end
				end
			end

			RunService:BindToRenderStep("TriggerScopeLate", 9000, function()
				if scopeSaved then scopeApply(scopeSaved.inst) end
			end)

			-- scoped = the game's own scope GUI is showing (automatic, always)
			local function scopedNow()
				local s = findGameScope()
				return s ~= nil and s.Visible == true
			end

			local function isScopedNow()
				return lp:GetAttribute("IsSniperScoped") == true or scopedNow()
			end

			-- share the scope state with the rest of the script: the FOV forcing below needs it.
			-- the IsSniperScoped attribute alone can lag behind or be missing, which used to make
			-- the every-frame FOV force overwrite the game's scoped zoom (scope not zooming)
			RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSScopeState", 9000, function()
				env.__gsScopedNow = isScopedNow() == true
			end)

			-- ── keep my gun on screen while scoped ──
			-- The game hides the whole viewmodel when you scope (Viewmodel.hide sets every part to
			-- Transparency 1). While the box above is ticked, that call is skipped for MY viewmodel
			-- when I'm scoped. The render step below is a backup: it restores anything that still
			-- got hidden (the game stores the original value in the HiddenTransparency attribute).
			local VMmod, origVMHide
			do
				local ok, mod = pcall(function()
					return require(ReplicatedStorage.Classes.WeaponComponent.Classes.Viewmodel)
				end)
				if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.hide) == "function" then
					VMmod = mod
					origVMHide = env.__gsOrigVMHide or mod.hide
					env.__gsOrigVMHide = origVMHide
					pcall(function()
						if setreadonly then setreadonly(mod, false) end
						mod.hide = function(self, ...)
							if keepGun:Get() and rawget(self, "Player") == lp
								and isScopedNow() and not isSpectating() then
								return
							end
							return origVMHide(self, ...)
						end
					end)
				end
			end

			RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSKeepGun", 9055, function()
				if not keepGun:Get() or isSpectating() or not isScopedNow() then return end
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not cam then return end
				for _, model in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
					if model:IsA("Model") and model:FindFirstChild("Stats") then
						for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
							if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Texture") then
								local a = d:GetAttribute("HiddenTransparency")
								if type(a) == "number" and d.Transparency > a then d.Transparency = a end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			-- ── keep the same mouse sensitivity while scoped ──
			-- The game's CameraController sets UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity every frame to
			--   (current FOV / default FOV) * mouse sensitivity * (zoom multiplier while scoped) * aim-assist friction
			-- so scoping always scales it down. Remember the value from before scoping and put it back.
			local CamCtl, defaultFov
			pcall(function() CamCtl = require(ReplicatedStorage.Controllers.CameraController) end)
			pcall(function() defaultFov = require(ReplicatedStorage.Database.Custom.Constants).DEFAULT_CAMERA_FOV end)

			local baseSens, unscopedSince = nil, os.clock()

			RunService:BindToRenderStep("GSKeepSens", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5, function()
				if not (CamCtl and defaultFov and type(CamCtl.getTargetFOV) == "function") then return end
				local ok, goal = pcall(CamCtl.getTargetFOV)
				if not ok or type(goal) ~= "number" then return end

				local cur = UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity
				if goal >= defaultFov - 0.5 then
					-- not zoomed: only trust the value once the FOV spring has settled
					if os.clock() - unscopedSince > 0.6 then baseSens = cur end
					return
				end
				unscopedSince = os.clock()

				if keepSens:Get() and baseSens and cur ~= baseSens then
					UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = baseSens
				end
			end)

			env.__gsSensCleanup = function()
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSKeepSens") end)
			end

			local function updateScope(p, allow)
				local isSniper = cameraHasSniper()
				local s = findGameScope()
				local now = os.clock()

				local show = (allow and scOn:Get()) == true
				if show and scSniperOnly:Get() and not isSniper then show = false end
				if show and not scopedNow() then show = false end

				-- the real scope is simply gone for as long as scope lines + "Hide game scope" are on:
				-- no dependence on being scoped / holding a sniper / a menu, so no gap can ever let
				-- it flash through
				setGameScopeHidden(s, scOn:Get() and scHideNative:Get())

				for _, f in pairs(scF) do
					if f.Visible ~= show then pcall(function() f.Visible = show end) end
				end
				if not show then return end

				local len, gap, th = scLen:Get(), scGap:Get(), scThick:Get()
				local col = scColor:Get()
				local alpha = scTrans:Get() / 100
				local fade = true
				local x, y = math.floor(p.X + 0.5), math.floor(p.Y + 0.5)
				local h = math.floor(th / 2)

				scF.R.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, th)
				scF.R.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + gap, y - h)
				scF.L.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, th)
				scF.L.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x - gap - len, y - h)
				scF.D.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(th, len)
				scF.D.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x - h, y + gap)
				scF.U.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(th, len)
				scF.U.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x - h, y - gap - len)
				for k, f in pairs(scF) do
					f.BackgroundColor3 = col
					f.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
					local g = scGrad[k]
					g.Rotation = SC_ROT[k]
					g.Transparency = fade and SC_FADE or SC_SOLID
				end
			end

			local arrows = {}
			local function getArrow(i)
				local a = arrows[i]
				if a then return a end
				local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				lbl.Text = "▼"
				lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
				lbl.TextScaled = true
				lbl.Visible = false
				lbl.Parent = dotGui2
				a = { root = lbl }
				arrows[i] = a
				return a
			end

			local function enemyList(cam, maxDist, teamCheck)
				local list = {}
				local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
				if not folder then return list end
				local mineTeam = teamCheck and myTeam() or nil
				local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position

				local function consider(m)
					if not m:IsA("Model") or m.Name == lp.Name then return end
					if m:FindFirstAncestor("Hostages") then return end
					if isCharacterDead(m) then return end
					if mineTeam then
						local theirs = armorTeam(m) or backupTeam(m, playerFromCharacter(m))
						if theirs and theirs == mineTeam then return end
					end
					local root = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m:FindFirstChild("Head")
					if not root then return end
					local d = (root.Position - camPos).Magnitude
					if d <= maxDist then list[#list + 1] = { pos = root.Position, dist = d } end
				end

				for _, c in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
					if c:IsA("Folder") then
						for _, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do consider(m) end
					else
						consider(c)
					end
				end
				table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.dist < b.dist end)
				return list
			end

			local function updateArrows(p, allow)
				if not (allow and arOn:Get()) then
					for _, a in ipairs(arrows) do a.root.Visible = false end
					return
				end
				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				local list = enemyList(cam, arDist:Get(), arTeam:Get())
				local limit = arClosest:Get() and 1 or 8
				local _, yaw = cam.CFrame:ToOrientation()
				local yawCF = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
				local col, n, radius = arColor:Get(), arSize:Get(), arRadius:Get()
				local offOnly = arOffOnly:Get()
				local used = 0

				for _, e in ipairs(list) do
					if used >= limit then break end
					local skip = false
					if offOnly then
						local _, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(e.pos)
						skip = onScreen
					end
					if not skip then
						used += 1
						local a = getArrow(used)
						local rel = yawCF:PointToObjectSpace(e.pos)
						local ang = math.atan2(rel.X, -rel.Z)

						a.root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(n, n)
						a.root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X + math.sin(ang) * radius, p.Y - math.cos(ang) * radius)
						-- the glyph's tip points down by default; flip it to point
						-- away from the crosshair, toward the enemy's screen direction
						a.root.Rotation = math.deg(ang) + 180
						a.root.TextColor3 = col
						a.root.Visible = true
					end
				end
				for i = used + 1, #arrows do arrows[i].root.Visible = false end
			end

			-- key = a player's UserId, or the model itself for bots
			local function killBodyPos(userId)
				local list = {}
				if typeof(userId) == "Instance" then
					list[#list + 1] = userId
				else
					local plr = userId and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
					if not plr then return nil end
					if plr.Character then list[#list + 1] = plr.Character end
					local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
					local fm = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)
					if fm then list[#list + 1] = fm end
				end
				for _, m in ipairs(list) do
					local r = m:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or m:FindFirstChild("Torso") or m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if r and r:IsA("BasePart") then return r.Position end
				end
				return nil
			end

			-- HvH-style kill marker: four diagonal ticks with a gap around the centre that pop outward,
			-- settle to the resting gap, hold, then fade
			local lastBody = {} -- [userId or model] = last known body position (the body can already be gone at the kill)
			local kmFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
			kmFolder.Name = "GSKillMark"
			kmFolder.Parent = workspace

			-- the marker is a BillboardGui on the enemy's own body (it follows the corpse / ragdoll), drawn on
			-- top of walls: four diagonal ticks around a centre gap that spin, pop out, settle, hold, fade
			local function killMarker(userId)
				local model = typeof(userId) == "Instance" and userId or nil
				if not model and userId then
					local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
					if plr then
						local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
						model = (chars and chars:FindFirstChild(plr.Name, true)) or plr.Character
					end
				end
				local body = model and (model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
					or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
				local worldPos = (body and body.Position) or killBodyPos(userId) or lastBody[userId]
				if not worldPos then return end

				local col, size, dur = kmColor:Get(), kmSize:Get(), kmTime:Get()
				local gap, outline, spin, th = kmGap:Get(), kmOutline:Get(), kmSpin:Get(), kmThick:Get()
				local len = size * 0.7

				local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
				anchor.Anchored = true
				anchor.CanCollide, anchor.CanQuery, anchor.CanTouch, anchor.CastShadow = false, false, false, false
				anchor.Transparency = 1
				anchor.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
				anchor.Position = worldPos
				anchor.Parent = kmFolder

				local span = (gap + len + size) * 2 + 24
				local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
				gui.Adornee = anchor
				gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
				gui.LightInfluence = 0
				gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(span, span)
				gui.Parent = anchor

				local root = Instance.new("Frame")
				root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				root.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
				root.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
				root.Parent = gui

				local ticks = {}
				for _, d in ipairs({ { 1, 1 }, { -1, -1 }, { 1, -1 }, { -1, 1 } }) do
					local f = Instance.new("Frame")
					f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					f.BorderSizePixel = 0
					f.BackgroundColor3 = col
					f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, th)
					f.Rotation = (d[1] * d[2] > 0) and 45 or -45
					f.Parent = root
					local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
					st.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
					st.Thickness = outline
					st.Parent = f
					ticks[#ticks + 1] = { f = f, st = st, d = d }
				end

				local t0 = os.clock()
				local conn
				conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
					local t = (os.clock() - t0) / dur
					if t >= 1 or not anchor.Parent then
						conn:Disconnect()
						pcall(function() anchor:Destroy() end)
						return
					end
					if body and body.Parent then anchor.Position = body.Position end -- rides on the body
					root.Rotation = (os.clock() - t0) * spin
					local pop = (1 - math.min(t * 7, 1)) * size * 0.5
					local r = gap + len / 2 + pop
					local fade = math.clamp((t - 0.6) / 0.4, 0, 1)
					for _, e in ipairs(ticks) do
						e.f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, e.d[1] * r / SQ, 0.5, e.d[2] * r / SQ)
						e.f.BackgroundTransparency = fade
						e.st.Transparency = fade
					end
				end)
			end

			-- hitmarker: one colour for everything now, kills just draw bigger
			dbg.hitmarker = function(kind, userId)
				if userId ~= nil then
					local bp = killBodyPos(userId)
					if bp then lastBody[userId] = bp end
				end
				if kind == "kill" and kmOn:Get() then
					if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
					pcall(killMarker, userId)
				end
				if not hmOn:Get() then return end
				if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
				local p = aimPoint()
				local kill = (kind == "kill")
				local col = hmColor:Get()
				local size = hmSize:Get() * (kill and 1.5 or 1)
				local gap, th, dur = hmGap:Get(), hmThick:Get(), hmTime:Get()
				local outline = hmOutline:Get()
				local r = gap + size / 2

				local root = Instance.new("Frame")
				root.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
				root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X, p.Y)
				root.Parent = dotGui2

				local info = TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				for _, d in ipairs({ { 1, 1 }, { -1, -1 }, { 1, -1 }, { -1, 1 } }) do
					local f = bar(root, (d[1] * d[2] > 0) and 45 or -45)
					f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, th)
					f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(d[1] * r / SQ, d[2] * r / SQ)
					f.BackgroundColor3 = col
					f.Visible = true
					TweenService:Create(f, info, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
					local s = f:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
					if s then
						s.Thickness = outline
						TweenService:Create(s, info, { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
					end
				end
				task.delay(dur + 0.1, function() root:Destroy() end)
			end

			local hiddenPieces = {}
			local function setGameCrosshairHidden2(hide)
				if hide then
					local ch = getCrosshair()
					if not ch then return end
					for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("GuiObject") and not d:FindFirstAncestor("Hitmarker") and d.Name ~= "Hitmarker" then
							if hiddenPieces[d] == nil then hiddenPieces[d] = d.Visible end
							d.Visible = false
						end
					end
				else
					for d, v in pairs(hiddenPieces) do
						if d.Parent then d.Visible = v end
					end
					table.clear(hiddenPieces)
				end
			end
			env.__gsSetCrosshairHidden = setGameCrosshairHidden2

			-- hide/show the real crosshair the moment the toggle flips, instead of
			-- waiting for the next render frame to notice
			local dotWasOn = dotOn:Get()
			setGameCrosshairHidden2(dotWasOn)
			dotOn:OnChanged(function(on)
				setGameCrosshairHidden2(on)
				dotWasOn = on
			end)

			-- The HUD loop can throw "The current thread cannot access 'Instance'
			-- (lacking capability Plugin)". It has three layers of protection:
			--  1. a RenderStepped connection instead of BindToRenderStep (a bound
			--     callback can run with a lower identity than a normal connection,
			--     and the other loops in this script already use Connect)
			--  2. the identity is re-applied at the start of every frame
			--  3. if the HUD still fails for 30 frames in a row, its ScreenGui moves
			--     into PlayerGui, which every identity is allowed to touch
			-- Each part is also guarded on its own (the warning carries a traceback),
			-- so one failing part can never switch the others off.
			local hudFails, hudMoved, hudLastErr = 0, false, nil

			local function runHud(name, fn, ...)
				local ok, err = xpcall(fn, function(e)
					return name .. ": " .. tostring(e) .. "\n" .. debug.traceback("", 2)
				end, ...)
				if not ok then return err end
				return nil
			end

			local function hudStep()
				local on = dotOn:Get()
				if on and not dotWasOn then
					setGameCrosshairHidden2(true)
					dotWasOn = true
				elseif not on and dotWasOn then
					setGameCrosshairHidden2(false)
					dotWasOn = false
				end

				if env.__gsElevate then env.__gsElevate() end
				local gameMenu = menusOpen()
				local p = aimPoint()
				local e1 = runHud("crosshair", updateCrosshair, p, on and not gameMenu)
				local e2 = runHud("scope", updateScope, p, not gameMenu)
				local e3 = runHud("arrows", updateArrows, p, not gameMenu)
				local hudErr = e1 or e2 or e3

				if not hudErr then
					hudFails = 0
					return
				end

				hudFails += 1
				if hudErr ~= hudLastErr or os.clock() - (env.__gsHudWarn or 0) > 10 then
					hudLastErr = hudErr
					env.__gsHudWarn = os.clock()
					warn("[HUD] " .. hudErr)
				end
				if hudFails >= 30 and not hudMoved then
					hudMoved = true
					local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
					if pg then
						local okMove = pcall(function() dotGui2.Parent = pg end)
						warn(okMove and "[HUD] still failing, moved the HUD into PlayerGui"
							or "[HUD] could not move the HUD into PlayerGui")
					end
				end
			end

			local hudConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(hudStep)

			env.__gsHudCleanup = function()
				pcall(function() setGameScopeHidden(gameScope, false) end)
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TriggerScopeLate") end)
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSKeepGun") end)
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("GSKeepSens") end)
				pcall(function() if VMmod and origVMHide then VMmod.hide = origVMHide end end)
				pcall(function() setGameCrosshairHidden2(false) end)
				pcall(function() dotGui2:Destroy() end)
				pcall(function() hudConn:Disconnect() end)
				pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TriggerDot") end)
				dbg.hitmarker = nil
				pcall(function() kmFolder:Destroy() end)
			end
		end)() end

	-- ───────────── DEBUG + OVERLAY + MAIN LOOP ─────────────
	local dbgGui, mainConn
	do
		local dbgSec = MiscTab:AddSection("Debug", "Right")
		local dbgOverlay = dbgSec:AddToggle("Overlay", false)
		dbgSec:AddSlider("Aim offset X", -100, 100, 0, function(v) aimOffset = Vector2.new(v, aimOffset.Y) end, "px", 1)
		dbgSec:AddSlider("Aim offset Y", -100, 100, 1, function(v) aimOffset = Vector2.new(aimOffset.X, v) end, "px", 1)

		dbgSec:AddButton("Print camera contents", function()
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			print("[camera] " .. (cam and cam:GetFullName() or "none"))
			if cam then
				for _, d in ipairs(cam:GetDescendants()) do
					print(string.format("  %s (%s)", d:GetFullName():gsub("^.-Camera%.", ""), d.ClassName))
				end
			end
			for _, c in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
				local n = string.lower(c.Name)
				if string.find(n, "viewmodel", 1, true) or string.find(n, "view_model", 1, true) then
					print("[workspace] " .. c:GetFullName() .. " (" .. c.ClassName .. ")")
				end
			end
			Window:Notify("Printed camera contents to the console (F9)")
		end)

		dbgSec:AddButton("Print character parts", function()
			for _, root in ipairs(characterRoots()) do
				print("[character] " .. root:GetFullName())
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then
						print(string.format("  %s (%s)  Transparency=%.2f  LocalMod=%.2f", d.Name, d.ClassName, d.Transparency, d.LocalTransparencyModifier))
					end
				end
			end
			Window:Notify("Printed character parts to the console (F9)")
		end)

		dbgSec:AddButton("Print character weapon", function()
			local roots = characterRoots()
			local function inRoots(i)
				for _, r in ipairs(roots) do
					if i:IsDescendantOf(r) then return true end
				end
				return false
			end

			for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
				print("[char tree] " .. root:GetFullName())
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
					local extra = ""
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then
						extra = string.format("  Transparency=%.2f  LocalMod=%.2f", d.Transparency, d.LocalTransparencyModifier)
					end
					print(string.format("  %s (%s)%s", d:GetFullName(), d.ClassName, extra))
				end
			end

			local n = 0
			for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("JointInstance") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
					local a, b = d.Part0, d.Part1
					if a and b and inRoots(a) ~= inRoots(b) then
						local outside = inRoots(a) and b or a
						print("[attached from outside] " .. d:GetFullName() .. "  ->  " .. outside:GetFullName())
						n += 1
					end
				end
			end
			print("[char weapon] " .. n .. " outside parts joined to the character")
			Window:Notify("Printed character tree + attached parts to the console (F9)")
		end)

		dbgSec:AddButton("Print ghost sources", function()
			local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
			local folderModel = chars and chars:FindFirstChild(lp.Name)

			local function info(label, m)
				if not m then
					print("[ghost] " .. label .. ": none")
					return
				end
				local total, visible = 0, 0
				for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("BasePart") then
						total += 1
						if d.Transparency < 0.99 then visible += 1 end
					end
				end
				print(string.format("[ghost] %s: %s  parts=%d  visible=%d  dead=%s",
					label, m:GetFullName(), total, visible, tostring(isCharacterDead(m))))
			end

			info("workspace.Characters." .. lp.Name, folderModel)
			info("lp.Character", lp.Character)
			print("[ghost] same instance: " .. tostring(folderModel ~= nil and folderModel == lp.Character))

			local pick = pickMyModel()
			print("[ghost] Only Me ghost is built from: " .. (pick and pick:GetFullName() or "nothing"))
			Window:Notify("Printed ghost sources to the console (F9)")
		end)

		dbgSec:AddButton("Test click", function()
			local ok = click()
			dbg.clicks += 1
			Window:Notify(ok and ("Sent a test click (" .. clickMethod:Get() .. ")") or "Click failed, see overlay")
		end)

		local function guiParent()
			local ok, hui = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
			if ok and hui then return hui end
			local ok2 = pcall(function() return CoreGui.Name end)
			if ok2 then return CoreGui end
			return lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
		end

		local panel
		local function buildOverlay(parent)
			if dbgGui then pcall(function() dbgGui:Destroy() end) end

			dbgGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			dbgGui.Name = "TriggerDebug"
			dbgGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
			dbgGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
			dbgGui.DisplayOrder = 999
			dbgGui.Parent = parent

			panel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 220)
			panel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
			panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
			panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
			panel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
			panel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			panel.TextSize = 13
			panel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			panel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			panel.Visible = false
			panel.Parent = dbgGui
			local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
			pad.PaddingLeft, pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6), UDim.new(0, 6)
			pad.PaddingTop, pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), UDim.new(0, 4)
			pad.Parent = panel
		end
		buildOverlay(guiParent())

		local function fmt(v) return string.format("%.1f, %.1f", v.X, v.Y) end

		local panelVisible = false

		local function updateOverlayUnsafe()
			local want = dbgOverlay:Get()
			if want ~= panelVisible then
				panel.Visible = want
				panelVisible = want
			end
			if not want then return end

			local p, src = aimPoint()
			local ch = getCrosshair()
			local _, menuName = menusOpen()
			local center = camera.ViewportSize / 2
			local lastAgo = dbg.lastClick > 0 and string.format("%.2fs ago", os.clock() - dbg.lastClick) or "never"

			local cfgNow = activeCfg()
			panel.Text = table.concat({
				"-- triggerbot debug --",
				"state:     " .. dbg.state,
				"crosshair: " .. (ch and ch:GetFullName():gsub("^.-PlayerGui%.", "") or "NOT FOUND"),
				"source:    " .. src,
				"aim point: " .. fmt(p) .. "  (vs center " .. fmt(p - center) .. ")",
				"our menu:  " .. tostring(isMenuVisible()) .. "  cursor on it: " .. tostring(mouseOverMenu()),
				"game menu: " .. (menuName or "none"),
				"ray hit:   " .. dbg.hit .. string.format(" (%.0f studs)", dbg.dist),
				"model:     " .. dbg.model,
				"matched by:" .. dbg.how,
				"teams:     " .. dbg.team,
				"on enemy:  " .. tostring(dbg.onEnemy),
				"target:    " .. targetPart:Get(),
				"enabled:   " .. tostring(trigOn:Get()) .. "  key: " .. (trigKey:GetKey() and trigKey:GetKey().Name or "None") .. "",
				"click via: " .. clickMethod:Get() .. (dbg.clickErr and ("  ERROR: " .. dbg.clickErr) or ""),
				"clicks:    " .. dbg.clicks .. "  last: " .. lastAgo,
				"spectating:" .. tostring(isSpectating()),
				"-- silent aim debug --",
				"status:    " .. dbg.silentStatus,
				"target:    " .. dbg.silentTarget,
				"distance:  " .. dbg.silentDist .. " studs",
				"wallbang:  " .. tostring(dbg.wallbangOn),
				"auto scan: " .. tostring(dbg.autoScan),
				"pen block: " .. tostring(dbg.penBlock),
				"weapon:    pen " .. tostring(dbg.activePen) .. "  dmg " .. tostring(dbg.activeDamage)
					.. "  head x" .. tostring(dbg.activeHeadMult) .. "  range " .. tostring(dbg.activeRange),
				"last shot: " .. tostring(dbg.shotMode),
				"-- ghost debug --",
				"ghost src: " .. dbg.ghostSource,
				"-- bhop debug --",
				"speed:     " .. string.format("%.1f", bhopDbg.speed) .. "  peak: " .. string.format("%.1f", bhopDbg.peak) .. "  ground: " .. tostring(bhopDbg.ground),
				"stamina:   " .. string.format("%.1f", bhopDbg.stamina),
				"gamemode:  " .. tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("ServerGamemode")) .. " / " .. tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("Gamemode")),
				"profile:   speedCap " .. tostring(cfgNow and cfgNow.BunnyHopSpeedCap)
					.. "  landPenalty>" .. tostring(cfgNow and cfgNow.BunnyHopPenaltyThreshold)
					.. "  airAccel " .. tostring(cfgNow and cfgNow.AirAccelerate)
					.. "  (speedCap 0 = none)",
			}, "\n")
		end

		local overlayErrors = 0
		local function updateOverlay()
			local ok, err = pcall(updateOverlayUnsafe)
			if ok then return end

			overlayErrors += 1
			if overlayErrors == 1 then
				local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
				if pg then pcall(buildOverlay, pg) end
				panelVisible = false
			end
		end

		local function setState(s)
			dbg.state = s
		end

		Window:AddConfigTab()
		do
			local f = Window.Flags["Config/Menu/Menu key"]
			if f then f.Obj:SetKey(Enum.KeyCode.RightShift) end
		end
		setMenuVisible(true)

		local lastClick = 0
		local lastAuto, lastAutoScan = 0, 0

		mainConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

			-- leave the FOV alone while scoped: the game zooms by changing FOV, so forcing
			-- our value every frame would cancel the scope zoom entirely. use the shared scope
			-- state (attribute + scope GUI) so the zoom is never stomped mid-transition
			if fovOn:Get() and not env.__gsScopedNow and lp:GetAttribute("IsSniperScoped") ~= true then
				camera.FieldOfView = fovVal:Get()
			end

			if antiFlash:Get() and FlashEffect then
				pcall(function()
					if FlashEffect.IsFlashed() then FlashEffect.CancelFlash() end
				end)
			end

			if antiSmoke:Get() and SmokeFx and os.clock() - lastSmokeSweep > 0.25 then
				lastSmokeSweep = os.clock()
				pcall(SmokeFx.DestroyAll)
			end

			if resOn:Get() then
				local Resolution = resVal:Get() / 100
				camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, Resolution, 0, 0, 0, 1)
			end

			local open, menuName = menusOpen()
			local overMenu = mouseOverMenu()

			local target, reason
			if open or overMenu then
				dbg.onEnemy = false
			else
				target, reason = getTrigTarget()
			end

			if not trigOn:Get() then
				setState("disabled")
			elseif overMenu then
				setState("paused: cursor over gamesense menu")
			elseif open then
				setState("paused: game menu open (" .. menuName .. ")")
			elseif not target then
				setState(reason)
			elseif os.clock() - lastClick < trigDelay:Get() / 1000 then
				setState("waiting for delay (" .. reason .. ")")
			else
				lastClick = os.clock()
				dbg.lastClick = lastClick
				dbg.clicks += 1
				click()
				setState("FIRED at " .. target.Name)
			end

			--silent aim auto shoot
			local sa = dbg.silentAuto
			if sa and sa.enabled:Get() and sa.on:Get() and not open and not overMenu and not isSpectating() then
				local now = os.clock()
				local fast = sa.fast and sa.fast:Get()
				local scanEvery = fast and 0 or ((dbg.wallbangOn and now - (dbg.autoAt or 0) > 0.5) and 0.06 or 0.03)
				local reactDelay = fast and 0 or sa.delay:Get() / 1000
				if now - lastAuto >= reactDelay and now - lastAutoScan >= scanEvery then
					lastAutoScan = now
					-- third person: the shot leaves from the real first-person eye, not the pulled-back camera
					local eyeOrigin = firstPersonCF and firstPersonCF.Position or nil
					-- cheap=false forces the full reachInfo check (line of sight OR wallbang penetration),
					-- so `part` is only ever returned when the shot can really land
					local part, autoAim = getClosestPlayerPartInFov(eyeOrigin, false, sa.visibleOnly and sa.visibleOnly:Get() or false, true)
					dbg.autoScan = part and "target found" or ("held: " .. tostring(dbg.silentStatus) .. (dbg.penBlock and (" | last block: " .. dbg.penBlock) or ""))
					local aligned = true
					if part then
						dbg.autoPick = { part = part, pos = autoAim, t = now }
						if sa.face and sa.face:Get() and typeof(autoAim) == "Vector3" then
							local cf = firstPersonCF or camera.CFrame
							local want = autoAim - cf.Position
							if want.Magnitude > 0.01 then
								local cosA = math.clamp(cf.LookVector:Dot(want.Unit), -1, 1)
								aligned = math.acos(cosA) < math.rad(fast and 4 or 2)
							end
						end
						if aligned then
							dbg.alignSince = nil
						else
							dbg.alignSince = dbg.alignSince or now
							-- if the camera never follows the sent look, don't wait forever
							if now - dbg.alignSince > (fast and 0.08 or 0.25) then aligned = true end
						end
					end
					if part and not aligned then
						dbg.autoScan = "turning onto the best spot"
					elseif part then
						lastAuto = now
						dbg.autoAt = now
						dbg.lastClick = now
						dbg.clicks += 1
						click()
						setState("AUTO SHOT at " .. part.Parent.Name)
					end
				end
			end

			updateOverlay()
		end)
	end

	env.__gsTriggerUnload = function()
		pcall(function() if env.__gsSkinCleanup then env.__gsSkinCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsKnifeGloveCleanup then env.__gsKnifeGloveCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsGloveCleanup then env.__gsGloveCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsBoardRestore then env.__gsBoardRestore() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsWorldCleanup then env.__gsWorldCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsHudCleanup then env.__gsHudCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsLightCleanup then env.__gsLightCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsWeaponRestore then env.__gsWeaponRestore() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsSensCleanup then env.__gsSensCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsEspCleanup then env.__gsEspCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsVmFxCleanup then env.__gsVmFxCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsReloadCleanup then env.__gsReloadCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsTPGunCleanup then env.__gsTPGunCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsDmgCleanup then env.__gsDmgCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsImpactCleanup then env.__gsImpactCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsPeekCleanup then env.__gsPeekCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if env.__gsSelfChamsCleanup then env.__gsSelfChamsCleanup() end end)
		pcall(function() if ghostConn then ghostConn:Disconnect() end end)
		cleanupAllGhosts()
		pcall(function() ghostFolder:Destroy() end)
		pcall(soundCleanup)
		pcall(tracerCleanup)
		pcall(function() mainConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() beganConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() endedConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() bhopConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() jumpBeganConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() jumpEndedConn:Disconnect() end)
		pcall(function() if CharClass and origSampleInput then CharClass.SampleInput = origSampleInput end end)
		pcall(function() if fireConn then fireConn:Disconnect() end end)
		pcall(function()
			for d, v in pairs(fireSaved) do if d.Parent then d.Enabled = v end end
		end)
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TriggerThirdPerson") end)
		pcall(restoreCharacter)
		pcall(restoreViewmodel)
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TriggerThirdPersonReset") end)
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("TriggerOwnHead") end)
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("SilentAimFovCircle") end)
		pcall(function() dbgGui:Destroy() end)
		pcall(function() fovCircle:Remove() end)
		pcall(function() Window:Destroy() end)
	end

end

Window:Notify("skeet.cc", 4)
